### [35] [Streaming Speech-to-Text Translation with a SpeechLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14766)
*Titouan Parcollet,Shucong Zhang,Xianrui Zheng,Rogier C. van Dalen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的流式语音到文本翻译架构，通过让模型学习自主决策音频采集时机，实现低延迟（1-2秒）实时翻译，翻译质量接近非流式基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SpeechLLM系统存在延迟问题：或等待完整语句后输出翻译，或以固定间隔输出token，无法满足实时应用需求。

Method: 提出基于LLM的流式语音翻译架构，模型不仅输出翻译文本，还学习自主决策是否已收集足够音频信息来生成翻译。训练采用输入语音与输出文本的自动对齐数据。

Result: 多语言对实验中，系统翻译质量接近非流式基线，同时实现仅1-2秒的低延迟。

Conclusion: This研究实现了一个低延迟（1-2秒）的流式语音到文本翻译系统，在保持接近非流式基线翻译质量的同时，解决了现有SpeechLLM系统无法真正实时输出的问题。

Abstract: Normally, a system that translates speech into text consists of separate modules for speech recognition and text-to-text translation. Combining those tasks into a SpeechLLM promises to exploit paralinguistic information in the speech and to reduce cascaded errors. But existing SpeechLLM systems are slow since they do not work in a real streaming fashion: they wait for a complete utterance of audio before outputting a translation, or output tokens at fixed intervals, which is not suitable for real applications. This work proposes an LLM-based architecture for real streaming speech-to-text translation. The LLM learns not just to emit output tokens, but also to decide whether it has seen enough audio to do so. The system is trained using automatic alignments of the input speech and the output text. In experiments on different language pairs, the system achieves a translation quality close to the non-streaming baseline, but with a latency of only 1-2 seconds.

</details>


### [36] [Graphs of Research: Citation Evolution Graphs as Supervision for Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14790)
*Songyang Gao,Yinghui Xia,Siyi Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GoR方法，通过构建基于引文位置、频率、前驱链接和发表时间的论文演化DAG图，并结合监督微调的LLM进行想法预测，在ML/NLP领域实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的研究想法生成方法主要依赖静态文献检索或复杂提示工程，未能充分挖掘参考文献之间的结构化关联关系，导致生成质量受限。

Method: 提出GoR方法，通过构建论文演化有向无环图（DAG）来建模研究脉络：1）提取种子论文的2跳引用邻域；2）从引用位置、频率、前驱链接、发表时间等维度推导参考文献间的关系；3）使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M进行监督微调，输入包含引文图、边信号、参考文献信息和任务定义的结构化文本提示，预测种子论文的研究想法。

Result: 在5个主要ML/NLP学术会议的数据集上（498/50/50的训练/验证/测试集，约7600条引用），GoR-SFT在与gpt-4o驱动基线的LLM评判对战中取得领先，验证了引文演化图作为监督信号的有效性。

Conclusion: GoR-SFT方法通过融合引用演化图结构信息，显著提升了基于LLM的研究想法生成质量。该工作为自动化科学研究提供了新的监督信号来源，有望加速科学创新。

Abstract: Research idea generation is the innovation-driving step of automated scientific research. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have shown potential for automating idea generation at scale. However, existing methods mainly condition LLMs on eliciting idea generation through static retrieval of relevant literature or complex prompt engineering, without discarding the structural relations among references. We propose Graphs of Research (GoR), a supervised fine-tuning method that extracts a 2-hop reference neighborhood for each seed paper, derives the relations among those references from citation position, frequency, predecessor links, and publication time, and organizes them into a paper-evolution directed acyclic graph (DAG). We construct an automated extraction pipeline that draws data from five major ML/NLP venues, comprising 498/50/50 train/validation/test seed papers and approximately 7,600 cited references. Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct-1M is fine-tuned on a structured-text prompt that includes the citation graph, edge signals, reference information, and task definition to predict the idea for the seed paper. Across head-to-head LLM-judge tournaments against gpt-4o-driven baselines, GoR-SFT achieves SOTA, demonstrating the effectiveness of citation-evolution graphs as supervision signal for LLM-based idea generation. We hope that this reduces the barrier for citation evolution graphs as a supervision, accelerating automated scientific innovation.

</details>


### [37] [Conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables to LMF. Application to French](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14816)
*Eric Laporte,Elsa Tolone,Mathieu Constant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: First experiment converting French Lexicon-Grammar tables into LMF format to enable cross-system interoperability and standardization of NLP lexical resources.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Lexicon-Grammar of French is a major source of lexical/syntactic information, but lacks interoperability across different NLP systems and contexts.

Method: Converting Lexicon-Grammar tables for French verbs into LMF format, addressing structural challenges during the transformation process.

Result: Successfully created an LMF-formatted resource derived from the original Lexicon-Grammar, while documenting the main difficulties encountered during conversion.

Conclusion: The conversion makes the Lexicon-Grammar usable in different contexts, contributing to standardization and interoperability of NLP dictionaries.

Abstract: We describe the first experiment of conversion of Lexicon-Grammar tables for French verbs into the Lexical Markup Framework (LMF) format. The Lexicon-Grammar of the French language is currently one of the major sources of lexical and syntactic information for French. Its conversion into an interoperable representation format according to the LMF standard makes it usable in different contexts, thus contributing to the standardization and interoperability of natural language processing dictionaries. We briefly introduce the Lexicon-Grammar and the derived dictionaries; we analyse the main difficulties faced during the conversion; and we describe the resulting resource.

</details>

Abstract: Foundation models tokenize Ukrainian legal text with vastly different efficiency, yet no systematic comparison exists for this domain. We benchmark seven models from five providers on 273 validated court decisions from Ukraine's state registry (EDRSR), measuring tokenizer fertility and zero-shot performance on three tasks. Three findings emerge. (1) Tokenizer fertility varies 1.6x: Qwen3 models consume 60% more tokens than Llama-family models on identical input, directly reducing API cost. (2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B) achieves the highest composite score (83.1), outperforming Mistral Large 3 (675B total, 41B active) -- a model with 5.6x more total parameters and 3.4x more active parameters per token -- at one-third the API cost. (3) Few-shot prompting degrades performance by up to 26 percentage points; stratified and prompt-sensitivity ablations confirm this is intrinsic to Ukrainian-language demonstrations, not an artifact of example selection. For practitioners: tokenizer analysis should precede model selection, and zero-shot is a more reliable default than few-shot for morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [39] [Chain-of-Procedure: Hierarchical Visual-Language Reasoning for Procedural QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14928)
*Guanhua Chen,Yutong Yao,Shenghe Sun,Ci-Jun Gao,Shudong Liu,Lidia S. Chao,Feng Wan,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对视觉过程问答（VP-QA）任务，提出了ProcedureVQA多模态基准测试和CoP层次推理框架，识别出现有VLM在跨模态检索和图像-文本粒度对齐方面的关键缺陷，并通过三阶段推理策略实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言模型在标准图像-文本任务上表现优异，但其在视觉过程问答（VP-QA）方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。VP-QA任务涉及用户通过上传复杂过程中间状态的图像来查询下一步操作，具有独特挑战性。

Method: 提出了包含三个阶段的层次推理框架CoP：1）基于视觉线索检索相关指令；2）通过语义分解进行步骤细化；3）生成下一步操作。

Result: 在六个VLM上的实验表明，CoP相比标准基线方法实现了最高13%的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: CoP框架通过分层推理策略有效解决了VP-QA任务中的跨模态检索和对齐问题，在多个VLM上实现了显著性能提升。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on standard image-text tasks, yet their potential for visual procedure question answering (VP-QA) remains largely unexplored. VP-QA presents unique challenges where users query next-step actions by uploading images for intermediate states of complex procedures. To systematically evaluate VLMs on this practical task, we propose ProcedureVQA, a novel multimodal benchmark specifically designed for visual procedural reasoning. Through comprehensive analysis, we identify two critical limitations in current VLMs: inadequate cross-modal retrieval of structured procedures given visual states, and misalignment between image sequence granularity and textual step decomposition. To address these issues, we present Chain-of-Procedure (CoP), a hierarchical reasoning framework that first retrieves relevant instructions using visual cues, then performs step refinement through semantic decomposition, and finally generates the next step. Experiments across six VLMs demonstrate CoP's effectiveness, achieving up to 13% absolute improvement over standard baselines.

</details>


### [40] [Performance-Driven Policy Optimization for Speculative Decoding with Adaptive Windowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14978)
*Jie Jiang,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PPOW是一种用于加速LLM推理的强化学习框架，通过窗口级优化而非传统token级模仿来改进投机解码的draft模型，结合成本感知奖励、分布邻近奖励和自适应发散感知窗口化策略，在多种模型和基准上实现约3.4-4.4倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前投机解码的两个核心问题：1) 硬draft位置瓶颈——早期token不匹配会导致接受前缀截断，使剩余speculative窗口失效；2) 现有基于学习的draft模型仍使用token级监督目标优化，但投机解码的效用本质上是窗口级且前缀敏感的。

Method: 提出PPOW框架，基于强化学习方法，包含三个核心组件：1) Cost-Aware Speedup Reward（成本感知加速奖励）；2) Distribution-Based Proximity Reward（基于分布的邻近奖励）；3) Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing（自适应发散感知窗口化），优先选择高置信度draft-target发散度的信息性窗口。

Result: 在统一解码协议下，跨多个模型族和基准测试实现平均接受长度6.29-6.52，加速比3.39-4.36×。

Conclusion: 性能驱动的窗口级优化是提升投机解码效率的实用方法。PPOW通过将优化目标从token级模仿转向window级优化，有效解决了硬draft位置导致的效率瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a lightweight draft model propose speculative windows of candidate tokens for parallel verification by a larger target model. In practice, speculative efficiency is often bottlenecked by hard-to-draft positions, where an early mismatch truncates the accepted prefix and invalidates the rest of the speculative window. Most learning-based drafters are still optimized with token-level supervised objectives, even though speculative utility is inherently window-level and prefix-sensitive. We propose PPOW (Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing), a reinforcement learning framework that shifts drafter optimization from token-level imitation to window-level optimization. PPOW combines a Cost-Aware Speedup Reward, a Distribution-Based Proximity Reward, and Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing, which prioritizes informative windows with high confidence-weighted draft-target divergence. PPOW achieves average acceptance lengths of 6.29-6.52 and speedups of 3.39-4.36$\times$ across multiple model families and benchmarks under a unified decoding protocol. These results show that performance-driven window-level optimization is a practical approach to improving speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [41] [Quantifying and Mitigating Premature Closure in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15000)
*Rebecca Handler,Suhana Bedi,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究定义了LLM的"过早承诺"现象，评估了五个前沿医疗LLM在此问题上的表现，发现其在结构化和开放式医疗问答中均存在高比例的不当回答行为，且安全干预措施虽有效但不足以完全解决问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过早承诺（premature closure）是人类诊断错误的重要原因，在LLM中尚未得到充分研究。论文旨在定义LLM情境下的过早承诺概念，并评估当前前沿LLM在医疗场景中是否也存在此类问题。

Method: 研究评估了5个前沿LLM在结构化问答（MedQA和AfriMed-QA数据集中移除正确答案后测试回答选择率）和开放式任务（HealthBench及医生编写的对抗性查询）中的表现，并测试了安全导向提示（safety-oriented prompting）的干预效果。

Result: 基线状态下，MedQA数据集的错误行动率为55-81%，AfriMed-QA为53-82%。开放式评估中，861个HealthBench问题平均有30%的不当回答率，对抗性查询中高达78%。安全导向提示可降低过早承诺，但残余失败仍然存在。

Conclusion: 医疗LLM在面对不确定性时存在显著的过早承诺问题，安全导向提示虽能降低但未能消除该现象。研究强调了评估医疗LLM"知其不知"能力的重要性，并指出这一缺陷可能在临床环境中带来严重风险。

Abstract: Premature closure, or committing to a conclusion before sufficient information is available, is a recognized contributor to diagnostic error but remains underexamined in large language models (LLMs). We define LLM premature closure as inappropriate commitment under uncertainty: providing an answer, recommendation, or clinical guidance when the safer response would be clarification, abstention, escalation, or refusal. We evaluated five frontier LLMs across structured and open-ended medical tasks. In MedQA (n = 500) and AfriMed-QA (n = 490) questions where the correct choice had been removed, models still selected an answer at high rates, with baseline false-action rates of 55-81% and 53-82%, respectively. In open-ended evaluation, models gave inappropriate answers on an average of 30% of 861 HealthBench questions and 78% of 191 physician-authored adversarial queries. Safety-oriented prompting reduced premature closure across models, but residual failure persisted, highlighting the need to evaluate whether medical LLMs know when not to answer.

</details>

*Peter A. Jansen*

Method: Method extraction failed

Abstract: Scientific contributions rarely develop in isolation, but instead build upon prior discoveries. We formulate the task of automated technological roadmapping as extracting scientific contributions from scholarly articles and linking them to their prerequisites. We present the Scientific Contribution Graph, a large-scale AI/NLP-domain resource containing 2 million detailed scientific contributions extracted from 230k open-access papers and connected by 12.5 million prerequisite edges. We further introduce scientific prerequisite prediction, a scientific discovery task in which models predict which existing technologies can enable future discoveries, and show that contemporary models are rapidly improving on this task, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting. We anticipate technological roadmapping resources such as this will support scientific impact assessment and automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [43] [COTCAgent: Preventive Consultation via Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15016)
*Zihan Deng,Xiaozhen Zhong,Chuanzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在纵向电子病历推理中的统计推理不足和时间依赖建模困难问题，提出COTCAgent框架，通过时序统计适配器、思维链补全层和有界补全模块三层架构实现专业临床推理，在自建数据集和HealthBench上分别取得90.47%和70.41%的Top-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在纵向电子病历推理中存在两个关键缺陷：①缺乏细粒度统计推理能力，在文本隐含定量证据时产生临床趋势和指标幻觉；②纵向病历的非均匀时序和稀缺标注阻碍模型捕获远程时间依赖性。

Method: 提出COTCAgent三层推理框架，包含：(1) 时序统计适配器(TSA)将分析计划转化为可执行代码；(2) 思维链补全(COTC)层利用症状-趋势-疾病知识库进行加权评分评估疾病风险；(3) 有界补全模块通过标准化查询和迭代评分约束获取结构化证据。

Result: 在自建数据集上达到90.47%的Top-1准确率，在HealthBench上达到70.41%，优于现有医疗智能体和主流大语言模型。

Conclusion: COTCAgent通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，消除了对复杂多模态输入的依赖，在保持较低计算开销的同时实现了高效纵向病历分析。实验表明其在医疗智能体和主流大语言模型中表现最优，为临床决策支持提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: As large language models empower healthcare, intelligent clinical decision support has developed rapidly. Longitudinal electronic health records (EHR) provide essential temporal evidence for accurate clinical diagnosis and analysis. However, current large language models have critical flaws in longitudinal EHR reasoning. First, lacking fine-grained statistical reasoning, they often hallucinate clinical trends and metrics when quantitative evidence is textually implied, biasing diagnostic inference. Second, non-uniform time series and scarce labels in longitudinal EHR hinder models from capturing long-range temporal dependencies, limiting reliable clinical reasoning. To address the above limitations, this work presents the Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion Agent (COTCAgent), a hierarchical reasoning framework for longitudinal electronic health records. It consists of three core modules. The Temporal-Statistics Adapter (TSA) converts analytical plans into executable code for standardized trend output. The Chain-of-Thought Completion (COTC) layer leverages a symptom-trend-disease knowledge base with weighted scoring to evaluate disease risk, while the bounded completion module acquires structured evidence through standardized inquiries and iterative scoring constraints to ensure rigorous reasoning. By decoupling statistical computation, feature matching, and language generation, the framework eliminates reliance on complex multi-modal inputs and enables efficient longitudinal record analysis with lower computational overhead. Experimental results show that COTCAgent powered by Baichuan-M2 achieves 90.47% Top-1 accuracy on the self-built dataset and 70.41% on HealthBench, outperforming existing medical agents and mainstream large language models. The code is available at https://github.com/FrankDengAI/COTCAgent/.

</details>


### [44] [From Scenes to Elements: Multi-Granularity Evidence Retrieval for Verifiable Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15019)
*Guanhua Chen,Chuyue Huang,Yutong Yao,Shudong Liu,Xueqing Song,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper addresses the coarse granularity problem in multimodal RAG systems by introducing GranuVistaVQA benchmark and GranuRAG framework. GranuRAG treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through element-level detection, multi-granularity alignment, and attribution-constrained generation, achieving 29.2% improvement over baselines while enabling transparent error diagnosis.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current multimodal RAG systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a fundamental mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. This limitation prevents users from diagnosing where and why the system fails, particularly in scenarios involving partial observations where individual images contain only subsets of entities.

Method: The paper proposes a multi-granularity framework called GranuRAG with three core stages: (1) Element-level detection and classification to identify fine-grained visual units; (2) Multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval across different granularity levels; (3) Attribution-constrained generation to ensure generated outputs are properly grounded. Additionally, the authors introduce GranuVistaVQA, a new benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints.

Result: Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines on the proposed benchmark, validating the effectiveness of element-level retrieval and multi-granularity alignment.

Conclusion: By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, the proposed GranuRAG framework enables transparent error diagnosis, addressing the fundamental problem of unverifiable failures in existing multimodal RAG systems.

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. We introduce GranuVistaVQA, a multimodal benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints, capturing the partial observation challenge where individual images contain only subsets of entities. We further propose GranuRAG, a multi-granularity framework that treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through three stages: element-level detection and classification, multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval, and attribution-constrained generation. By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, our approach enables transparent error diagnosis. Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines for this task.

</details>


### [45] [Concurrency without Model Changes: Future-based Asynchronous Function Calling for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15077)
*Guangyu Feng,Huanzhi Mao,Prabal Dutta,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AsyncFC通过解耦LLM解码与函数执行，实现解码与执行的重叠以及函数间并行，显著降低延迟且保持准确性，无需微调或修改协议，并揭示了LLM具有推理符号化未来的原生能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体的核心能力——函数调用（或工具使用）通常受同步执行语义约束。在同步语义下，LLM解码被阻塞直到每个函数调用完成，导致端到端延迟不断增加。

Method: AsyncFC是纯执行层框架，解耦LLM解码与函数执行，支持解码与函数执行的重叠，以及依赖许可时的函数间并行处理。该框架无需微调或修改标准同步函数调用协议。

Result: 在标准函数调用基准测试和适配的软件工程基准测试中，AsyncFC显著降低端到端任务完成时间，同时保持任务准确性。

Conclusion: AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while preserving task accuracy across multiple benchmarks, revealing that LLMs have native capability to reason over symbolic futures representing unresolved execution results.

Abstract: Function calling, also known as tool use, is a core capability of modern LLM agents but is typically constrained by synchronous execution semantics. Under these semantics, LLM decoding is blocked until each function call completes, resulting in increasing end-to-end latency. In this work, we introduce AsyncFC, a pure execution-layer framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution, enabling overlap between model decoding and function execution as well as inter-function parallelism when dependencies permit. AsyncFC layers over existing models and unmodified function implementations, requiring no fine-tuning or changes to the standard synchronous function-calling protocol. Across standard function-calling benchmarks and adapted software engineering benchmarks, AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while preserving task accuracy. Furthermore, these results reveal that LLMs possess a native capability to reason over symbolic futures that represent unresolved execution results, enabling an asynchronous paradigm for model-tool interaction.

</details>


### [46] [ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081)
*Ziyin Zhang,Zihan Liao,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ML-Embed introduces a 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning framework to address computational costs, linguistic inclusivity, and transparency barriers in text embeddings. The suite offers models from 140M to 8B parameters, achieving state-of-the-art results on 9 of 17 MTEB benchmarks while being fully open-source.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current text embedding development faces three critical barriers: (1) prohibitive computational costs, (2) narrow linguistic focus neglecting most world languages, and (3) lack of transparency from closed-source/open-weight models hindering research progress.

Method: 3D-ML framework combining three strategies: (1) Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) for storage efficiency, (2) Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL) for flexible inference-time depth control, and (3) Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. Models trained on curated massively multilingual dataset with 140M to 8B parameter range.

Result: On 430 tasks across 17 MTEB benchmarks, ML-Embed sets new records on 9 benchmarks; notably strong performance on low-resource languages.

Conclusion: ML-Embed provides a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems; their released models demonstrate strong performance, particularly in low-resource languages, while maintaining transparency through full open-source release.

Abstract: The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [47] [Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Xiaoming Huang,Piao Tong,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Recall Thinking (SRT)框架，通过自研判别链机制解决多轮对话中的长距离上下文依赖和信息稀疏问题，无需外部模块即可实现可解释的上下文召回推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在多轮对话系统中面临的两个核心挑战：跨非相邻轮次的依赖追踪困难，以及对话延长后关键信息稀疏、计算效率低下的问题。

Method: 采用三阶段架构：(1)依赖构建：将对话历史转换为自研判别链；(2)能力初始化：通过训练使模型习得recall token能力；(3)推理改进：利用可验证奖励优化召回与推理的准确率。

Result: 在多个数据集上实验验证，F1提升4.7%，端到端延迟降低14.7%，实现了推理延迟与精度的平衡，性能超越现有最优方法。

Conclusion: SRT通过自研判别链和可验证奖励机制，在长对话场景下实现了精度与效率的平衡。实验表明该方法在多个数据集上F1提升4.7%，延迟降低14.7%，优于现有最优方法，无需外部模块即可实现可解释的上下文召回推理。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [48] [MeMo: Memory as a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15156)
*Ryan Wei Heng Quek,Sanghyuk Lee,Alfred Wei Lun Leong,Arun Verma,Alok Prakash,Nancy F. Chen,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MeMo框架，通过将新知识编码到独立记忆模型来更新LLM，同时保持LLM参数不变。该方法具有捕捉跨文档关系、抗检索噪声、避免灾难性遗忘、无需访问LLM权重、支持黑盒API调用、推理成本与语料库规模无关等优势，在多个基准测试上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练后参数冻结，难以及时获取领域特定知识，需要高效机制注入新知识。

Method: 将新知识编码到专用记忆模型中，LLM参数保持冻结，支持即插即用集成。

Result: 在BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA和MuSiQue三个基准测试上达到强竞争力性能。

Conclusion: MeMo通过独立记忆模型实现知识更新，避免了参数修改的诸多缺陷，为LLM知识更新提供了灵活高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.

</details>


### [49] [Text Knows What, Tables Know When: Clinical Timeline Reconstruction via Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15168)
*Sayantan Kumar,Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Juyong Kim,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出检索增强的多模态对齐框架，通过图式多步策略将非结构化临床叙事与结构化EHR数据融合以重建临床时间线。实验证明该方法显著提升了时间戳准确性和时间一致性，揭示了多模态对齐在临床事件重建中的独特价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间线重建对于建模患者轨迹和风险预测至关重要。现有数据源存在互补性缺陷：非结构化临床叙事语义丰富但时间精度不足；结构化EHR数据时间精确但遗漏大量临床有意义的事件。

Method: 提出了检索增强的多模态对齐框架，将时间线重建建模为基于图的逐步过程：首先从叙事文本中提取核心锚定事件构建初始时间骨架，其次将非核心事件相对于该骨架进行定位，最后利用检索到的结构化EHR记录作为外部时间证据进行校准。在i2m4基准数据集（MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV）上使用指令微调的大语言模型进行评估。

Result: 多模态管道在绝对时间戳准确性（AULTC）和时间一致性方面均优于单模态文本重建方法，在几乎所有评估模型上均取得了一致的改进。实证分析显示34.8%的文本衍生事件在表格记录中完全缺失，证明了多模态对齐的价值。

Conclusion: 通过多模态对齐框架对齐文本和结构化数据，能够生成比单一数据源更完整、时间精度更高的临床时间线重建结果。实验表明该方法在多个评估模型上一致性地提升了时间戳准确性和时间一致性，且无需牺牲事件匹配率。

Abstract: Reconstructing precise clinical timelines is essential for modeling patient trajectories and forecasting risk in complex, heterogeneous conditions like sepsis. While unstructured clinical narratives offer semantically rich and contextually complete descriptions of a patient's course, they often lack temporal precision and contain ambiguous event timing. Conversely, structured electronic health record (EHR) data provides precise temporal anchors but misses a substantial portion of clinically meaningful events. We introduce a retrieval-augmented multimodal alignment framework that bridges this gap to improve the temporal precision of absolute clinical timelines extracted from text. Our approach formulates timeline reconstruction as a graph-based multistep process: it first extracts central anchor events from narratives to build an initial temporal scaffold, places non-central events relative to this backbone, and then calibrates the timeline using retrieved structured EHR rows as external temporal evidence. Evaluated using instruction-tuned large language models on the i2m4 benchmark spanning MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, our multimodal pipeline consistently improves absolute timestamp accuracy (AULTC) and improves temporal concordance across nearly all evaluated models over unimodal text-only reconstruction, without compromising event match rates. Furthermore, our empirical gap analysis reveals that 34.8% of text-derived events are entirely absent from tabular records, demonstrating that aligning these modalities can produce a more temporally faithful and clinically informative reconstruction of patient trajectories than either source alone.

</details>


### [50] [Is Grep All You Need? How Agent Harnesses Reshape Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15184)
*Sahil Sen,Akhil Kasturi,Elias Lumer,Anmol Gulati,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过两项实验系统比较了LLM agent系统中grep与向量检索策略的性能差异，发现grep检索表现更优，同时揭示了agent框架选择对最终性能的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献缺乏对检索策略选择与agent架构及工具调用范式之间交互关系的系统比较；工具输出呈现方式、检索在更多无关上下文干扰下的性能表现等实际维度尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 设计两项实验：实验1在LongMemEval的116个问题上比较grep和向量检索，使用Chronos自定义agent框架及Claude Code、Codex、 Gemini CLI等提供商CLI，测试内联工具结果和文件读取型工具结果两种呈现方式；实验2在检索中逐步混入无关对话历史，增加干扰信息。

Result: grep检索在实验1中的准确率普遍高于向量检索；但整体分数仍强烈依赖于具体的harness和工具调用方式，即使底层对话数据相同。

Conclusion: 在Chronos和提供商CLI中，grep检索策略整体表现优于向量检索；同时，整体性能高度依赖于agent框架和工具调用方式的选择，即使底层对话数据相同。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Model (LLM) agents have enabled complex agentic workflows where models autonomously retrieve information, call tools, and reason over large corpora to complete tasks on behalf of users. Despite the growing adoption of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in agentic search systems, existing literature lacks a systematic comparison of how retrieval strategy choice interacts with agent architecture and tool-calling paradigm. Important practical dimensions, including how tool outputs are presented to the model and how performance changes when searches must cope with more irrelevant surrounding text, remain under-explored in agent loops. This paper reports an empirical study organized into two experiments. Experiment 1 compares grep and vector retrieval on a 116-question sample from LongMemEval, using a custom agent harness (Chronos) and provider-native CLI harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, and Gemini CLI), for both inline tool results and file-based tool results that the model reads separately. Experiment 2 compares grep-only and vector-only retrieval while progressively mixing in additional unrelated conversation history, so that each query is embedded in more distracting material alongside the passages that matter. Across Chronos and the provider CLIs, grep generally yields higher accuracy than vector retrieval in our comparisons in experiment 1; at the same time, overall scores still depend strongly on which harness and tool-calling style is used, even when the underlying conversation data are the same.

</details>

### [51] [Clustering with Locally Bounded Ignorance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13917)
*Jaroslav Garvardt,Christian Komusiewicz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究Correlation Clustering问题中模糊边图结构对计算复杂度的影响，证明了在退化度和闭包参数下存在多项式核，并给出了在限制图结构下的硬度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Correlation Clustering与Edge Multicut的紧密联系，尽管已知成本至多k的划分问题是FPT（固定参数可解），但多项式核的存在被认为是不可行的。因此，本文旨在探究模糊边图的特殊结构能否改善这一复杂性结果，特别是考察在图结构受到约束时的核化可能性。

Method: 作者通过研究模糊边图（由权重为0的边诱导的图）的结构特性来分析问题复杂度。具体地，利用退化度(degeneracy)和闭包(closure)作为参数化工具，设计了核化算法来证明正性结果；通过归约技术证明了限制图结构下的硬度下界。

Result: 证明了当参数化为k+d（d为模糊边图的退化度）或k+c（c为模糊边图的闭包）时，Correlation Clustering存在多项式问题核。同时，对于边和非边诱导的图结构受到严格限制的情形，证明了问题的硬度。

Conclusion: Correlation Clustering在参数化复杂性方面取得了进展，证明了在模糊边图的退化度d或闭包c参数下存在多项式核，但同时也揭示了当图结构受到严格限制时的计算硬度，从而深化了对该问题参数敏感性的理解。

Abstract: In Correlation Clustering, the input is a graph $G=(V,E)$ with weight function $ω: {V \choose 2}\to Z$
  and the task is to partition the vertex set into clusters such that
  the total weight of edges between clusters and missing edges
  inside clusters is minimized. Due to close connections
  between Correlation Clustering and Edge Multicut,
  deciding whether there is a partition with total cost at most $k$ is
  FPT with respect to $k$ but a polynomial kernel is presumably
  impossible. We study the influence of the structure of the fuzzy
  edge graph, that is, the graph induced by the weight-0 edges, on the
  problem complexity. We show in particular that Correlation
  Clustering admits a polynomial problem kernel when parameterized
  by $k+d$, where $d$ is the degeneracy of the fuzzy edge graph, and when
  parameterized by $k+c$, where $c$ is the closure of the fuzzy edge
  graph. We complement these positive results by showing hardness for
  several settings where the graph induced by the edges and nonedges has very restricted structure.

</details>


### [52] [Non-Redundancy of Low-Arity Symmetric Boolean CSPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14007)
*Amatya Sharma,Santhoshini Velusamy*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文对布尔对称CSP中arity ≤ 5谓词的非冗余性参数进行了近乎完整的分类，通过引入t-balancedness概念建立了上下界判据，并结合计算实验与归约框架解决了所有低元谓词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非冗余性是CSP结构参数中最重要的参数之一，控制着核化、精确/近似稀疏化和精确流式复杂度。然而此前缺乏对具体谓词非冗余性增长的系统分类研究。该研究聚焦于仅依赖于Hamming权重的对称布尔关系（即二元或多元约束满足问题中的自然谓词如割和k-SAT子句）。

Method: 上界证明：引入t-balancedness概念，证明其等价于存在度为t的多线性多项式表示关系R，从而得到NRD_n(R)=O(n^t)的界。下界证明：采用Carbonnel (CP 2022)的框架，通过从k元OR到目标谓词的特殊归约来继承下界。此外还结合计算实验验证分类结果。

Result: 1. 给出arity ≤ 5对称布尔谓词的非冗余性增长的近乎完整分类；2. 完全确定所有arity ≤ 4的谓词；3. 确定除两个外的所有arity-5谓词；4. 建立t-balancedness与度-t多项式表示的等价性；5. 未解决的arity-5谓词具有Ω(n²)下界和O(n³)上界。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了对称布尔CSP（非冗余参数）的近乎完整分类，仅有两个arity-5谓词未完全确定，其确切分类可归约为极值集合系统问题。这为CSP参数化复杂性的理论框架提供了重要的分类结果。

Abstract: Non-redundancy, introduced by Bessiere, Carbonnel, and Katsirelos (AAAI 2020), is a structural parameter for Constraint Satisfaction Problems ($\mathsf{CSPs}$) that governs kernelization, exact and approximate sparsification, and exact streaming complexity. It is the largest size of a $\mathsf{CSP}$ instance admitting no smaller subinstance with the same satisfying assignments.
  We study non-redundancy $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for Boolean symmetric $\mathsf{CSPs}$ defined by an $r$-ary relation $R$ whose value depends only on Hamming weight. An instance of $\mathsf{CSP}(R)$ has $n$ variables and constraints given by $r$-tuples; a constraint is satisfied exactly when the induced tuple lies in $R$. This class includes natural predicates such as cuts and $k$-SAT clauses. Our main result is a near-complete classification of the asymptotic growth of $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for symmetric Boolean predicates of arity at most $5$. Using computational experiments and algebraic upper- and lower-bound criteria, we resolve every predicate of arity at most $4$ and all but two predicates of arity $5$.
  For upper bounds, we introduce $t$-balancedness, a lifted, higher-degree version of the balancedness notion of Chen, Jansen, and Pieterse (Algorithmica 2020). We prove that $t$-balancedness is equivalent to the existence of degree-$t$ multilinear polynomials capturing $R$, and hence implies $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)=O(n^t)$. For lower bounds, we use Carbonnel's (CP 2022) framework: predicates admitting a special reduction from $k$-ary OR inherit OR's lower bound $Ω(n^k)$. The only unresolved arity-$5$ predicates in our framework have bounds $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^3)$; we reduce their exact classification to natural extremal set-system questions.

</details>


### [53] [Hybrid Sketching Methods for Dynamic Connectivity on Sparse Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15173)
*Quinten De Man,Gilvir Gill,Michael A. Bender,Laxman Dhulipala,David Tench*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文利用现实世界图中普遍存在的dense core结构，提出混合sketching方法（对稠密核心使用线性sketch，对稀疏外围无损存储），结合新设计的BalloonSketch l0采样器，实现了首个在实际图上节省空间的基于sketch的动态连通性算法，在各种密度图上均有显著空间节省（15%-97%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图连通性的理论突破（基于per-vertex线性sketch的算法）虽然能在理论上实现Θ(V log²V)的空间复杂度，但per-vertex sketch每个顶点需要数千字节，在现实世界的图上并不实用——只有在图变得极度稠密时sketching才能节省空间。

Method: 提出混合sketching方法：对稠密核心顶点使用per-vertex线性sketch，对稀疏外围顶点无损存储；设计BalloonSketch（一种l0采样器），将per-vertex sketch大小减少最多8倍；开发模块化系统HybridSCALE，将无损组件和基于sketch的组件作为子程序。

Result: 设计的新混合算法在完全动态和半流式连通性场景下实现空间复杂度O(min{V+E, V log V log(2+E/V)}) w.h.p.，同时在稀疏图上匹配无损边界、稠密图上匹配sketching边界，并在中间密度区域超越两者。

Conclusion: HybridSCALE在稀疏图(平均度<100)上节省15%空间，在中等密度图(平均度~100-1000)上节省92%，在稠密图(平均度>1000)上节省97%，首次实现了基于sketch的动态连通性算法在实际图上的空间节省。

Abstract: Dynamic connectivity is a fundamental dynamic graph problem, and recent algorithmic breakthroughs on dynamic graph sketching have reshaped what is theoretically possible: by encoding the graph as per-vertex linear sketches, these algorithms solve dynamic connectivity in only $Θ(V \log^2 V)$ space, independent of the number of edges,outperforming lossless $Θ(V+E)$-space structures that grow as the graph becomes denser. Prior to this work, no practical dynamic connectivity algorithm has been able to translate these theoretical breakthroughs into space savings on real-world graphs. The main obstacle is that per-vertex sketches cost thousands of bytes per vertex, so sketching only pays off once the graph becomes extremely dense.
  We observe that sparse real-world graphs are often not uniformly sparse, these graphs can contain dense cores on a small subset of vertices that account for a large fraction of edges. We exploit this structure via hybrid sketching: sketch only the dense core, and store the sparse periphery losslessly.
  We design new hybrid algorithms for fully-dynamic and semi-streaming connectivity with space $O(\min\{V+E, V \log V \log(2+E/V)\})$ w.h.p., simultaneously matching the lossless bound on sparse graphs, the sketching bound on dense graphs, and improving on both in an intermediate regime. A key component is BalloonSketch, a new l0-sampler reducing per-vertex sketch sizes by up to 8x. We implement HybridSCALE, a modular system treating the lossless and sketch-based components as subroutines. HybridSCALE is the first sketch-based dynamic connectivity system to save space on common real-world graphs. Compared to the state-of-the-art lossless baseline, HybridSCALE saves up to 15% space on sparse graphs (average degree < 100), up to 92% on intermediate density graphs (average degree ~ 100-1000), and up to 97% on dense graphs (average degree > 1000).

</details>


### [54] [New Algorithms for Parity-SAT and Its Bounded-Occurrence Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14093)
*Sanjay Jain,Junqiang Peng,Frank Stephan,Haoyun Tang,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对Parity-SAT问题，研究变量出现次数受限的Parity-d-occ-SAT变体。通过随机化算法、精细分支技术和公式长度参数化，在三个层面取得突破：任意固定d下打破2^m屏障、d=2时达到亚指数复杂度、以及一般Parity-SAT的O*(1.1052^L)算法。这些结果均优于对应精确计数问题的最佳已知界限，证明了奇偶性计算相比精确模型计数具有本质性的算法优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parity-SAT作为典型的⊕P完全问题，在强指数时间假设（SETH）下无法突破2^n或2^m的指数屏障。本文旨在通过结构限制和奇偶性的精细利用来重新审视这一经典障碍，探索在特定条件下打破该障碍的可能性。

Method: 采用随机化算法设计、精细的分支算法优化以及公式长度参数化技术。针对变量出现次数受限的Parity-d-occ-SAT问题，利用结构化分析和奇偶性特征的精细利用来设计算法。

Result: 取得三项核心成果：1）针对Parity-d-occ-SAT的随机化算法达到O*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})，为任意固定d打破2^m屏障；2）d=2时获得更优的分支算法，时间复杂度为O*(1.1193^n)或O*(1.3248^m)；3）基于d=2的结构洞察，设计出针对一般Parity-SAT的O*(1.1052^L)算法。所有算法均使用多项式空间。

Conclusion: 本文证明了奇偶性（Parity）在结构化限制下确实比精确计数（#SAT）具有更高效的算法， bounded occurrence 策略可以系统性地绕过经典的指数时间壁垒。这为parity计算领域提供了新的理论突破和算法设计范式。

Abstract: Parity-SAT is the problem of determining whether a given CNF formula has an odd number of satisfying assignments. As a canonical $\oplus$P-complete problem, it represents a fundamental variant of the exact model counting problem (#SAT). Under the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH), Parity-SAT admits no $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^n)$-time or $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^m)$-time algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ and $m$ denote the numbers of variables and clauses, respectively. Thus, breaking the $2^n$ or $2^m$ barrier appears impossible in full generality.
  In this work, we revisit this barrier through structural restrictions and a refined exploitation of parity. We study Parity-$d$-occ-SAT, where each variable appears in at most $d$ clauses, and obtain three main results. First, we design {a randomized} $O^*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})$-time algorithm, thereby breaking the $2^m$ barrier for every fixed $d$. Second, for the special case $d=2$, we develop a significantly sharper branching algorithm running in $O^*(1.1193^n)$ time or $O^*(1.3248^m)$ time. Third, leveraging the structural insights underlying the $d=2$ case, we obtain an $O^*(1.1052^L)$-time algorithm for general Parity-SAT, where $L$ denotes the formula length. All algorithms use only polynomial space. Notably, our running-time bounds are better than the best known bounds for the corresponding exact counting counterparts, highlighting a genuine algorithmic advantage of parity over counting. Conceptually, our results demonstrate that parity admits finer structural reductions and more efficient branching than exact model counting, and that bounded occurrence can be systematically leveraged to circumvent classical exponential barriers.

</details>


### [55] [Fast Leaf-to-Ancestor Minimum Query in the Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14112)
*Aleksey Upirvitskiy,Aleksandr Levin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study leaf-to-ancestor path-minimum queries on a rooted, weighted tree in the oracle model, where the only allowed value operation is a comparison oracle on edge (or node) weights. We give a static data structure that, after O(n log h) preprocessing time, space, and oracle calls (where $n$ is the number of nodes and $h$ is the tree height), answers any leaf-to-ancestor query in O(1) worst-case time with zero oracle calls at query time. The method combines (I) an edge-to-node weight conversion with a deterministic tie-break to obtain a total order; (II) ladder (longest-path) decomposition; (III) binary lifting; and (IV) sparse-table RMQ built over ladder arrays, storing indices selected via the oracle during preprocessing. We also show that the preprocessing oracle-comparison bound is tight in the deterministic comparison model.

</details>


### [56] [Stochastic Matching via Local Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14195)
*Sara Ahmadian,Edith Cohen,Mohammad Roghani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究在线匹配问题中通信带宽受限的场景，提出两阶段本地稀疏化框架，通过本地剪枝到k条边后由中心协调器优化全局匹配，在理论上量化近似比与spread的关系，并在NYC打车数据和合成数据上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线随机匹配问题假设匹配决策必须即时且不可撤销，但现代去中心化系统（如实时打车和分布式云计算）的瓶颈在于本地通信带宽，而非匹配时机。

Method: 提出参数化的本地选择策略，以期望实例的分数解为输入；通过分析解的"spread"量化近似比，证明在足够spread条件下，稀疏化器能全局保持最大匹配期望规模。

Result: 在足够spread条件下，稀疏化器能全局保持最大匹配期望规模；实验中接近最优的全局匹配可通过极度受限的本地预算实现，显著优于标准在线基准。

Conclusion: 实验验证了所提方法的有效性，在纽约市打车数据集和对抗性合成基准测试中，即使本地预算极度受限，仍能接近最优的全局匹配效果，显著优于标准在线基准算法。

Abstract: The classic online stochastic matching problem typically requires immediate and irrevocable matching decisions. However, in many modern decentralized systems such as real-time ride-hailing and distributed cloud computing, the primary bottleneck is often local communication bandwidth rather than the timing of the match itself. We formalize this challenge by introducing a two-stage local sparsification framework. In this setting, arriving requests must prune their realized compatibility sets to a strict budget of $k$ edges before a central coordinator optimizes the global matching. This creates a "middle ground" between local information constraints and global optimization utility.
  We propose a local selection strategy, parametrized by a fractional solution of the expected instance. Theoretically, we quantify the approximation ratio as a function of the solution's {\em spread}. We prove that under sufficient spread, our sparsifier globally preserves the expected size of the maximum matching. Empirically, we demonstrate the robustness of our approach using the New York City ride-hailing datasets and adversarial synthetic benchmarks. Our results show that near-optimal global matching is achievable even with highly constrained local budgets, significantly outperforming standard online baselines.

</details>


### [57] [Semi-Streaming Algorithms for Submodular Maximization under Random Arrival Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14296)
*Niv Buchbinder,Moran Feldman,Siyue Liu,Sherry Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究随机顺序半流算法在拟阵、p-奇偶拟阵、p-交换系统等约束下的次模函数最大化问题，提出将离线算法转换为随机顺序半流算法的通用方法，并证明对拟阵约束可实现指数级的pass数改进，首次在这些约束类上超越对抗顺序算法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决次模函数最大化问题在不同约束类上的半流算法效率问题，缩小随机顺序与对抗顺序算法之间的性能差距，并探索随机顺序相对于对抗顺序的潜在优势。

Method: 提出两个技术工具：(1) 将离线算法转换为随机顺序半流算法的通用框架；(2) 针对拟阵约束的半流算法变体。采用随机顺序处理数据流，突破传统对抗顺序的限制。

Result: 在多数约束类上首次超越已知对抗顺序结果；对拟阵约束实现了从指数级到常数级pass数的指数级改进；证明了p系统上类似的随机顺序优势分离现象。

Conclusion: 该研究在随机顺序半流算法领域取得重要进展，首次在多个约束类上超越对抗顺序算法，并证明了随机顺序相对于对抗顺序的指数级优势，特别是对拟阵约束下的单调次模函数最大化问题。

Abstract: We study random order semi-streaming algorithms for submodular maximization under a wide range of combinatorial constraint classes, including matroids, matroid $p$-parity, $p$-exchange systems and $p$-systems. For most of these classes of constraints, our results are the first improvement over what is known to be achievable for adversarial order. For matroids, matching and $p$-matchoids, previous random order results were known, and we improve over some of these as well. In the case of matroids, our improved results show a separation between adversarial and random order semi-streaming algorithms, and exponentially improve the number of passes necessary for getting $1 - 1/e - \varepsilon$ approximation for maximizing a monotone submodular function subject to a matroid constraint. We also prove a new hardness result showing a similar separation for $p$-systems. Our results are based on two new technical tools. One tool provides a general way to translate offline algorithms for many classes of constraints into random order semi-streaming algorithms. The other tool is a semi-streaming variant of a recently proposed offline algorithm for matroid constraints.

</details>


### [58] [zSort: Stable Distribution Sort using Z-Score Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14419)
*Hriday Jain,Ketan Sabale,Aditya Shastri,Hiren Kumar Thakkar,Ashutosh Londhe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: zSort是一种基于z-score的自适应分布排序算法，在保证稳定性的同时避免了随键宽增加的传递复杂性，在10^7元素数据集上实现比传统稳定排序快3-4.5倍，并达到与不稳定高性能排序算法（如Skasort）相当的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序是现代数据处理的基础原语，但当前算法面临"稳定性税"困境：实践者必须在保持顺序的高吞吐量和保持稳定性的执行速度之间做出取舍。这种二分法限制了高性能数据管道的执行效率。

Method: 提出zSort算法，一种基于z-score的自适应分布排序算法。该算法通过z-score变换处理数据分布，在保证稳定性的同时避免了传统方法中随键宽增加的传递复杂性（pass complexity）。

Result: 微架构分析显示zSort的错误推测开销（19.7%）低于稳定基线和不稳定算法，IPC达1.44。在10^7元素（64位）数据集上，比比较式稳定排序快3-4.5倍；对重复多和部分有序的输入，比LSD Radix表现更好；吞吐量与Skasort等高性能不稳定算法相当；在Pdqsort擅长的自适应工作负载上同样表现良好。

Conclusion: zSort显著缩小了稳定排序与不稳定排序之间的传统性能差距，提供了一种高效、稳定的排序替代方案。

Abstract: Sorting is a foundational primitive in modern data processing, influencing the execution speed of high-performance data pipelines. However, the algorithmic landscape is currently bifurcated by a pervasive "Stability Tax": practitioners must sacrifice either order preservation for high throughput or execution speed for stability. To address these limitations, this paper introduces, zSort, an adaptive z-score based distribution sorting algorithm that guarantees stability while avoiding pass complexity that scales with key-width. The performance of the proposed technique is evaluated using Microarchitectural analysis and experimental results. Microarchitectural analysis shows that zSort achieves a lower bad-speculation overhead (19.7%) than both stable baselines and several high-performance unstable algorithms and sustains a competitive IPC of 1.44. Empirical evaluation across diverse input distributions and datasets of up to 10^7 elements (64 bit) demonstrates that zSort consistently outperforms widely used comparison based stable sorting algorithms, achieving up to 3x-4.5x speedups, and a relatively better performance compared to LSD Radix, with larger gains on duplicate heavy and partially ordered inputs. Despite providing stability, zSort achieves comparable throughput as compared to high-performance unstable algorithms such as Skasort. It also maintains this performance on adaptive workloads where methods like Pdqsort typically excel and doesn't exhibit any extreme worst case. These results indicate that zSort substantially narrows the traditional performance gap between stable and unstable sorting and provides an efficient, stable sorting alternative.

</details>


### [59] [Branch-width of represented matroids in matrix multiplication time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14428)
*Mujin Choi,Tuukka Korhonen,Sang-il Oum*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文通过利用快速矩阵乘法技术将拟阵分支宽度计算从Ω(n^3)优化至O_{k,F}(n^2) + O(n^ω)，证明了该问题的计算复杂度下界与矩阵奇异性问题相当，提供了目前最优的算法设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前算法（包括Hliněný和Oum 2008及Jeong、Kim和Oum 2021的工作）均需至少Ω(n^3)时间。该论文旨在突破这一时间壁垒，通过利用快速矩阵乘法技术实现更高效的分支宽度计算。

Method: 该算法结合了矩阵乘法加速技术与递归分解方法，在标准形式输入下可达到O_{k,F}(n^2)复杂度。核心思想是通过高效矩阵运算加速分支宽度的计算，并利用奇异性问题的归约来证明算法下界。

Result: 提出一种时间复杂度为O_{k,F}(n^2) + O(n^ω)的算法，在标准形式输入下可降至O_{k,F}(n^2)。推导出有向图秩宽和有限域上拟阵路径宽的快速算法，并给出了无穷域上分支宽度的近似算法。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了目前最快的一类计数型矩阵乘法算法，时间复杂度为O(n^ω)，其中ω < 2.3714，并在理论上证明了其最优性。同时揭示了分支宽度计算与矩阵奇异性问题之间的归约关系，确立了计算复杂度的下界。

Abstract: For an $n$-element matroid $M$ given by an $n \times n$ matrix representation over a finite field $\mathbb F$ and an integer $k$, we present an $(O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)+O(n^ω))$-time algorithm that either finds a branch-decomposition of $M$ of width at most $k$, or confirms that the branch-width of $M$ is more than $k$, where $ω< 2.3714$ is the matrix multiplication exponent, and the $O_{k,\mathbb F}(\cdot)$-notation hides factors that depend on $k$ and $\mathbb F$ in a computable manner. All previous algorithms including Hliněný and Oum [SIAM J. Comput. (2008)] and Jeong, Kim, and Oum [SIAM J. Discrete Math. (2021)] run in at least $Ω(n^3)$ time. Moreover, if the input matrix representation is given by a standard form, our algorithm runs in $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$-time, since $O(n^ω)$-time is only needed for finding a standard form of the input matrix. When $M$ is given by an $m \times n$ matrix, the overhead for finding a standard form is $O(mn \min(m,n)^{ω-2})$.
  As corollaries, we obtain faster algorithms for rank-width of directed graphs and path-width of matroids represented over a fixed finite field. Furthermore, we also present an approximation algorithm for finding branch-width that works on infinite fields provided that the input matrix is of a standard form and contains a bounded number of distinct values of entries.
  To suggest that our algorithm is optimal, we observe that for every field $\mathbb F$, deciding whether the branch-width of a matroid represented over $\mathbb F$ is $0$ is as hard as deciding whether a square matrix over $\mathbb F$ is singular. Under the assumption that singularity testing requires $Ω(n^ω)$-time, this implies that the overhead of $O(n^ω)$ is unavoidable. We also show strengthenings of this observation to rule out some approximations under this assumption.

</details>


### [60] [Hardness of Burning Number Problem on Regular Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14730)
*Dhanyamol Antony,L. Sunil Chandran,Anita Das,Shirish Gosavi,Dalu Jacob,Shashanka Kulamarva*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 燃烧数问题（BNP）在连通正则图上被证明是NP完全且APX难的。特别地，对所有固定度数d≥3的连通d正则图，BNP的复杂度都得到了刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The Burning Number Problem (BNP) models information/contagion spread in networks. While BNP was known to be NP-complete on restricted classes like path forests, its complexity on connected regular graphs—a natural and previously unexplored class—had not been studied.

Method: The paper proves hardness results by establishing NP-completeness and APX-hardness for BNP on connected regular graphs, specifically for cubic graphs and extending to all fixed degrees d ≥ 4.

Result: BNP is proved to be NP-complete on connected cubic graphs and APX-hard under this restriction. Additionally, BNP remains APX-hard on connected d-regular graphs for every fixed d ≥ 4.

Conclusion: The results establish that BNP is computationally hard on connected regular graphs, providing complexity characterizations for cubic graphs and all fixed higher degrees.

Abstract: The Burning Number Problem (BNP) models the spread of information or contagion in a network through a discrete-time process on a graph. At each step, one new vertex is selected as a burning source, while fire simultaneously spreads from previously burned vertices to their neighbors. The burning number of a graph is the minimum number of steps required to burn all vertices. The decision version asks whether the burning number is at most a given integer $k$. BNP is known to be NP-complete even on restricted graph classes such as path forests. We study BNP on connected regular graphs, a natural and previously unexplored graph class. We prove that BNP is NP-complete on connected cubic graphs, and moreover APX-hard under this restriction. We further show that BNP remains APX-hard on connected $d$-regular graphs for every fixed $d \geq 4$.

</details>

### [61] [Distributed Statistical Zero-Knowledge Proofs via Sumcheck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14015)
*Benjamin Jauregui,Masayuki Miyamoto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We study distributed zero-knowledge proofs, introduced by Bick, Kol, and Oshman (SODA 2022). While distributed interactive proofs have advanced rapidly, general-purpose techniques for distributed zero-knowledge remain limited and mostly problem-specific. We address this gap by introducing distributed statistical zero-knowledge, requiring that each node's view be simulatable within negligible statistical distance, and by lifting the classical Sumcheck protocol (Lund, Fortnow, Karloff, and Nisan, FOCS 1990) into a modular primitive for distributed zero-knowledge proofs.
  Our main contribution is a distributed zero-knowledge implementation of Sumcheck. Given oracle access to a polynomial F over a finite field $\mathbb{F}$ with N variables, we design a protocol verifying claims of the form $\sum_{x\in\mathbb{F}} F(x)=a$ using $O(N)$ rounds of $O(\log |\mathbb{F}|)$-bit messages, while achieving statistical zero-knowledge and small soundness error.
  We apply this primitive to two problems. For non-k-colorability, we obtain an $O(n)$-round distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof deciding whether a graph is not k-colorable, for any constant k, using $O(log^{1+o(1)} n)$-bit messages. This is the first nontrivial distributed interactive proof for this problem, even without zero-knowledge guarantees. For Subgraph Counting, we obtain an $O(k \log n)$-round, $O(k \log n)$-bit distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof for counting copies of a given k-node pattern, improving previous distributed interactive proofs while additionally providing statistical zero-knowledge. Finally, we show that additional round compression of Sumcheck is problem-dependent: for non-3-colorability on constant-degree graphs, we prove a lower bound excluding $o(n/\log n)$ rounds under polynomial-time local computation.

</details>


### [62] [Analysis of wireless network access logs for a hierarchical characterization of user mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14540)
*Francisco Talavera,Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Wi-Fi数据构建层次化用户移动性模型，通过接入点地理空间特征递归分组降低大规模场景复杂度，采用聚类算法进行用户画像。实验验证该方法在转换矩阵估计上效果良好，层次化结构可有效降低模型复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着雾计算等新型计算范式的发展，需要精确的用户移动性模型来支持资源分配和服务部署。现有的移动性模型在处理大规模场景时复杂度较高，且缺乏对不同地理空间特征的适应性。

Method: 提出一种基于Wi-Fi连接数据构建层次化用户移动性模型的方法：1）根据接入点的地理空间特征递归分组形成多粒度层次结构；2）将用户轨迹定义为接入点序列；3）使用聚类算法进行用户画像，每个用户类型由覆盖区域间的转换矩阵和时间长度向量表征。

Result: 在巴利阿里群岛大学校园的实验结果表明：转换矩阵的均方误差分析显示效果良好；时间向量定义仍需改进；层次化模型（每栋建筑为一个区域，三层结构）比非层次化模型（整个校园作为一个区域）复杂度更低。

Conclusion: 该方法在转换矩阵估计上表现良好，层次化模型相比非层次化模型具有更低的复杂度，但时间向量定义需要进一步改进。该研究为雾计算场景中的用户移动性模拟提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a method that generates a hierarchical user mobility model from the analysis of the data available from Wi-Fi connections. The data obtained from the Wi-Fi infrastructure is defined in terms of the coverage areas of the access points that the users move through. These access points are recursively grouped into different levels of granularity based on their geospatial features. The track of a user is defined as a sequence of Wi-Fi access points, which is enough to simulate user mobility in, for example, fog scenarios. The hierarchical definition of the region under study is proposed to reduce the complexity of the model in high-scale scenarios and to increase the adaptability between scenarios with different geospatial features. The model creation is based on a user profiling method that uses a clustering algorithm and each user type is defined with a transition matrix between coverage areas and a time length vector for the areas. The method is applied to the case of the campus of the University of the Balearic Islands. From the analysis of the mean square error of the results, we determined that the proposed method obtains good results for the transition matrices, but that the time vector definition should be improved. The results also show lower complexity in the case of the hierarchical model, with one area for each building and three levels, in regard to a non-hierarchical model, with only one area and one level for the whole campus.

</details>


### [63] [Malleable Molecular Dynamics Simulations with GROMACS and DMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14655)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Íñigo Aréjula-Aísa,Berk Hess,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DMR middleware enables MPI process malleability in Slurm, and its integration with GROMACS allows dynamic adaptation of MPI process counts, achieving node-hour savings for time-varying workloads while managing reconfiguration overhead.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Static resource allocations in HPC cause inefficiencies (idle resources, queue delays, higher node-hour costs) when workloads are time-varying, necessitating dynamic resource management solutions.

Method: Integration of DMR middleware with GROMACS via communication-efficiency-aware reconfiguration combined with native checkpoint/restart mechanism; experimental evaluation on MareNostrum 5 supercomputer comparing dynamic runs against static executions.

Result: Quantified reconfiguration overheads, time-to-solution improvements, and node-hour savings for bursty GROMACS workloads on a real supercomputer system.

Conclusion: The dynamic GROMACS variant demonstrates that DMR-enabled malleability can achieve significant node-hour savings for bursty workloads while maintaining competitive time-to-solution performance.

Abstract: Static resource allocations in high-performance computing (HPC) lead to inefficiencies for time-varying workloads, causing idle resources, queue delays, and higher node-hour costs. The Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) middleware enables MPI process malleability in Slurm via a simple API decoupled from scheduler internals. In this work, we integrate DMR into the GROMACS molecular dynamics engine to obtain a malleable variant that can dynamically adapt its MPI process count by combining communication-efficiency-aware reconfiguration with GROMACS' native checkpoint/restart mechanism. We evaluate this design on the MareNostrum~5 supercomputer, comparing dynamic runs against static executions and quantifying reconfiguration overheads, time-to-solution, and node-hour savings for bursty GROMACS workloads.

</details>


### [64] [Supervised Distributed Computing: Efficiency and Robustness under a Majority of Adversarial Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14784)
*John Augustine,Henning Hillebrandt,Manish Kumar,Christian Scheideler,Julian Werthmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文扩展并改进了标准主从式并行计算范式，提出监督式分布式计算范式。该范式中监督者将任务检查工作外包给工作者，从而消除了主节点瓶颈。证明了在任意恒定比例工作者为对抗性（β<1）的情况下，均可实现稳健高效的解决方案，诚实工作者的预期工作量接近每个任务单次执行，显著优于已知的主从式和点对点方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准主从式方法中，主节点需要检查工作者是否正确执行任务，造成严重瓶颈。提出一种改进的监督式分布式计算范式。

Method: 要求存在轻量级验证机制，允许诚实工作者检查任务输出的正确性。将检查工作外包给工作者，而非由主节点检查。

Result: 对任意常数 β<1（即任意恒定比例的对抗性工作者）均可实现稳健高效的监督式解决方案。

Conclusion: 本文证明了对任意常数 β<1 都可实现稳健高效的监督式解决方案，且诚实工作者的预期工作量接近每个任务单次执行，同时要求存在轻量级验证机制供诚实工作者检查任务输出的正确性。该方法显著优于所有已知的稳健主从式和点对点方法。

Abstract: We consider a recently proposed \emph{supervised distributed computing} paradigm \cite{augustine2025supervised} that extends and refines the standard master-worker paradigm for parallel computations. In this paradigm, there is a supervisor, a source, a target, and a collection of workers. The distributed computation is given as an acyclic task graph that is known to the supervisor. The source initially stores the input and the target is supposed to store the output of the computation. The individual tasks of the computation are supposed to be executed by the workers under the guidance of the supervisor. The source, target and supervisor are assumed to be reliable, while a $β$-fraction of the workers might be adversarial, for some $β\in [0,1)$. This covers, for example, the case where a supervisor has to work with untrusted volunteers. In the standard master-worker approach, the master checks whether the workers correctly execute the assigned tasks, creating a severe bottleneck, whereas in the supervised approach, the supervisor outsources this checking to the workers. Prior to this work, only supervised solutions were known for the case that $β$ is a sufficiently small constant. We show that robust and efficient supervised solutions are possible for \emph{any} constant $β<1$ while the expected work for the honest workers is close to a \emph{single} execution per task, given that there is a lightweight verification mechanism that allows honest workers to check the correctness of task outputs, which is significantly better than all robust master-worker as well as peer-to-peer approaches known so far.

</details>


### [65] [Embedded Made Easy -- Rethinking Embedded + Cloud Software Development (WIP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14863)
*Anthony Arnold,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一语言和运行时系统的愿景，旨在解决边缘/嵌入式应用在跨边缘和云端部署中的复杂性挑战，通过单一语言和运行时实现无缝扩展，并提供集成诊断调试工具支持分布式事件的记录与重放分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘/嵌入式应用的工程和部署面临巨大复杂性：软件栈非均匀（不同组件使用不同语言和运行时）、边缘与云端的部署决策不可逆、故障诊断困难（多栈并存且调试支持有限），这些因素使得分布式应用的调试分析变得极其困难。

Method: 采用单一语言和运行时系统，实现应用在单一环境中开发、测试和部署到从资源受限控制器到大型云服务器的任意组件；通过可重定向的技术栈提供集成诊断和调试工具，支持本地记录和重放分布式事件进行调试分析。

Result: 实现应用从单一环境开发测试到任意系统组件（从资源受限控制器到大型云服务器）的无缝部署；提供集成诊断调试工具，支持分布式事件的本地记录与重放分析。

Conclusion: 提出了一个统一语言和运行时系统的愿景，支持跨边缘和云端的无缝扩展，以及记录和重放分布式事件用于调试的集成诊断工具，旨在简化边缘/嵌入式应用的工程和部署流程。

Abstract: The process of engineering and deploying applications in the edge/embedded space is massively complicated by the non-homogeneous nature of the software stack and the complexity of diagnostics & debugging. Often different languages and runtimes are used for different components of the system forcing designers to, irrevocably, make decisions about what components run on the periphery and what components run in the cloud. Further complications arise when handling and diagnosing failures in the system. Multiple stacks and, often, limited support for debugging complicate the already difficult task of analyzing distributed applications.
  This paper presents a work-in-progress vision for a unified language and runtime system that allows applications to scale seamlessly across the edge and cloud. Using a single language and runtime, applications can be developed and tested in a single environment, and then deployed to any component of the system -- from resource limited controllers to large cloud servers. Further, we outline how this retargetable stack can provide integrated diagnostics and debugging tools that allow developers to record and replay distributed events locally for analysis and debugging.

</details>
