Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental tool for compressing high-dimensional embeddings, yet existing multi-codebook methods rely on static codebooks that limit expressiveness under heterogeneous data geometry. While recent dynamic quantizers like QINCo adapt codebooks to individual inputs and improve expressiveness, their strict sequential dependencies create decoding bottlenecks. We propose Residual Quantization via Mixture of Experts (RQ-MoE), a framework combining a two-level MoE with dual-stream quantization to enable input-dependent codebook adaptation for efficient vector quantization. RQ-MoE enables dynamic codebook construction and decouples instruction from quantization, facilitating parallel decoding. Theoretically, we show that standard Residual Quantization and QINCo can be recovered as constrained special cases of RQ-MoE, and derive a guideline for setting expert dimensionality in RQ-MoE. Extensive experiments show that RQ-MoE achieves state-of-the-art or on-par performance in reconstruction and retrieval, while providing 6x-14x faster decoding than prior vector quantization methods. The implementation is available at https://github.com/KDEGroup/RQ-MoE.

</details>


### [134] [MoRe: Modular Representations for Principled Continual Representation Learning on Squantial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14364)
*Jiaqi Sun,Boyang Sun,Mohamad Rasmy,Xiangchen Song,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MoRe框架，通过在表示层面识别模块化而非架构层面，将知识分解为基本模块和特定模块的层次结构，实现持续学习中的知识保留与适应，实验验证了其有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习需要在保持原有知识的同时适应新数据。现有方法通过修改模型参数或架构以监督的、任务特定的方式解决这一问题，但根本问题是表征性的：任务需要既相互独立又有结构关联的表征，可选择性更新而不干扰其他表征，且结构应反映数据内在组织而非任务边界。在序列数据中，时间延迟依赖关系为揭示这种组织提供了自然信号。

Method: MoRe框架在表示本身识别模块化，而非在架构层面分配模块。该方法将知识分解为基本模块和特定模块的层次结构，具有可识别性保证，支持模块复用、对齐和扩展，构造性地保留旧模块。

Result: 在合成基准和真实LLM激活数据上的实验表明，MoRe能够产生可解释的层次结构，并改善了可塑性-稳定性权衡。

Conclusion: MoRe为持续适应提供了一个有原则的基础，通过表示层面的模块化分解而非架构层面的修改来实现知识保留与新知识获取的平衡。实验验证了其在合成基准和真实LLM激活数据上的有效性。

Abstract: Continual learning requires models to adapt to new data while preserving previously acquired knowledge. At its core, this challenge can be viewed as principled one-step adaptation: incorporating new information with minimal interference to existing representations. Most existing approaches address this challenge by modifying model parameters or architectures in a supervised, task-specific manner. However, the underlying issue is representational: tasks require distinct yet structured representations that can be selectively updated without disrupting representations, while structure should reflect intrinsic organization in the data rather than task boundaries. In sequential data, time-delayed dependencies provide a natural signal for uncovering this organization, revealing how fundamental representations give rise to more specific ones. Inspired by the modular organization of the human brain, we propose MoRe, a framework that identifies modularity in the representation itself rather than allocating it at the architectural level. MoRe decomposes knowledge into a hierarchy of fundamental and specific modules with identifiability guarantees, enabling principled module reuse, alignment, and expansion during adaptation while preserving old modules by construction. Experiments on synthetic benchmarks and real-world LLM activations demonstrate interpretable hierarchical structure, improved plasticity-stability trade-offs, suggesting MoRe as a principled foundation for continual adaptation

</details>


### [135] [LoMETab: Beyond Rank-1 Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14365)
*Changryeol Choi,Hyewon Park,Yujin Kwon,Gowun Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Recent tabular learning benchmarks increasingly show a tight performance cluster rather than a clear hierarchy among leading methods, spanning gradient boosted decision trees, attention-based architectures, and implicit ensembles such as TabM. As benchmark gains plateau, a complementary goal is to understand and control the mechanisms that make simple neural tabular models competitive. We propose LoMETab, a rank-$r$ generalization of multiplicative implicit ensembles. LoMETab lifts the rank-1 BatchEnsemble/TabM modulation to a rank-$r$ identity-residual Hadamard family by parameterizing each member weight as $W_k = W \odot (1 + A_kB_k^\top)$, where $W$ is shared and $(A_k, B_k)$ are member-specific low-rank factors. This exposes two practical diversity-control axes: the adapter rank $r$ and the initialization scale $σ_{\mathrm{init}}$, and we prove that for $r \ge 2$ this generalization strictly enlarges BatchEnsemble's hypothesis class. Empirically, we show that this added capacity manifests as measurable predictive diversity after training: on representative classification datasets, LoMETab sustains higher pairwise KL than an additive low-rank ablation, and $(r, σ_{\mathrm{init}})$ provides broad control over pairwise KL, varying by up to several orders of magnitude across configurations. The induced diversity is reflected in task-appropriate output-level measures: argmax disagreement for classification and ambiguity for regression, indicating that the control extends beyond pairwise KL to decision- and output-level member variation. Finally, experiments sweeping over adapter rank $r$ and initialization scale $σ_{\mathrm{init}}$ reveal that predictive performance is dataset-dependent over the $(r, σ_{\mathrm{init}})$ grid, supporting LoMETab as a controllable family of implicit ensembles rather than a fixed rank-1 construction.

</details>


### [136] [Turning Stale Gradients into Stable Gradients: Coherent Coordinate Descent with Implicit Landscape Smoothing for Lightweight Zeroth-Order Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14373)
*Chen Liang,Xiatao Sun,Qian Wang,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对零阶优化中样本效率与方差的固有矛盾，提出确定性方法CoCD，通过利用历史梯度信息实现O(1)查询复杂度，并证明更大有限差分步长反而改善收敛稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZO优化方法存在根本性权衡：标准有限差分样本效率低，随机子空间方法方差高。

Method: 提出CoCD方法，通过梯度相干性理论将历史陈旧梯度转化为计算资产，实现与带"热启动"的BCCD等效；采用更大有限差分步长产生隐式平滑效应，降低有效平滑常数。

Result: 达到O(1)每步查询复杂度，保持全局下降方向；发现反直觉结论——更大步长可降低有效平滑常数，提升收敛稳定性；在MLP、CNN、ResNet（最高27万参数）上显著优于BCCD和随机ZO方法。

Conclusion: 确定性、结构感知的更新为轻量级ZO优化提供了优于随机化的替代方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization is pivotal for scenarios where backpropagation is unavailable, such as memory-constrained on-device learning and black-box optimization. However, existing methods face a stark trade-off: they are either sample-inefficient (e.g., standard finite differences) or suffer from high variance due to randomized estimation (e.g., random subspace methods). In this work, we propose Coherent Coordinate Descent (CoCD), a deterministic, sample-efficient, and budget-aware ZO optimizer. Theoretically, we formalize the notion of gradient coherence and demonstrate that CoCD is equivalent to Block Cyclic Coordinate Descent (BCCD) with ``warm starts,'' effectively converting historical (stale) gradients from a liability into a computational asset. This mechanism enables $O(1)$ query complexity per step while maintaining global descent directions. Furthermore, we derive error bounds revealing a counter-intuitive insight: larger finite-difference step sizes can induce an implicit smoothing effect on the optimization landscape by reducing the effective smoothness constant, thereby improving convergence stability. Experiments on MLP, CNN, and ResNet architectures (up to 270k parameters) demonstrate that CoCD significantly outperforms BCCD in terms of sample efficiency and convergence loss/accuracy, and exhibits superior stability over randomized ZO methods. Our results suggest that deterministic, structure-aware updates offer a superior alternative to randomization for lightweight ZO optimization.

</details>


### [137] [Optimal Pattern Detection Tree for Symbolic Rule-Based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14374)
*Young-Chae Hong,Yangho Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OPDT模型，基于混合整数规划发现可解释的最优规则，通过BSC框架融入领域知识，在保证解最优性的同时提升规则发现的可解释性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，而符号规则发现虽能提供人类可理解的规则，但现有方法难以保证解的最优性或在模型中融入先验知识与约束条件。

Method: 提出Optimal Pattern Detection Tree (OPDT)，基于新型混合整数规划(MIP)通过二分分类发现单个最优模式；引入Branching Structure Constraints (BSC)框架将领域知识和合规要求编码到模型中。

Result: 实验表明OPDT能在合理运行时间内，对中等规模数据集发现带最优性保证的模式。

Conclusion: OPDT通过MIP框架在小规模数据集上实现了带最优性保证的规则发现，BSC框架增强了模型的实用性。

Abstract: Pattern discovery in data plays a crucial role across diverse domains, including healthcare, risk assessment, and machinery maintenance. In contrast to black-box deep learning models, symbolic rule discovery emerges as a key data mining task, generating human-interpretable rules that offer both transparency and intuitive explainability. This paper introduces the Optimal Pattern Detection Tree (OPDT), a rule-based machine learning model based on novel mixed-integer programming to discover a single optimal pattern in data through binary classification. To incorporate prior knowledge and compliance requirements, we further introduce the Branching Structure Constraints (BSC) framework, which enables decision makers to encode domain knowledge and constraints directly into the model. This optimization-based approach discovers a hidden underlying pattern in datasets, when it exists, by identifying an optimal rule that maximizes coverage while minimizing the false positive rate due to misclassification. Our computational experiments show that OPDT discovers a pattern with optimality guarantees on moderately sized datasets within reasonable runtime.

</details>


### [138] [NodeSynth: Socially Aligned Synthetic Data for AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14381)
*Qazi Mamunur Rashid,Xuan Yang,Zhengzhe Yang,Yanzhou Pan,Erin van Liemt,Darlene Neal,Kshitij Pancholi,Jamila Smith-Loud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NodeSynth是一种基于证据的合成查询生成方法，通过微调分类生成器在真实证据基础上生成社会相关测试数据，评估发现主流LLM失败率显著高于人类基准，且现有防护模型存在关键漏洞，现已开源以支持大规模模型安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI合成数据缺乏敏感领域所需的社会技术细微差别，亟需针对性方法提升数据质量

Method: NodeSynth方法论：使用基于真实世界证据微调的分类生成器(TaG)生成社会相关的合成查询

Result: 在4个主流LLM（如Claude 4.5 Haiku）上诱发高达人类基准测试5倍的失败率；消融研究证实细粒度分类扩展显著驱动失败率；独立验证揭示Llama-Guard-3等主流防护模型的严重缺陷

Conclusion: 开源端到端研究原型和数据集，实现大规模高风险模型评估和有针对性的安全干预

Abstract: Recent advancements in generative AI facilitate large-scale synthetic data generation for model evaluation. However, without targeted approaches, these datasets often lack the sociotechnical nuance required for sensitive domains. We introduce NodeSynth, an evidence-grounded methodology that generates socially relevant synthetic queries by leveraging a fine-tuned taxonomy generator (TaG) anchored in real-world evidence. Evaluated against four mainstream LLMs (e.g., Claude 4.5 Haiku), NodeSynth elicited failure rates up to five times higher than human-authored benchmarks. Ablation studies confirm that our granular taxonomic expansion significantly drives these failure rates, while independent validation reveals critical deficiencies in prominent guard models (e.g., Llama-Guard-3). We open-source our end-to-end research prototype and datasets to enable scalable, high-stakes model evaluation and targeted safety interventions (https://github.com/google-research/nodesynth).

</details>


### [139] [GeoViSTA: Geospatial Vision-Tabular Transformer for Multimodal Environment Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14406)
*Yuhao Liu,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoViSTA是一种视觉-表格融合架构，通过双侧交叉注意力和地理感知注意力机制学习来自配准栅格图像和表格数据的统一地理空间嵌入，旨在解决现有地理空间基础模型忽略社会经济数据的问题，提升复杂环境-社会-健康关联推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间基础模型未能直接建模表格形式的结构化社会经济协变量，导致模态差距，限制了其捕捉完整总体环境的能力，这对于推理复杂的环境、社会和健康相关结果至关重要。

Method: GeoViSTA采用双侧交叉注意力机制进行跨模态信息交换，并通过地理感知注意力机制将连续图像块与不规则人口普查区域标记对齐。训练采用自监督联合掩码自编码目标，强制模型利用局部空间上下文和跨模态线索恢复缺失图像块和表格行。

Result: GeoViSTA的统一嵌入在下游任务中提升了线性探测性能，在疾病特异性死亡率和火灾风险频率预测任务上超越了基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模物理环境和结构化社会经济背景产生了可迁移的地理空间表示，在疾病死亡率预测和火灾风险预测等下游任务中超越了基线方法。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining on Earth observation imagery has yielded powerful representations of the natural and built environment. However, most existing geospatial foundation models do not directly model the structured socioeconomic covariates typically stored in tabular form. This modality gap limits their ability to capture the complete total environment, which is critical for reasoning about complex environmental, social, and health-related outcomes. In this work, we propose GeoViSTA (Geospatial Vision-Tabular Transformer), a vision-tabular architecture that learns unified geospatial embeddings from co-registered gridded imagery and tabular data. GeoViSTA utilizes bilateral cross-attention to exchange spatial and semantic information across modalities, guided by a geography-aware attention mechanism that aligns continuous image patches with irregular census-tract tokens. We train GeoViSTA with a self-supervised joint masked-autoencoding objective, forcing it to recover missing image patches and tabular rows using local spatial context and cross-modal cues. Empirically, GeoViSTA's unified embeddings improve linear probing performance on high-impact downstream tasks, outperforming baselines in predicting disease-specific mortality and fire hazard frequency across held-out regions. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling the physical environment alongside structured socioeconomic context yields highly transferable representations for holistic geospatial inference.

</details>


### [140] [What if Tomorrow is the World Cup Final? Counterfactual Time Series Forecasting with Textual Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14422)
*Shuqi Gu,Yongxiang Zhao,Baoyu Jing,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper introduces counterfactual time series forecasting with textual conditions, addressing limitations of traditional methods that overlook counterfactual scenarios and simple structured conditions. They propose an evaluation framework supporting both factual and counterfactual settings without ground truth data, along with a text-attribution mechanism that separates mutable from immutable factors to improve forecasting accuracy under complex textual conditions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional time series forecasting relies on historical data or factual future conditions while overlooking counterfactual scenarios, and many existing approaches are restricted to simple structured conditions, limiting generalization to real-world complexities.

Method: The paper introduces counterfactual time series forecasting with textual conditions, a comprehensive evaluation framework for factual and counterfactual settings, and a novel text-attribution mechanism that distinguishes mutable from immutable factors.

Result: Improved forecast accuracy under sophisticated and stochastic textual conditions.

Conclusion: The proposed text-attribution mechanism distinguishes mutable from immutable factors, enabling more flexible and condition-aware forecasting, with improved accuracy under sophisticated and stochastic textual conditions. The framework supports both factual and counterfactual evaluation even without ground truth time series.

Abstract: Time series forecasting has become increasingly critical in real-world scenarios, where future sequences are influenced not only by historical patterns but also by forthcoming events. In this context, forecasting must dynamically adapt to complex and stochastic future conditions, which introduces fundamental challenges in both forecasting and evaluation. Traditional methods typically rely on historical data or factual future conditions, while overlooking counterfactual scenarios. Furthermore, many existing approaches are restricted to simple structured conditions, limiting their ability to generalize to the real-world complexities. To address these gaps, we introduce the task of counterfactual time series forecasting with textual conditions, enabling more flexible and condition-aware forecasting. We propose a comprehensive evaluation framework that encompasses both factual and counterfactual settings, even in the absence of ground truth time series. Additionally, we present a novel text-attribution mechanism that distinguishes mutable from immutable factors, thereby improving forecast accuracy under sophisticated and stochastic textual conditions. The project page is at https://seqml.github.io/TADiff/

</details>


### [141] [FrontierSmith: Synthesizing Open-Ended Coding Problems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14445)
*Runyuan He,Qiuyang Mang,Shang Zhou,Kaiyuan Liu,Hanchen Li,Huanzhi Mao,Qizheng Zhang,Zerui Li,Bo Peng,Lufeng Cheng,Tianfu Fu,Yichuan Wang,Wenhao Chai,Jingbo Shang,Alex Dimakis,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FrontierSmith系统，从封闭赛题自动生成开放式编程问题用于训练LLM编码能力，显著提升模型在开放编码任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放赛题数据稀缺且构建成本高昂，难以训练LLM完成开放式编程任务。

Method: 从竞赛编程题出发，通过目标变更、输出限制、输入泛化生成变体；采用想法发散度指标筛选多样化问题；由智能体生成测试用例和验证器。

Result: Qwen3.5-9B在FrontierCS上提升+8.82分，ALE-bench上提升+306.36分；Qwen3.5-27B分别提升+12.12和+309.12分。

Conclusion: 封闭赛题可作为长程编程数据的实用起点。

Abstract: Many real-world coding challenges are open-ended and admit no known optimal solution. Yet, recent progress in LLM coding has focused on well-defined tasks such as feature implementation, bug fixing, and competitive programming. Open-ended coding remains a weak spot for LLMs, largely because open-ended training problems are scarce and expensive to construct. Our goal is to synthesize open-ended coding problems at scale to train stronger LLM coders. We introduce FrontierSmith, an automated system for iteratively evolving open-ended problems from existing closed-ended coding tasks. Starting from competitive programming problems, FrontierSmith generates candidate open-ended variants by changing the problems'goals, restricting outputs, and generalizing inputs. It then uses a quantitative idea divergence metric to select problems that elicit genuinely diverse approaches from different solvers. Agents then generate test cases and verifiers for the surviving candidates. On two open-ended coding benchmarks, training on our synthesized data yields substantial gains over the base models: Qwen3.5-9B improves by +8.82 score on FrontierCS and +306.36 (Elo-rating-based performance) on ALE-bench; Qwen3.5-27B improves by +12.12 and +309.12, respectively. The synthesized problems also make agents take more turns and use more tokens, similar to human-curated ones, suggesting that closed-ended seeds can be a practical starting point for long-horizon coding data.

</details>


### [142] [When Answers Stray from Questions: Hallucination Detection via Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14449)
*Siyang Yao,Erhu Feng,Yubin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAOD是一种单次推理的幻觉检测框架，通过将答案表示投影到问题正交方向来消除领域相关的变异性，同时保留域无关的事实性信号。该方法结合Fisher重要性进行层和神经元选择，设计了联合探测和纯正交探测两种策略，分别优化域内检测和跨域泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型幻觉检测需要在准确性、效率和分布偏移鲁棒性之间取得平衡。现有的黑盒一致性方法需要重复推理开销大，单次白盒探针将答案表示孤立处理，在领域偏移下性能急剧下降。

Method: QAOD通过投影消除答案表示中的问题对齐方向获得问题正交分量；采用多样性惩罚的Fisher评分选择关键层；使用Fisher重要性筛选判别神经元；设计两种探测策略——联合探测（正交分量+问题上下文）和纯正交探测。

Result: 联合探测器在所有评估的模型-数据集对上取得最佳域内AUROC；正交分量探测器实现最强OOD迁移，在BioASQ上超越最佳白盒基线达21%，计算成本低于生成成本的25%。

Conclusion: QAOD通过正交分解平衡了域内检测和跨域泛化，joint probe在域内表现最佳，orthogonal-only probe在OOD迁移上最优，且计算成本仅为生成成本的25%以下。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) requires balancing accu racy, efficiency, and robustness to distribution shift. Black-box consistency methods are effective but demand repeated inference; single-pass white-box probes are effi cient yet treat answer representations in isolation, often degrading sharply under domain shift. We propose QAOD (Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition), a single-pass framework that projects away the question-aligned direction from the answer representation to obtain a question-orthogonal component that suppresses domain-conditioned variation. To identify informative signals, QAOD further selects layers via diversity-penalized Fisher scoring and discriminative neurons via Fisher importance. To address both in-domain detection and cross-domain generalization, we design two complementary probing strategies: pairing the or thogonal component with question context yields a joint probe that maximizes in-domain discriminability, while using the orthogonal component alone preserves domain-agnostic factuality signals for robust transfer. QAOD's joint probe achieves the best in-domain AUROC across all evaluated model-dataset pairs, while the orthogonal-only probe delivers the strongest OOD transfer, surpassing the best white-box baseline by up to 21% on BioASQ at under 25% of generation cost.

</details>


### [143] [LiSA: Lifelong Safety Adaptation via Conservative Policy Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14454)
*Minbeom Kim,Lesly Miculicich,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Mihir Parmar,Phillip Wallis,Bharath Chandrasekhar,Kyomin Jung,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiSA是一个保守策略归纳框架，通过结构化记忆使AI护栏能够在稀疏、噪声反馈场景下持续自适应提升，解决了AI智能体在复杂工作流环境中护栏失效的实践挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体从聊天界面扩展到读取私密数据、调用工具、执行多步骤工作流的系统时，护栏失效的后果从单纯的答案质量问题升级为实际的部署危害——可能泄露机密、授权不安全操作或阻断合法工作。最棘手的失败往往是情境依赖的：某动作是否可接受取决于本地隐私规范、组织策略和用户期望，而这些难以在部署前完全规范。这造成了实践缺口：护栏必须适应自身运行环境，但部署反馈通常局限于稀疏的、噪声化的用户报告失败，且反复微调往往不切实际。

Method: 提出LiSA（Lifelong Safety Adaptation）框架，采用保守策略归纳方法，通过三个核心机制改进固定基础护栏：1）将偶发失败转化为可复用的策略抽象，使稀疏报告能够泛化到个别案例之外；2）加入冲突感知的局部规则，防止混合标签场景下的过度泛化；3）通过后验下界实现证据感知的置信度门控，使记忆复用随累积证据而非单纯的经验准确率扩展。

Result: 在PrivacyLens+、ConFaide+和AgentHarm三个数据集上，LiSA在稀疏反馈条件下持续优于强基线方法；在20%标签翻转率的噪声用户反馈下仍保持鲁棒性；在延迟-性能前沿上超越骨干模型扩展方案。

Conclusion: LiSA提供了一条务实路径，使AI智能体能够应对现实世界中不可预测的长尾边缘风险。通过结构化记忆和保守策略归纳，该框架在稀疏反馈和噪声反馈条件下均表现出色，同时在延迟-性能权衡上优于单纯依靠骨干模型扩展的方案。

Abstract: As AI agents move from chat interfaces to systems that read private data, call tools, and execute multi-step workflows, guardrails become a last line of defense against concrete deployment harms. In these settings, guardrail failures are no longer merely answer-quality errors: they can leak secrets, authorize unsafe actions, or block legitimate work. The hardest failures are often contextual: whether an action is acceptable depends on local privacy norms, organizational policies, and user expectations that resist pre-deployment specification. This creates a practical gap: guardrails must adapt to their own operating environments, yet deployment feedback is typically limited to sparse, noisy user-reported failures, and repeated fine-tuning is often impractical. To address this gap, we propose LiSA (Lifelong Safety Adaptation), a conservative policy induction framework that improves a fixed base guardrail through structured memory. LiSA converts occasional failures into reusable policy abstractions so that sparse reports can generalize beyond individual cases, adds conflict-aware local rules to prevent overgeneralization in mixed-label contexts, and applies evidence-aware confidence gating via a posterior lower bound, so that memory reuse scales with accumulated evidence rather than empirical accuracy alone. Across PrivacyLens+, ConFaide+, and AgentHarm, LiSA consistently outperforms strong memory-based baselines under sparse feedback, remains robust under noisy user feedback even at 20% label-flip rates, and pushes the latency--performance frontier beyond backbone model scaling. Ultimately, LiSA offers a practical path to secure AI agents against the unpredictable long tail of real-world edge risks.

</details>


### [144] [Focused PU learning from imbalanced data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14467)
*Elias Zavitsanos,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对高度不平衡数据集的PU（正样本和无标签样本）学习新方法，通过聚焦经验风险估计器整合正样本和无标签样本训练二分类器，在SCAR和SAR两种标注机制下均表现优异，并成功应用于财务舞弊检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多问题（如疾病基因识别、目标营销、欺诈检测、推荐系统）面临标注数据有限且类别高度不平衡的挑战。现有PU学习方法难以有效处理不平衡场景和难以检测的弱正样本（与负样本高度相似）。

Method: 采用聚焦经验风险估计器（focused empirical risk estimator），同时利用正样本和无标签样本信息训练二分类器，方法适用于两种标注机制：完全随机选择正样本（SCAR）和随机选择（SAR）。

Result: 在不平衡数据集上取得最先进的性能，优于现有PU学习方法。此外，该方法成功应用于财务舞弊检测这一实际场景，验证了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理不平衡数据集中的PU学习问题，具有较强的通用性和实用价值，为欺诈检测等实际应用提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We propose a new method of learning from positive and unlabeled (PU) examples in highly imbalanced datasets. Many real-world problems, such as disease gene identification, targeted marketing, fraud detection, and recommender systems, are hard to address with machine learning methods, due to limited labeled data. Often, training data comprises positive and unlabeled instances, the latter typically being dominated by negative, but including also several positive instances. While PU learning is well-studied, few methods address imbalanced settings or hard-to-detect positive examples that resemble negative ones. Our approach uses a focused empirical risk estimator, incorporating both positive and unlabeled examples to train binary classifiers. Empirical evaluations demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on imbalanced datasets under two labeling mechanisms - selecting positives completely at random (SCAR) and selecting at random (SAR). Beyond these controlled experiments, we demonstrate the value of the proposed method in the real-world application of financial misstatement detection.

</details>


### [145] [Enjoy Your Layer Normalization with the Computational Efficiency of RMSNorm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14521)
*Yuxin Guo,Yihao Yue,Yunhao Ni,Yizhou Ruan,Jie Luo,Wenjun Wu,Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种框架，通过将Layer Normalization的中心化操作折叠到上游线性层（CCC/CBWC技术），判断任意DNN中的LN是否可被RMSNorm替代而保持功能不变。开发了基于图的检测算法，实验表明可实现2%-12%推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Layer Normalization（LN）因逐样本的中心化和缩放操作引入不可忽视的推理开销；RMSNorm通过移除中心化操作提升效率，但可能丢失中心化带来的好处。

Method: 通过列中心约束（CCC）和基于列的权重中心化（CBWC）将LN的中心化操作折叠到上游通用线性层中；开发了基于图的检测算法来识别任意DNN中的可折叠LN层。

Result: 在多种任务族上的实验表明，该方法可实现2%-12%的端到端加速而不改变模型预测；在实际训练环境中精确等价性被部分破坏时，仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: ManyLN层在常用架构中是可折叠的，可实现2%-12%的推理加速而不改变模型预测；在实际训练场景精确等价性被部分打破时，该方法仍保持竞争力。

Abstract: Layer normalization (LN) is a fundamental component in modern deep learning, but its per-sample centering and scaling introduce non-negligible inference overhead. RMSNorm improves efficiency by removing the centering operation, yet this may discard benefits associated with centering. This paper propose a framework to determine whether an LN in an arbitrary DNN can be replaced by RMSNorm without changing the model function. The key idea is to fold LN's centering operation into upstream general linear layers by enforcing zero-mean outputs through the column-centered constraint (CCC) and column-based weight centering (CBWC). We extend the analysis to arbitrary DNNs, define such LNs as foldable LNs, and develop a graph-based detection algorithm. Our analysis shows that many LNs in widely used architectures are foldable, enabling exact inference-time conversion and end-to-end acceleration of 2% to 12% without changing model predictions. Experiments across multiple task families further show that, when exact equivalence is partially broken in practical training settings, our method remains competitive with vanilla LN while improving efficiency.

</details>


### [146] [Lang2MLIP: End-to-End Language-to-Machine Learning Interatomic Potential Development with Autonomous Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14527)
*Wenwen Li,Yuki Orimo,Nontawat Charoenphakdee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lang2MLIP automates machine learning interatomic potential (MLIP) development for non-experts by using LLM-based multi-agent sequential decision-making that dynamically selects actions based on current system state, eliminating the need for predefined pipelines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLIP development requires expertise in atomistic simulations, machine learning, and workflow design, plus iterative active learning; existing automated pipelines assume fixed sequences or require domain experts, limiting adaptability for heterogeneous materials systems.

Method: Multi-agent framework using LLMs as decision-making agents that observe dataset, model, evaluation results, and logs to autonomously select improvement actions at each step; removes predefined pipeline constraints and enables self-correction through revisiting earlier subsystems.

Result: Demonstrated successfully on solid electrolyte interphase (SEI) system with multiple components and interfaces, showing the approach can handle complex multi-component materials.

Conclusion: Lang2MLIP enables non-experts to develop MLIPs through LLM-based multi-agent sequential decision-making, with successful demonstration on multi-component SEI systems.

Abstract: Developing machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for complex materials systems remains challenging because it requires expertise in atomistic simulations, machine learning, and workflow design, as well as iterative active learning procedures. Existing automated pipelines typically assume a fixed sequence of stages or depend on domain experts, which limits their adaptability to heterogeneous materials systems where the optimal curriculum is not known in advance. To lower the barrier to developing MLIPs for non-experts, we propose Lang2MLIP, a multi-agent framework that takes natural-language input and formulates end-to-end MLIP development as a sequential decision-making problem solved by large language models (LLMs). At each step, a decision-making agent observes the current dataset, model, evaluation results, and execution log, and then automatically selects an appropriate action to improve the model. This removes the need for a predefined pipeline and enables the agent to self-correct by revisiting earlier subsystems when new failures arise. We evaluate this approach on a solid electrolyte interphase (SEI) system with multiple components and interfaces. These results suggest that LLM-based multi-agent systems are a promising direction for automating MLIP development and making it more accessible to non-experts.

</details>


### [147] [Exploring Geographic Relative Space in Large Language Models through Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14535)
*Stef De Sabbata,Rahul Baiju,Stefano Mizzaro,Kevin Roitero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This abstract explores the safety implications of deploying Large Language Models in geographic applications by examining how these models process relative geographic space through activation patching—a mechanistic interpretability approach—driven by concerns about limited understanding of LLM internal workings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The rapid integration of LLMs in geography raises safety concerns due to the limited understanding of how these models function internally. There's a critical need to understand the underlying mechanisms before widespread deployment in spatial analyses and geographic processes.

Method: Utilizing activation patching, a mechanistic interpretability technique, to investigate how LLMs process relative geographic space.

Result: Preliminary findings suggest that activation patching can effectively probe and reveal how LLMs handle relative geographic concepts, offering a pathway to better understand spatial reasoning in these models.

Conclusion: The work aims to provide insights into LLM spatial reasoning mechanisms to inform safer deployment of LLMs in geographic applications.

Abstract: The increased use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in geography raises substantial questions about the safety of integrating these tools across a wide range of processes and analyses, given our very limited understanding of their inner workings. In this extended abstract, we examine how LLMs process relative geographic space using activation patching, an emerging tool for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [148] [RxEval: A Prescription-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14543)
*Shuhao Chen,Weisen Jiang,Changmiao Wang,Xiaoqing Wu,Xuanren Shi,Yu Zhang,James T. Kwok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RxEval是一个用于评估大语言模型处方能力的处方级基准测试，通过多选题形式考察模型选择药物-剂量-给药途径三元组的能力。结果显示当前模型表现仍有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试将任务 formulation为粗粒度的入院级药物代码预测，采用多热诊断和操作代码输入，无法捕捉真实处方的时间点级、信息丰富特征。

Method: 提出RxEval，一个处方级基准测试，通过多选题形式评估LLM开药能力。题目包含详细患者档案和时间序临床轨迹，需从真实处方中选择特定药物-剂量-给药途径三元组，并通过推理链扰动生成患者特定干扰项。数据集包含1547道题，覆盖584例患者、18个诊断类别和969种独特药物。

Result: 评估16种LLM后，F1分数范围为45.18至77.10，最佳精确匹配率仅为46.10%。错误分析表明前沿模型可能忽略明确的患者信息且无法推导临床结论。

Conclusion: RxEval具有挑战性和区分性，目前前沿模型仍难以准确整合患者信息并推导临床结论。

Abstract: Inpatient medication recommendation requires clinicians to repeatedly select specific medications, doses, and routes as a patient's condition evolves. Existing benchmarks formulate this task as admission-level prediction over coarse drug codes with multi-hot diagnostic and procedure code inputs, failing to capture the per-timepoint, information-rich nature of real prescribing. We propose RxEval, a prescription-level benchmark that evaluates LLM prescribing capability by multiple-choice questions: each question presents a detailed patient profile and time-ordered clinical trajectory, requiring selection of specific medication-dose-route triples from real prescriptions and patient-specific distractors generated via reasoning-chain perturbation. RxEval comprises 1,547 questions spanning 584 patients, 18 diagnostic categories, and 969 unique medications. Evaluation of 16 LLMs shows that RxEval is both challenging and discriminative: F1 ranges from 45.18 to 77.10 across models, and the best Exact Match is only 46.10%. Error analysis reveals that even frontier models may overlook stated patient information and fail to derive clinical conclusions.

</details>


### [149] [Resolving Action Bottleneck: Agentic Reinforcement Learning Informed by Token-Level Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14558)
*Langzhou He,Junyou Zhu,Yue Zhou,Zhengyao Gu,Junhua Liu,Wei-Chieh Huang,Henry Peng Zou,David Wipf,Philip S. Yu,Qitian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现Agentic RL训练中存在"Action Bottleneck"现象：训练信号高度集中于action token而非推理token。基于此观察，提出简单高效的ActFocus token重加权方法，无需额外计算成本即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPO和GRPO等策略梯度方法对轨迹中每个token采用均匀的信用分配，但这种均匀分配可能导致训练信号错配，特别是在推理token和action token数量差异悬殊的情况下。

Method: 提出了ActFocus方法，包含两个核心机制：(1) 对推理token进行梯度降权；(2) 基于能量的重新分配机制，对不确定性较高的action token增加权重。

Result: 在四种环境和不同模型规模下，ActFocus持续优于PPO和GRPO。相对于PPO和GRPO，最终性能提升分别高达65.2和63.7个百分点。

Conclusion: ActFocus通过简单但有效的token重加权策略，成功解决了Agentic RL中的Action Bottleneck问题。该方法不仅性能显著提升，而且无需额外计算成本，为理解和改进大型语言模型的强化学习训练提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning trains large language models using multi-turn trajectories that interleave long reasoning traces with short environment-facing actions. Common policy-gradient methods, such as PPO and GRPO, treat each token in a trajectory equally, leading to uniform credit assignment. In this paper, we critically demonstrate that such uniform credit assignment largely misallocates token-level training signals. From an energy-based modeling perspective, we show that token-level training signals, quantified by their correlations with reward variance of different rollouts sampled from a given prompt, concentrate sharply on action tokens rather than reasoning tokens, even though action tokens account for only a small fraction of the trajectory. We refer to this phenomenon as the Action Bottleneck. Motivated by this observation, we propose an embarrassingly simple token reweighting approach, ActFocus, that downweights gradients on reasoning tokens, along with an additional energy-based redistribution mechanism that further increases the weights on action tokens with higher uncertainty. Across four environments and different model sizes, ActFocus consistently outperforms PPO and GRPO, yielding final-step gains of up to 65.2 and 63.7 percentage points, respectively, without any additional runtime or memory cost.

</details>


### [150] [Multi-Dimensional Model Integrity and Responsibility Assessment Index and Scoring Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14550)
*Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Thanh Hung Do,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MIRAI是一个统一的模型评估框架，用于在高风险表格数据场景下评估可解释性、公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和可持续性五个维度。实验证明，更好的预测性能不一定带来更好的综合 Integrity，简单模型有时优于复杂深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前实践仍主要孤立地评估可解释性、公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和可持续性，但在高风险表格领域，仅靠预测性能评估人工智能是不够的。

Method: MIRAI通过标准化和方向对齐的维度评分结合已有指标，在受控比较设置下跨五个维度评估表格模型，并将其聚合成单一评分，实现不同架构和计算配置模型间的直接比较。

Result: 医疗、金融和社会经济数据集上的实验表明，更高的预测性能不一定意味着更好的整体诚信和责任性；在若干案例中，较简单的模型比复杂的深度表格架构实现了更强的跨维度平衡。

Conclusion: MIRAI为受监管环境中的负责任模型选择提供了简洁实用的基础。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in high-stakes tabular domains cannot be evaluated by predictive performance alone, yet current practice still assesses explainability, fairness, robustness, privacy, and sustainability mostly in isolation. We propose the Model Integrity and Responsibility Assessment Index (MIRAI), a unified evaluation framework that measures tabular models across these five dimensions under a controlled comparison setting and aggregates them into a single score. MIRAI combines established metrics through normalized and direction-aligned dimension scores, which enables direct comparison across models with different architectural and computational profiles. Experiments on healthcare, financial, and socioeconomic datasets show that higher predictive performance does not necessarily imply better overall integrity and responsibility. In several cases, simpler models achieve a stronger cross-dimensional balance than more complex deep tabular architectures. MIRAI provides a compact and practical basis for responsible model selection in regulated settings.

</details>


### [151] [SeesawNet: Towards Non-stationary Time Series Forecasting with Balanced Modeling of Common and Specific Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14551)
*Hao Li,Lu Zhang,Liu Chong,Yankai Chen,Pengyang Wang,Yingjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeesawNet通过自适应平稳-非平稳注意力机制(ASNA)解决时间序列预测中实例归一化导致信息过度平滑的问题，实现共同依赖与实例特定依赖的动态平衡，在多变量时间序列预测任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实例归一化(IN)虽能减少分布偏移并突出样本间的共同模式，但会过度平滑实例特有的结构信息，而这些信息对建模时序和跨通道异质性至关重要。现有方法虽试图抑制分布差异或恢复时序依赖，但均未解决如何根据每个实例的非平稳性结构自适应平衡共同依赖与实例特定依赖这一核心问题。

Method: 提出SeesawNet架构，核心为自适应平稳-非平稳注意力机制(ASNA)，该机制从归一化序列中捕获共同依赖，从原始序列中提取特定依赖，并根据实例级非平稳性进行自适应融合。在此基础上，SeesawNet交替进行时序和通道关系建模，联合捕获长程依赖和跨变量依赖。

Result: ASNA机制可同时从归一化序列和原始序列中提取互补信息，实现共同依赖与实例特定依赖的自适应融合，有效提升了模型对时序异质性和跨通道异质性的建模能力。

Conclusion: SeesawNet通过ASNA机制成功实现了共同依赖与实例特定依赖的动态平衡，在多个真实世界基准数据集上显著优于现有方法，为非平稳多变量时间序列预测提供了一种有效的统一架构。

Abstract: Instance normalization (IN) is widely used in non-stationary multivariate time series forecasting to reduce distribution shifts and highlight common patterns across samples. However, IN can over-smooth instance-specific structural information that is essential for modeling temporal and cross-channel heterogeneity. While prior methods further suppress distribution discrepancies or attempt to recover temporal specific dependencies, they often ignore a central tension: how to adaptively model common and instance-specific dependency based on each instance's non-stationary structures. To address this dilemma, we propose SeesawNet, a unified architecture that dynamically balances common and instance-specific dependency modeling in both temporal and channel dimensions. At its core is Adaptive Stationary-Nonstationary Attention (ASNA), which captures common dependencies from normalized sequences and specific dependencies from raw sequences, and adaptively fuses them according to instance-level non-stationarity. Built upon ASNA, SeesawNet alternates dedicated temporal and channel relationship modeling to jointly capture long-range and cross-variable dependencies. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SeesawNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [152] [Efficient Multi-objective Prompt Optimization via Pure-exploration Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14553)
*Donghao Li,Chengshuai Shi,Weijuan Ou,Cong Shen,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大型语言模型提示工程中的多目标提示选择问题，将问题形式化为纯探索多臂老虎机，提出的算法在理论上具有识别误差保证，在实验中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提示选择研究忽视了提示性能评估的多面性特征——单一指标无法全面刻画提示的有效性，因此需要研究多目标提示选择问题。

Method: 将多目标提示选择问题映射到纯探索老虎机框架，借鉴多目标老虎机中的已有高效算法，并针对结构化老虎机场景提出了一种新的最优可行臂识别设计方法，且在线性情形下提供了识别误差的理论保证。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于老虎机的方法在多个大型语言模型上显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本研究为多目标提示优化提供了一个原则性且高效的理论框架，通过将问题建模为纯探索多臂老虎机问题，并引入结构化老虎机中的最优可行臂识别新设计，为大规模语言模型的提示工程提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: Prompt engineering has become central to eliciting the capabilities of large language models (LLMs). At its core lies prompt selection -- efficiently identifying the most effective prompts. However, most prior investigations overlook a key challenge: the inherently multi-faceted nature of prompt performance, which cannot be captured by a single metric. To fill this gap, we study the multi-objective prompt selection problem under two practical settings: Pareto prompt set recovery and best feasible prompt identification. Casting the problem into the pure-exploration bandits framework, we adapt provably efficient algorithms from multi-objective bandits and further introduce a novel design for best feasible arm identification in structured bandits, with theoretical guarantees on the identification error in the linear case. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs show that the bandit-based approaches yield significant improvements over baselines, establishing a principled and efficient framework for multi-objective prompt optimization.

</details>


### [153] [Woodelf++: A Fast and Unified Partial Dependence Plot Algorithm for Decision Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14578)
*Ron Wettenstein,Alexander Nadel,Udi Boker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Woodelf++是一个统一且高效的决策树集成模型可解释性工具计算框架，整合了PDP、Joint-PDP、Any-Order-PDIVs等方法，并引入Full PDPs。相比现有技术达到数量级加速，尤其在Any-Order-PDIVs上实现指数级提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性工具（PDP、Joint-PDP、PDIVs）缺乏统一、高效的计算框架，尤其是Any-Order-PDIVs在大规模数据集上的计算成本极高，现有方法难以胜任。

Method: 通过在伪布尔函数上导出合适的度量，将PDP、Joint-PDP和Any-Order-PDIVs统一在单一框架内计算。该方法基于Woodelf算法（原本用于高效SHAP计算）构建，并引入了Full PDPs来利用模型的分裂阈值捕捉所有特征值下的模型行为。

Result: Woodelf++实现了相较于现有技术的显著复杂度改进，Any-Order-PDIVs获得指数级加速。Full PDPs能够忠实捕捉模型在所有特征值下的行为。

Conclusion: Woodelf++在纯Python中实现，支持GPU加速。在400,000行数据集上，比最先进的方案快6倍，比scikit-learn快五个数量级。对于Any-Order-PDIVs，计算所有交互值仅需5分钟，而最先进方案估计需要超过100万年。

Abstract: Partial Dependence Plots (PDPs) visualize how changes in a single feature affect the average model prediction. They are widely used in practice to interpret decision tree ensembles and other machine learning models. Joint-PDPs extend this idea to pairs of features, revealing their combined effect. Partial Dependence Interaction Values (PDIVs) measure feature interactions. The Any-Order-PDIVs task computes these interactions for every feature subset across all rows of the dataset.
  We introduce Woodelf++, a unified and efficient approach for computing all these useful explainability tools on decision tree ensembles, building on Woodelf, an algorithm for efficient SHAP computation. By deriving suitable metrics over pseudo-Boolean functions, Woodelf++ can compute PDPs (exact and approximate), Joint-PDPs, and Any-Order-PDIVs in a unified framework. Our method delivers substantial complexity improvements over the state of the art, including an exponential gain for Any-Order-PDIVs. Additionally, we introduce and efficiently compute Full PDPs, which leverage the model's split thresholds to faithfully capture its behavior across all possible feature values.
  Woodelf++ is implemented in pure Python and supports GPU acceleration. On a dataset with 400,000 rows, Woodelf++ computes PDP and Joint-PDP up to 6x faster than the state of the art and up to five orders of magnitude faster than scikit-learn. For Any-Order-PDIVs, the gap is even larger: Woodelf++ computes all interaction values in 5 minutes, while the state of the art is estimated to require over 1,000,000 years.

</details>


### [154] [Angel or Demon: Investigating the Plasticity Interventions' Impact on Backdoor Threats in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14587)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Yang Dai,Jiahao Chen,Chunyi Zhou,Linkang Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文首次系统研究可塑性干预对深度强化学习后门安全的影响，通过大规模实验发现SAM干预会通过梯度放大机制加剧后门威胁，而其他干预通过破坏激活通路和压缩表征空间缓解威胁，并提出SCC框架与损失景观尖锐度指标用于后门检测与理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击研究局限于标准场景，忽略现代DRL智能体中广泛使用的可塑性干预机制；而这些干预对DRL后门漏洞的影响尚不明确，威胁实际部署安全

Method: 构建14,664个案例的实验平台，整合多种代表性干预策略与攻击场景进行系统实证研究；采用病理学分析方法深入探究不同干预效果的作用机制

Result: 发现SAM干预会加剧后门威胁，其余干预则缓解后门威胁；病理分析揭示：SAM的恶化效应源于后门梯度放大效应，而缓解效应则来自激活通路破坏与表征空间压缩；异常损失景观尖锐度可有效指示DRL后门存在

Conclusion: 提出SCC概念框架阐明干预与后门机制互作，并发现异常损失景观尖锐度可作为DRL后门检测指标，为安全部署DRL提供理论与实践指导

Abstract: Extensive research has highlighted the severe threats posed by backdoor attacks to deep reinforcement learning (DRL). However, prior studies primarily focus on vanilla scenarios, while plasticity interventions have emerged as indispensable built-in components of modern DRL agents. Despite their effectiveness in mitigating plasticity loss, the impact of these interventions on DRL backdoor vulnerabilities remains underexplored, and this lack of systematic investigation poses risks in practical DRL deployments. To bridge this gap, we empirically study 14,664 cases integrating representative interventions and attack scenarios. We find that only one intervention (i.e., SAM) exacerbates backdoor threats, while other interventions mitigate them. Pathological analysis identifies that the exacerbation is attributed to backdoor gradient amplification, while the mitigation stems from activation pathway disruption and representation space compression. From these findings, we derive two novel insights: (1) a conceptual framework SCC for robust backdoor injection that deconstructs the mechanistic interplay between interventions and backdoors in DRL, and (2) abnormal loss landscape sharpness as a key indicator for DRL backdoor detection.

</details>


### [155] [Boosting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards via Randomly Selected Few-Shot Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15012)
*Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FEST是一种少样本演示引导的RLVR算法，通过将监督信号、on-policy信号与衰减权重策略相结合，仅需128个随机演示即可在多个基准上匹配甚至超越使用完整SFT数据集的基线，显著提升了RLVR的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在数学和编程等需要链式思维推理的任务中已取得成功，但在难题上样本效率低下——正确 rollout 的生成本身就很困难。现有方法尝试通过演示引导的RLVR（SFT+RL）来解决，但SFT阶段通常需要大量数据，成本高昂。

Method: FEST的核心设计包含三个关键组件：监督信号（来自少量演示的SFT）、on-policy强化学习信号，以及对演示数据集采用衰减权重策略以防止多轮训练中的过拟合。算法流程为在RLVR训练失败的场景下，结合SFT进行引导式学习。

Result: FEST在多个基准测试上显著优于基线方法，仅用128个随机演示即可达到与完整数据集相当甚至更好的性能。

Conclusion: FEST通过128个随机采样的演示样本即可匹配甚至超越使用完整数据集的基线方法，显著降低了SFT数据需求，同时保持了RLVR的链式推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has achieved great success in developing Large Language Models (LLMs) with chain-of-thought rollouts for many tasks such as math and coding. Nevertheless, RLVR struggles with sample efficiency on difficult problems where correct rollouts are hard to generate. Prior works propose to address this issue via demonstration-guided RLVR, i.e., to conduct Supervised FineTuning (SFT) when RL fails; however, SFT often requires a lot of data, which can be expensive to acquire. In this paper, we propose FEST, a FEw-ShoT demonstration-guided RLVR algorithm. It attains compelling results with only 128 demonstrations randomly selected from an SFT dataset. We find that three components are vital for the success: supervised signal, on-policy signal, and decaying weights on the few-shot SFT dataset to prevent overfitting from multiple-epoch training. On several benchmarks, FEST outperforms baselines with magnitudes less SFT data, even matching their performance with full dataset.

</details>


### [156] [Silent Collapse in Recursive Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14588)
*Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了递归学习系统中"静默崩溃"现象——模型内部分布在标准指标稳定时悄然退化，发现崩溃前存在三个可检测的轨迹级前兆（锚点熵收缩、表征漂移冻结、尾部覆盖侵蚀），并据此提出MTR元认知框架实现无需干净数据的早期预警与主动干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归学习（模型在自身生成的先前版本数据上训练）在LLM、自主智能体和自监督系统中日益普及，但标准性能指标（损失、困惑度、准确率）往往无法在不可逆退化发生前检测到内部衰减，导致"静默崩溃"现象——模型内部分布（预测熵、表示多样性、尾部覆盖）即使在常规指标稳定或改善时也会持续收缩。

Method: 通过分析递归学习条件下模型内部分布的演化轨迹，识别出三个预警信号：锚点熵收缩、表征漂移冻结和尾部覆盖侵蚀。基于这些前兆信号，设计了MTR（Monitor-Trust-Regulator）元认知框架，包含轨迹统计监控、慢时标信任变量估计和自适应学习强度调节三个核心组件。

Result: 证实静默崩溃并非骤然发生，在标准验证指标退化前多个迭代周期即可检测到三类可靠的前兆信号。该框架无需访问原始干净数据即可提供早期预警并主动阻止静默崩溃。

Conclusion: MTR框架在不依赖原始干净数据的情况下，为递归学习系统提供了早期预警和主动预防机制，解决了当原始数据不可用、受污染或涉及隐私时的关键挑战。

Abstract: Recursive learning -- where models are trained on data generated by previous versions of themselves -- is increasingly common in large language models, autonomous agents, and self-supervised systems. However, standard performance metrics (loss, perplexity, accuracy) often fail to detect internal degradation before it becomes irreversible. Here we identify a phenomenon we call silent collapse: under broad recursive conditions, model internal distributions -- predictive entropy, representational diversity, and tail coverage -- progressively contract even as conventional metrics appear stable or improving.
  We discover that silent collapse is not abrupt. Its onset is reliably preceded by three trajectory-level precursors: (1) contraction of anchor entropy, (2) freezing of representation drift, and (3) erosion of tail coverage. These signals manifest multiple generations before any degradation in standard validation metrics, enabling early warning.
  Based on these precursors, we propose the MTR (Monitor--Trust--Regulator) framework, a lightweight metacognitive loop that monitors trajectory statistics, estimates a slow-timescale trust variable, and adaptively modulates the effective learning intensity. MTR provides early warning and actively prevents silent collapse without requiring access to pristine real data -- a critical advantage when original data is unavailable, contaminated, or private.

</details>


### [157] [Fast Rates for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14599)
*Andreas Schlaginhaufen,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了熵正则化 Min-Max 逆强化学习（Min-Max-IRL）在一般 Borel 空间中的结构等价性和快速统计收敛率，核心贡献包括 MLE 等价性、O(1/n) 收敛保证及软最优值函数的导数性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆强化学习（IRL）旨在从专家演示中恢复奖励函数，但 min-max 变体的理论基础此前缺乏系统的结构和统计分析。该研究旨在建立 Min-Max-IRL 在一般 Borel 空间中的理论基础。

Method: 利用 Min-Max-IRL 损失的伪自协调（pseudo-self-concordance）性质，通过统计学习理论方法建立误差界。

Result: 证明了 MLE 与 Min-Max-IRL 在总体水平等价，在确定性动力学下经验水平也等价；轨迹级 KL 散度和 Hessian 范数下参数误差均以 O(1/n) 速率收敛；将奖励可识别性结果推广到一般 Borel 空间，并导出软最优值函数对奖励参数的导数公式。

Conclusion: 理论分析涵盖线性奖励类、有限时域、 Borel 状态和动作空间的 MDP；结果在经验水平上需要确定性动力学假设，在理论上对错误设定和探索要求具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We establish novel structural and statistical results for entropy-regularized min-max inverse reinforcement learning (Min-Max-IRL) with linear reward classes in finite-horizon MDPs with Borel state and action spaces. On the structural side, we show that maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and Min-Max-IRL are equivalent at the population level, and at the empirical level under deterministic dynamics. On the statistical side, exploiting pseudo-self-concordance of the Min-Max-IRL loss, we prove that both the trajectory-level KL divergence and the squared parameter error in the Hessian norm decay at the fast rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of expert trajectories. Our guarantees apply under misspecification and require no exploration assumptions. We further extend reward-identifiability results to general Borel spaces and derive novel results on the derivatives of the soft-optimal value function with respect to reward parameters.

</details>


### [158] [Proposal and study of statistical features for string similarity computation and classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15110)
*E. O. Rodrigues,D. Casanova,M. Teixeira,V. Pegorini,F. Favarim,E. Clua,A. Conci,Panos Liatsis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将视觉计算中的共现矩阵和游程矩阵特征迁移应用于字符串相似性计算，提出了一种与语言无关的纯统计方法，在合成实验和真实抄袭检测中均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有字符串相似性度量方法（如编辑距离等）多依赖语言相关特征，限制了其在跨语言或无语法结构场景中的应用。

Method: 将视觉计算领域的共现矩阵(COM)和游程矩阵(RLM)特征适配用于字符串相似性计算，并与最长公共子序列、最大连续公共子序列、互信息和编辑距离等常用统计度量进行比较。

Result: 在合成数据集实验中，COM和RLM特征显著优于其他统计特征（4个案例中3个P值<0.001）；在真实文本抄袭数据集上，RLM特征表现最佳。

Conclusion: 提出的COM和RLM特征可用于任意语言或语法结构中的字符串相似性计算，且在合成数据集和真实文本抄袭检测中均优于传统统计方法。

Abstract: Adaptations of features commonly applied in the field of visual computing, co-occurrence matrix (COM) and run-length matrix (RLM), are proposed for the similarity computation of strings in general (words, phrases, codes and texts). The proposed features are not sensitive to language related information. These are purely statistical and can be used in any context with any language or grammatical structure. Other statistical measures that are commonly employed in the field such as longest common subsequence, maximal consecutive longest common subsequence, mutual information and edit distances are evaluated and compared. In the first synthetic set of experiments, the COM and RLM features outperform the remaining state-of-the-art statistical features. In 3 out of 4 cases, the RLM and COM features were statistically more significant than the second best group based on distances (P-value < 0.001). When it comes to a real text plagiarism dataset, the RLM features obtained the best results.

</details>


### [159] [DRL-STAF: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Framework for State-Aware Forecasting of Complex Multivariate Hidden Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14632)
*Manrui Jiang,Jingru Huang,Yong Chen,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Forecasting multivariate hidden Markov processes is challenging due to nonlinear and nonstationary observations, latent state transitions, and cross-sequence dependencies. While deep learning methods achieve strong predictive accuracy, they typically lack explicit state modeling, whereas Hidden Markov Models (HMMs) provide interpretable latent states but struggle with complex nonlinear emissions and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose DRL-STAF, a Deep Reinforcement Learning based STate-Aware Forecasting framework that jointly predicts next-step observations and estimates the corresponding hidden states for complex multivariate hidden Markov processes. Specifically, DRL-STAF models complex nonlinear emissions using deep neural networks and estimates discrete hidden states using reinforcement learning, reducing the reliance on predefined transition structures and enabling flexible adaptation to diverse temporal dynamics. In particular, DRL-STAF mitigates the state-space explosion encountered by typical multivariate HMM-based methods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DRL-STAF outperforms HMM variants, standalone deep learning models, and existing DL-HMM hybrids in most cases, while also providing reliable hidden-state estimates.

</details>


### [160] [Forgetting That Sticks: Quantization-Permanent Unlearning via Circuit Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15138)
*Saisab Sadhu,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 揭示量化后训练可逆转机器遗忘的系统性双重失效，提出MANSU方法，通过因果电路归因、零空间投影和幅度下界三重机制，首次实现兼顾遗忘有效性、保留保真度、量化稳定性和结构化擦除四项属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署模型的量化特性与训练后评估之间的不一致导致机器遗忘失效：基于梯度的方法若能实现有意义遗忘则在量化下失效；能抗量化的方法几乎不改变模型。根因在于参数更新幅度比NF4量化阈值低47-828倍，扩散至数十亿参数无法跨越量化边界——此为"稀疏性-永久性权衡"。

Method: MANSU结合三种机制：(1)因果归因定位最小遗忘子图；(2)电路约束零空间投影（带对角Fisher保留约束）；(3)参数级幅度下界确保量化抗性。

Result: 提出的CAD指标可区分结构化遗忘与行为抑制。MANSU在多模型族和危害基准上首次同时满足四项属性且各有余裕，而基线方法在压缩下精度可恢复高达+0.05。

Conclusion: MANSU是首个在多个模型族和危险基准上同时满足全部四项属性的方法，且每项均有余量；基线方法在压缩下精度可恢复高达+0.05。

Abstract: Standard unlearning evaluations measure behavioral suppression in full precision, immediately after training, despite every deployed language model being quantized first. Recent work has shown that 4-bit post-training quantization can reverse machine unlearning; we show this is not a tuning artefact but a systematic dual failure: gradient-based methods that achieve meaningful forgetting lose it under compression, while methods that survive quantization barely change the model. Both failures trace to the same root cause: across all baselines, per-parameter updates lie 47-828x below the NF4 quantization bin width; updates diffused across billions of parameters cannot clear quantization bin boundaries, a consequence we formalize as a sparsity-permanence tradeoff. We present MANSU (Mechanistic-Aligned Null-Space Unlearning), which resolves both modes by combining causal circuit attribution to isolate the minimal forget-set subgraph, circuit-restricted null-space projection with a diagonal-Fisher retain bound, and a per-parameter magnitude floor guaranteeing quantization survival by construction. We additionally introduce Circuit Attribution Divergence (CAD), a mechanistic verification metric distinguishing structural erasure from behavioral suppression, a distinction existing metrics cannot make. Across multiple model families and hazard benchmarks, MANSU is the first method to jointly satisfy all four properties with margin on each (meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure), while gradient-based baselines recover up to +0.05 accuracy under compression.

</details>


### [161] [Unbiased and Second-Order-Free Training for High-Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14643)
*Jaemin Seo,Surin Lee,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A training framework that eliminates Euler-Maruyama discretization bias in BSDE-based PDE solvers without requiring expensive second-order derivative computations, preserving the method's computational advantages.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BSDE methods are competitive alternatives to PINNs for high-dimensional PDEs, avoiding the curse of dimensionality with second-order-free training. However, the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses.

Method: Propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that eliminates EM-induced bias without requiring expensive second-order spatial derivative evaluations.

Result: Framework achieves unbiased training while maintaining computational efficiency comparable to standard EM-based BSDE methods.

Conclusion: Preserving the computational advantages of BSDE methods while eliminating discretization bias, providing both theoretical analysis and a practical solution.

Abstract: Deep learning methods based on backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) have emerged as competitive alternatives to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs). By leveraging probabilistic representations, BSDE approaches can avoid the curse of dimensionality and often admit second-order-free training objectives that do not require explicit Hessian evaluations. It has recently been established that the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses. While high-order schemes such as Heun can fully eliminate this bias, such schemes re-introduce second-order spatial derivatives and incur substantial computational overhead. In this work, we provide a principled analysis of EM-induced loss bias and propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that preserves the computational advantages of BSDE methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/seojaemin22/Un-EM-BSDE.

</details>


### [162] [Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15155)
*Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Jinyang Wu,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SDAR方法，通过门控机制将OPSD辅助目标与RL主优化结合，解决多轮agent训练中的不稳定性问题，在多个benchmark上实现9-10%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练LLM agent时仅提供粗糙的轨迹级奖励信号，在长时域交互中监督粒度不足；OPSD虽然提供密集的token级指导，但迁移至多轮agent时面临复合多轮不稳定性问题，以及负向教师拒绝的不对称处理问题。

Method: SDAR将OPSD作为门控辅助目标，保持RL为主优化骨干，使用sigmoid门控机制映射解耦的token级信号，对教师认可的正向gap tokens强化蒸馏，对负向教师拒绝进行软衰减。

Result: 相比GRPO基线显著提升：ALFWorld +9.4%，Search-QA +7.0%，WebShop-Acc +10.2%；避免了naive GRPO+OPSD的不稳定性；在Qwen2.5和Qwen3系列不同规模模型上均保持一致超越。

Conclusion: SDAR provides a stable and effective approach for multi-turn LLM agent training by gating OPSD supervision, achieving consistent improvements across model families and benchmarks.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a central paradigm for post-training LLM agents, yet its trajectory-level reward signal provides only coarse supervision for long-horizon interaction. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) complements RL by introducing dense token-level guidance from a teacher branch augmented with privileged context. However, transferring OPSD to multi-turn agents proves problematic: compounding multi-turn instability destabilizes supervision, while skill-conditioned privileged guidance requires asymmetric treatment for negative teacher rejections may arise from imperfect skills retrieval or utilization. We introduce SDAR (Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning), which treats OPSD as a gated auxiliary objective while keeping RL as the primary optimization backbone. SDAR maps detached token-level signals into a sigmoid gate, strengthening distillation on teacher-endorsed positive-gap tokens and softly attenuating negative teacher rejections. Across the Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 families on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA, SDAR substantially improves over GRPO (+9.4% on ALFWorld, +7.0% on Search-QA, +10.2% on WebShop-Acc), avoids the instability of naive GRPO+OPSD, and consistently outperforms hybrid RL--OPSD baselines across model scales.

</details>


### [163] [Slower Generalization, Faster Memorization: A Sweet Spot in Algorithmic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14659)
*Shin So,Kyelim Lee,Albert No*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文通过Needleman-Wunsch矩阵生成任务发现，数据规模与泛化收敛之间存在反直觉的非单调关系：小规模Transformer在中间数据集规模下收敛最快，而非数据越多越好。研究区分了"泛化起始的临界数据量"与"优化收敛的数据库"两个概念，揭示了结构化输出任务中规则学习与精确拟合可能分离的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论认为存在"临界数据规模"——一旦数据足以识别潜在规则，更大数据应加速验证收敛。本研究挑战这一直觉，探究是否所有结构化输出任务都符合该规律。

Method: 采用Needleman-Wunsch矩阵生成任务（结构化输出任务）和乘法基线任务，通过控制数据集大小，观察小规模Transformer的验证精确度随梯度更新次数的变化。使用受控实验方法对比不同数据集规模下的收敛特性。

Result: 发现"数据规模甜蜜点"现象：小规模Transformer在中间数据集规模下泛化收敛最快，而非最大数据集。超过该阈值后，泛化仍可实现但需更多梯度更新。此外，在部分验证能力首次出现的阶段，更大数据集反而可能需要更少更新达到高训练准确率。乘法任务未呈现相同模式。

Conclusion: 研究表明，数据规模与泛化收敛之间存在非单调关系。关键数据量（引发泛化的最小量）与最优收敛量（收敛最快的量）是两个不同概念。在结构化输出任务中，学习规则与精确拟合可能产生分歧，为理解grokking现象提供了新视角。

Abstract: Critical-data-size accounts of grokking suggest a natural post-threshold intuition: once training data is sufficient to identify the underlying rule, additional data should accelerate validation convergence. We show that this intuition can fail in a controlled structured-output task. In Needleman--Wunsch (NW) matrix generation, small Transformers reach high validation exact-match accuracy fastest at an intermediate dataset size, not at the largest one. Past this dataset-size sweet spot, generalization remains achievable but requires more gradient updates. Conversely, in the regime where partial validation competence first appears, larger datasets can require fewer updates to reach high training accuracy, suggesting that emerging rule structure can accelerate fitting beyond example-wise memorization. A multiplication baseline does not show the same post-threshold slowdown. These results separate the critical data size for the onset of generalization from the dataset size that optimizes update-based convergence, and identify structured-output tasks where learning the rule and completing exact-fitting can diverge.

</details>


### [164] [FutureSim: Replaying World Events to Evaluate Adaptive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15188)
*Shashwat Goel,Nikhil Chandak,Arvindh Arun,Ameya Prabhu,Steffen Staab,Moritz Hardt,Maksym Andriushchenko,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FutureSim基准测试，通过时间顺序回放真实世界事件来评估AI智能体预测和适应长期世界事件的能力，揭示当前前沿模型在此任务上表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体被越来越广泛地部署在动态开放的环境中，需要适应不断到达的新信息，但现有评估方法无法真实测量这种持续适应能力。

Method: 构建了名为FutureSim的仿真环境，按照时间顺序回放真实世界事件（新闻文章和问题的解答），让AI智能体在自身测试框架中预测超过其知识截止日期后的世界事件。对前沿模型进行了从2026年1月到3月为期三个月的评估测试。

Result: 最佳智能体的准确率仅为25%，许多智能体的Brier技能分数甚至不如不做任何预测的基线。

Conclusion: FutureSim为研究长时域开放环境中的AI适应能力提供了现实的测试平台，可用于探索测试时适应、搜索、记忆和不确定性推理等新兴研究方向，有望推动AI在真实世界中的长期适应能力评估。

Abstract: AI agents are being increasingly deployed in dynamic, open-ended environments that require adapting to new information as it arrives. To efficiently measure this capability for realistic use-cases, we propose building grounded simulations that replay real-world events in the order they occurred. We build FutureSim, where agents forecast world events beyond their knowledge cutoff while interacting with a chronological replay of the world: real news articles arriving and questions resolving over the simulated period. We evaluate frontier agents in their native harness, testing their ability to predict world events over a three-month period from January to March 2026. FutureSim reveals a clear separation in their capabilities, with the best agent's accuracy being 25%, and many having worse Brier skill score than making no prediction at all. Through careful ablations, we show how FutureSim offers a realistic setting to study emerging research directions like long-horizon test-time adaptation, search, memory, and reasoning about uncertainty. Overall, we hope our benchmark design paves the way to measure AI progress on open-ended adaptation spanning long time-horizons in the real world.

</details>


### [165] [AQKA: Active Quantum Kernel Acquisition Under a Shot Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14672)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对量子核学习中测量shots这一核心瓶颈，提出AQKA方法通过考虑每个矩阵元素对分类器贡献的非均匀shot分配，在预算受限场景下相比均匀分配提升8-32个百分点，并提供完整的理论分析（修正的稀疏感知Cauchy-Schwarz率、plug-in regret bound）和首个多种子量子硬件实时自适应分配验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子核估计方法（Nyström-QKE、ShoFaR、核目标对齐）虽然在采样哪些条目方面进行了优化，但在选定的子集内仍采用均匀shot分配，忽视了不同条目对下游分类器的贡献差异。这导致在预算受限场景下效率低下，成为近-term量子硬件部署的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出AQKA（自适应量子核分配）方法，采用非均匀shot分配策略。基于 pair-level acquisition theory: $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$，其中$g_{ij}$对KRR为$|β_iα_j+β_jα_i|$，对SVM通过envelope theorem为$|η_i^*η_j^*|$。建立了完整的方法理论：修正的稀疏感知Cauchy-Schwarz率$ρ\le 2m/N$、显式常数的plug-in regret bound（Theorem 2）、更紧的SVM上限$ρ^{\mathrm{SVM}} \le m_{\mathrm{sv}}^2/N^2$。

Result: 实验表明：AQKA在稀疏敏感KRR的预算受限场景（$B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$）下全面胜出，随N从225增至1000，优势从+8 pts增至+25 pts；在ibm_pittsburgh (156量子比特Heron)硬件核上达+26--32 pts。Nyström-QKE仅在饱和预算的稀疏 planted场景下通过低秩重构获胜；ShoFaR仅在极端低预算时有竞争力。

Conclusion: AQKA在预算受限场景下显著优于均匀分配策略，且首次实现了多种子实时在线量子硬件上的自适应shot分配，在N=20时获得+17.0±4.8 pts提升（N=30时+14.0±8.5 pts），为量子核学习的大规模实际部署提供了可行的shot高效方案。

Abstract: Estimating an $N \times N$ quantum kernel from circuit fidelities requires $Θ(N^2 S)$ measurement shots, the dominant bottleneck for deployment on near-term hardware. Existing budget-saving methods (Nyström-QKE, ShoFaR, kernel-target alignment) sub-sample \emph{which} entries to measure but allocate shots \emph{uniformly} within their chosen subset, ignoring how much each entry drives the downstream classifier. We close this gap with two contributions. \textbf{First, a complete regime decomposition} for shot-budgeted quantum kernel learning: a principled menu of when each allocator wins. Our method, \emph{AQKA}, dominates the budget-limited regime ($B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$) on sparse-sensitivity KRR, with the gap \emph{growing} from $+8$ to $+25$ pts over uniform as $N$ scales $225{\to}1000$ and reaching $+26$--$32$ pts on an \texttt{ibm\_pittsburgh} (156-qubit Heron) hardware kernel; Nyström-QKE wins at saturating budgets on planted-sparse via low-rank reconstruction; ShoFaR is competitive only at extreme low budgets. \textbf{Second, a closed-form pair-level acquisition theory}: $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$ with explicit gradient $g_{ij}$ for KRR (Lemma~1, $|β_iα_j+β_jα_i|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$) and SVM via the envelope theorem ($|η_i^*η_j^*|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$); a \emph{corrected} sparsity-aware Cauchy--Schwarz rate $ρ\le 2m/N$ matching empirics (vs.\ the naive $m^2/N^2$); an explicit-constant plug-in regret bound (Theorem~2); and a tighter SVM ceiling $ρ^{\mathrm{SVM}} \le m_{\mathrm{sv}}^2/N^2$. We close with the first multi-seed live online adaptive shot allocation on quantum hardware: $+17.0 \pm 4.8$ pts at $N{=}20$ on \texttt{ibm\_aachen} ($3.5σ$, 5 seeds), with the advantage holding at $N{=}30$ at higher budget on \texttt{ibm\_berlin} ($+14.0 \pm 8.5$ pts, 5 seeds).

</details>


### [166] [ReMIA: a Powerful and Efficient Alternative to Membership Inference Attacks against Synthetic Data Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14686)
*Davide Scassola,Andrea Coser,Sebastiano Saccani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReMIA是一种实用的合成数据隐私度量指标，通过比较两个合成数据集的来源识别能力来评估成员推断攻击风险。相比需要数百次训练和高昂辅助数据成本的SOTA方法，ReMIA仅需两次训练且辅助数据不超过原始训练集，同时保持与SOTA MIAs相当的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: State-of-the-art membership inference attacks (MIAs) for synthetic data generators are powerful but impractical, requiring hundreds of SDG training runs and auxiliary data several times larger than the training set. Fast proxy metrics like DCR are efficient but lack sensitivity to MIA risk. A practical privacy metric that balances effectiveness and efficiency is needed.

Method: ReMIA generates two synthetic datasets from two source datasets and trains a binary classifier to identify which source a record originated from, measuring attack advantage as the privacy metric. Compared to shadow-model-based MIAs, it requires only two training runs instead of hundreds, and auxiliary data no larger than the original training set instead of several times larger.

Result: Experiments across multiple tabular datasets and SDGs demonstrate that ReMIA achieves sensitivity comparable to state-of-the-art MIAs while being substantially more practical. Additionally, SDGs can achieve privacy-utility trade-offs that traditional noise-based anonymization methods cannot match.

Conclusion: ReMIA achieves MIA-level sensitivity with only two SDG training runs and minimal auxiliary data, significantly improving practicality. Moreover, synthetic data generators can achieve privacy-utility trade-offs unattainable by traditional noise-based anonymization methods, demonstrating the potential superiority of SDGs over conventional approaches.

Abstract: Tabular data sharing under privacy constraints is increasingly important for research and collaboration. Synthetic data generators (SDGs) are a promising solution, but synthetic data remains vulnerable to attacks, such as membership inference attacks (MIAs), which aim to determine whether a specific record was part of the training data. State-of-the-art MIAs are powerful but impractical: they rely on shadow modeling, requiring hundreds of SDG training runs, and need auxiliary data several times larger than the original training set. Fast proxy metrics like distance to closest record (DCR) are efficient but have limited sensitivity to MIA risk. We introduce ReMIA (Relative Membership Inference Attack), a practical privacy metric that requires only two SDG training runs and additional data no larger than the original training set. Rather than predicting whether a record was in the training set, ReMIA generates two synthetic datasets from two source datasets and measures whether a classifier can identify which source a record came from. Experiments across multiple tabular datasets and SDGs show that ReMIA has a sensitivity comparable to state-of-the-art MIAs while being substantially more practical. We further observe that SDGs can achieve privacy-utility trade-offs that traditional noise-based anonymization methods do not match. Code is available at https://github.com/aindo-com/remia.

</details>


### [167] [The Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity Tradeoff in SAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14694)
*Tommaso Mencattini,Francesco Montagna,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper identifies and formalizes a fundamental Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity tradeoff in Sparse Autoencoders, demonstrating both theoretically and empirically that achieving monosemanticity comes at the cost of higher rate and distortion, and that the degree of polysemanticity is determined by feature co-occurrence probabilities in training data.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard SAEs optimizing for low distortion (reconstruction accuracy) and low rate (sparse feature usage) often fail to produce monosemantic (interpretable) representations, which limits their utility in mechanistic interpretability research.

Method: Theoretical and empirical analysis using toy-modeling assumptions; derivation of necessary conditions for polysemanticity measures when the data-generating process is unknown; benchmarking existing proxy metrics on SAEs trained on LLMs.

Result: Restricting SAEs to be monosemantic necessarily increases both rate and distortion. Optimal SAE polysemanticity is determined by feature co-occurrence probabilities in training data. Polysemanticity is a data problem rather than a purely technical one.

Conclusion: Polysemanticity is fundamentally a data problem determined by feature co-occurrence probabilities in training data, requiring architectural and optimization-level solutions rather than purely technical fixes.

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) that can accurately reconstruct their input (minimizing distortion) by making efficient use of few features (minimizing the rate) often fail to learn monosemantic representations (highly interpretable), limiting their usefulness for mechanistic interpretability. In this paper, we characterise this tension in learning faithful, efficient, and interpretable explanations, introducing the Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity tradeoff in SAEs. Under toy-modeling assumptions, we theoretically and empirically show that restricting the SAE to be monosemantic necessarily comes with an increase in rate and distortion. Assuming a generative model behind the input observations, we further demonstrate that the degree of polysemanticity of optimal SAEs is determined by the training data distribution, especially by the probability of features to co-occur. Finally, we extend the analysis to real-world settings by deriving necessary conditions that a polysemanticity measure should satisfy when the data-generating process is unknown, and we benchmark existing proxy metrics on SAEs trained on Large Language Models. Taken together, our findings show that polysemanticity is a data problem that should be accounted for when addressing it at the architectural and optimization level.

</details>


### [168] [NeuroAtlas: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Clinical EEG and Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14698)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Trui Osselaer,Stylianos G. Mouslech,Angeliki-Ilektra Karaiskou,Guido Gagliardi,Thomas Strypsteen,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Anku Rani,Maarten Vanmarcke,Miguel Bhagubai,Chanakya Ekbote,Jaedong Hwang,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroAtlas是目前最大的EEG基准测试，包含42个数据集和260k小时EEG数据，覆盖癫痫、睡眠医学、脑年龄估计和脑机接口等临床任务。研究评估了EEG基础模型和通用时序模型，结果表明：现有模型性能相近，EEG专用模型无明显优势；标准ML指标需被临床专用指标替代；当前模型尚未实现"开箱即用"的统一EEG基础模型愿景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型评估存在不一致性：数据集不同、预处理流程各异、评估指标差异大，导致临床相关性被掩盖。缺乏统一的、大规模的EEG基准来客观衡量模型性能。

Method: NeuroAtlas构建了42个数据集、260k小时EEG的基准；采用EEG专用模型与通用时序模型对比；针对不同临床任务设计专用评估指标（事件级决策质量、睡眠 hypnogram 特征、脑年龄差值）。

Result: 三个核心发现：1）EEG专用模型并不一致地优于通用时序模型；2）标准机器学习指标不足以评估临床效用，需采用临床相关的评估指标；3）同一领域内模型排名和性能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 当前预训练模型性能表现相近，仅有少数模型展现出微弱优势，尚未实现开箱即用的统一EEG基础模型。NeuroAtlas基准揭示了这一差距，并提供了下一代统一EEG基础模型所需的数据集和评估指标。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) promise to extract unified representations that generalize across downstream tasks. They have emerged across fields, including electroencephalography (EEG), but it is less clear how effective they are in this particular field. Published evaluations differ in datasets, in the EEG-specific preprocessing that might influence reported results, and in the reported metrics, frequently obscuring the clinical relevance in EEG. We introduce NeuroAtlas, the largest EEG benchmark to date: 42 datasets and 260k hours covering clinical EEG (epilepsy, sleep medicine, brain age estimation) and brain-computer interfaces, and include multiple datasets per task along with bespoke clinical evaluation metrics. Besides evaluating EEG-FMs with respect to supervised baselines, we present results from generic time-series FMs. We report three findings. First, EEG-specific FMs do not consistently outperform time-series FMs, which have neither EEG-focused architectures nor been pretrained on EEG. Second, standard machine learning metrics are insufficient to assess clinical utility: thus, we thoroughly evaluate more appropriate measures such as the quality of event-level decision-making, hypnogram-derived features, and the brain-age gap in the domains of epilepsy, sleep, and brain age, respectively. Third, model rankings and performance can vary substantially within domains. We conclude that pretrained models perform largely on par, with only narrow advantages for a few, and that current models do not yet deliver on the promise of an out-of-the-box unified EEG model. NeuroAtlas exposes this gap and provides the datasets and metrics for the next generation of unified EEG FMs.

</details>


### [169] [TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738)
*Krish Sharma,Omar Naim,Soumadeep Saha,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper investigates why task-aware layer pruning improves model performance, discovering it benefits out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization but not in-distribution (ID) accuracy. The mechanism is geometric: pruning removes layers that distort the task-adapted representation geometry, realigning OOD inputs with the model's learned geometric structure.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work showed task-aware layer pruning improves performance on specific tasks, but the mechanism and conditions under which these improvements occur were not understood.

Method: Controlled experiments across polynomial regression tasks and large language models; layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profile analysis; causal validation via distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions across multiple model scales.

Result: Task-aware pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution data but consistently improves out-of-distribution accuracy. The authors provide a geometric explanation: OOD inputs distort the task-adapted geometry, and pruning removes layers that amplify this distortion, thereby realigning OOD representations.
