Conclusion: Task-aware pruning selectively removes layers that distort task-adapted geometry for OOD inputs, effectively realigning OOD representations with the model's learned geometric structure and improving OOD generalization.

Abstract: Recent work has promoted task-aware layer pruning as a way to improve model performance on particular tasks, as shown by TALE. In this paper, we investigate when such improvements occur and why. We show first that, across controlled polynomial regression tasks and large language models, such pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution (ID) data but consistently improves out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy. We further show empirically that OOD inputs induce layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profiles that deviate from the corresponding ID profiles. This leads to a geometric explanation of task-aware pruning: each task induces a task-adapted geometry, characterized empirically by the representation profiles observed on ID inputs. OOD inputs can introduce a distorted version of the task-adapted geometry. Task-aware pruning identifies layers that create or amplify this distortion; by removing them, it shifts OOD representational norms and pairwise distances toward those observed on the adapted distribution. This realigns OOD inputs with the model's task-adapted geometry and improves performance. We provide causal evidence through controlled distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions, and demonstrate consistent behavior across model scales.

</details>


### [170] [Selective Safety Steering via Value-Filtered Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14746)
*Bat-Sheva Einbinder,Hen Davidov,Yee Whye Teh,Yarin Gal,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的测试时 steering 方法，通过价值过滤解码减少不必要的干预，同时改善不安全输出的安全性。该方法使用单一阈值超参数控制误干预概率边界，可在安全性与保真度之间灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解码时 steering 方法存在过度干预问题，将原本安全的输出也进行了不必要的修改，导致基础模型的关键特性（如有用性、流畅性、风格和连贯性）被扭曲

Method: 提出了一种价值过滤解码（Value-Filtered Decoding）方法，通过基于价值的安全标准过滤token，并提供一个关于错误干预概率的显式边界来控制干预程度

Result: 在多个数据集和实验上，该方法优于现有基线，在安全性、有用性和与基础模型的相似性之间取得了更好的权衡

Conclusion: 实现了更好的安全性与有用性之间的权衡，同时保持了与基础模型的更高相似性

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with human values, their generations may still violate safety constraints. A growing line of work addresses this problem by modifying the model's sampling policy at decoding time using a safety reward. However, existing decoding-time steering methods often intervene unnecessarily, modifying generations that would have been safe under the base model. Such unnecessary interventions are undesirable, as they can distort key properties of the base model such as helpfulness, fluency, style, and coherence. We propose a new test-time steering method designed to reduce such unnecessary interventions while improving the safety of unsafe responses. Our approach filters tokens using a value-based safety criterion and provides an explicit bound on the probability of false interventions. A single threshold hyperparameter controls this bound, allowing practitioners to trade off higher rates of unnecessary intervention for better output safety. Across multiple datasets and experiments, we show that our value-filtered decoding method outperforms existing baselines, achieving better trade-offs between safety, helpfulness, and similarity to the base model.

</details>


### [171] [Cognitive-Uncertainty Guided Knowledge Distillation for Accurate Classification of Student Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14752)
*Qirui Liu,Hao Chen,Weijie Shi,Jiajie Xu,Jia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段知识蒸馏框架，用于解决学生错误识别的三个核心挑战：数据稀缺与长尾分布、标注噪声高、以及部署困境（大小模型各有缺陷）。第一阶段进行标准蒸馏，第二阶段通过认知不确定性驱动的双层边际选择机制识别四类关键样本，并采用难度自适应机制平衡硬/软标签贡献。最终仅用4B参数模型在10.30%的精选样本下即可达到84.38%的准确率，显著超越67.73%的SOTA大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Accurately identifying student misconceptions faces three key challenges: (1) data scarcity with long-tail distribution making authentic student reasoning difficult to synthesize; (2) fuzzy boundaries between error categories with high annotation noise; (3) deployment paradox — large models cannot be deployed on edge and overlook unconventional approaches due to pretraining bias, while small models overfit to noise. Traditional methods relying on large-scale data synthesis are inadequate.

Method: Two-stage knowledge distillation framework: (1) Standard distillation to transfer task capabilities from teacher model; (2) Dual-layer marginal selection mechanism based on cognitive uncertainty to identify four types of critical samples. Additionally, difficulty-adaptive mechanism is designed to balance hard/soft label contributions for different data subsets, enabling the student model to inherit inter-class relationships from teacher soft labels while distinguishing ambiguous error types.

Result: On MAP-Charting dataset: MAP@3 of 0.9585, a +17.8% improvement over baseline, using only 10.30% of filtered samples. On cross-topic middle school algebra misconception benchmarks: 84.38% accuracy with a 4B parameter model, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art LLM (67.73%) and standard fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%).

Conclusion: The proposed two-stage knowledge distillation framework achieves state-of-the-art performance on student misconception identification tasks, with a 4B parameter model significantly outperforming large language models (67.73%) and fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%), using only 10.30% of filtered training samples. Code is available at the provided GitHub repository.

Abstract: Accurately identifying student misconceptions is crucial for personalized education but faces three challenges: (1) data scarcity with long-tail distribution, where authentic student reasoning is difficult to synthesize; (2) fuzzy boundaries between error categories with high annotation noise; (3) deployment parado-large models overlook unconventional approaches due to pretraining bias and cannot be deployed on edge, while small models overfit to noise. Unlike traditional methods that increase diversity through large-scale data synthesis, we propose a two-stage knowledge distillation framework that mines high-value samples from existing data. The first stage performs standard distillation to transfer task capabilities. The second stage introduces a dual-layer marginal selection mechanism based on cognitive uncertainty, identifying four types of critical samples based on teacher model uncertainty and confidence differences. For different data subsets, we design difficulty-adaptive mechanism to balance hard/soft label contributions, enabling student models to inherit inter-class relationships from teacher soft labels while distinguishing ambiguous error types. Experiments show that with augmented training on only 10.30% of filtered samples, we achieve MAP@3 of 0.9585 (+17.8%) on the MAP-Charting dataset, and using only a 4B parameter model, we attain 84.38% accuracy on cross-topic tests of middle school algebra misconception benchmarks, significantly outperforming sota LLM (67.73%) and standard fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%). Our code is available at https://github.com/RoschildRui/acl2026_map.

</details>


### [172] [Crys-JEPA: Accelerating Crystal Discovery via Embedding Screening and Generative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14759)
*Nian Liu,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Yann LeCun,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes Crys-JEPA to address the stability-novelty trade-off in crystal generation by learning energy-aware latent space, enabling embedding-based stability assessment without expensive energy evaluation. Achieves significant improvements on V.S.U.N metric.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current crystal generative models suffer from stability-novelty trade-off: maximizing likelihood of observed crystals preserves stability but limits novelty, while moving away from observed distribution destroys stability. The useful region for discovering crystals that are both stable and novel is extremely narrow.

Method: Crys-JEPA: joint embedding predictive architecture that learns energy-aware latent space preserving formation-energy differences, enabling stability assessment via embedding-based comparison against training crystals.

Result: On MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 datasets, Crys-JEPA achieves improvements over baselines up to 81.4% and 82.6% on V.S.U.N metric, respectively.

Conclusion: Screening-and-refinement pipeline identifies promising generated crystals and reintroduces them to refine the generative model, effectively escaping the stability-novelty trade-off.

Abstract: De novo crystal generation seeks to discover materials that are not merely realistic, but also stable and novel. However, most existing generative models are trained to maximize the likelihood of observed crystals, which encourages samples to stay close to known materials yet not necessarily align with the criteria that matter in discovery. Through an empirical investigation, we show that current crystal generative models are caught in a pronounced stability--novelty trade-off: moving toward the observed distribution preserves stability but limits novelty, whereas moving away from it quickly destroys stability. This suggests that the useful region for discovering crystals that are both stable and novel is extremely narrow. To escape the trade-off, we introduce Crys-JEPA, a joint embedding predictive architecture for crystals that learns an energy-aware latent space preserving formation-energy differences. In this space, stability assessment can be reformulated as an embedding-based comparison against accessible training crystals, reducing the reliance on expensive energy evaluation and task-specific external references. Building on Crys-JEPA, we further develop a screening-and-refinement pipeline that identifies promising generated crystals and reintroduces them to refine the generative model. On MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 datasets, we achieve improvements over baselines up to 81.4% and 82.6% on V.S.U.N metric, respectively.

</details>


### [173] [Compositional Sparsity as an Inductive Bias for Neural Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14764)
*Hongyu Lin,Antonio Briola,Yuanrong Wang,Tomaso Aste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The authors propose Homological Neural Networks (HNNs) combined with Information Filtering Networks to leverage compositional sparsity for overcoming the curse of dimensionality in deep learning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Deep Neural Networks suffer from the curse of dimensionality, and the hypothesis driving this work is that effective high-dimensional learning requires compositional sparsity—target functions decomposing into constituents supported on low-dimensional variable subsets.

Method: The authors combine Information Filtering Networks (IFNs) for sparse dependency structure extraction via constrained information maximization with Homological Neural Networks (HNNs) that map inferred topology into fixed-wiring sparse neural graphs, formalizing the design principles underlying this construction.

Result: HNNs are orders of magnitude sparser than standard DNNs, require minimal hyperparameter tuning, recover underlying compositional structure on synthetic tasks, remain stable as dimensionality increases (where dense alternatives degrade), and consistently match or outperform dense baselines on real-world datasets while using fewer parameters, showing lower variance and reduced hyperparameter sensitivity.

Conclusion: HNNs provide an interpretable framework where abstraction emerges through hierarchical composition, offering a theoretically grounded approach to high-dimensional learning with improved stability and generalization.

Abstract: Identifying the structural priors that enable Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) to overcome the curse of dimensionality is a fundamental challenge in machine learning theory. Existing literature suggests that effective high-dimensional learning is driven by compositional sparsity, where target functions decompose into constituents supported on low-dimensional variable subsets. To investigate this hypothesis, we combine Information Filtering Networks (IFNs), which extract sparse dependency structures via constrained information maximisation, with Homological Neural Networks (HNNs), which map the inferred topology into fixed-wiring sparse neural graphs. We formalise the design principles underlying this construction and present an interpretable pipeline in which abstraction emerges through hierarchical composition. HNNs are orders of magnitude sparser than standard DNNs and require only minimal hyperparameter tuning. On synthetic tasks with known sparse hierarchies, HNNs recover the underlying compositional structure and remain stable in regimes where dense alternatives degrade as dimensionality increases. Across a broad suite of real-world datasets, HNNs consistently match or outperform dense baselines while using far fewer parameters, exhibiting lower variance and showing reduced sensitivity to hyperparameters.

</details>


### [174] [Composable Crystals: Controllable Materials Discovery via Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14769)
*Nian Liu,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于概念的组合式晶体生成框架，利用 VQ-VAE 自动发现可解释的晶体概念（涵盖局域原子环境和全局对称性模式），通过概念重组实现可控探索训练分布之外的新型晶体，并引入组合生成器迭代优化提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖黑盒随机采样，缺乏对生成结构超出观测分布程度的控制能力。

Method: 采用向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）自动发现可复用的晶体概念作为构建模块；引入组合生成器并通过模型自身生成的高质量样本迭代优化概念组合，用于条件化下游晶体生成。

Result: 相比基线模型，V.S.U.N 指标提升分别达 53.2% 和 51.7%， novelty 提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 提出的概念组合框架在 MP-20 和 Alex-MP-20 数据集上，通过分别组合概念使基础模型在 V.S.U.N 指标上分别提升 53.2% 和 51.7%，尤其在 novelty 维度提升显著，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation, a central task in materials discovery, aims to generate crystals that are simultaneously valid, stable, unique, and novel. Existing methods mainly rely on black-box stochastic sampling, providing limited control over how generated structures move beyond the observed distribution. In this paper, we introduce a concept-based compositional framework for crystal generation. We train a vector-quantized variational autoencoder to automatically discover a shared set of reusable crystal concepts, which serve as building blocks for guided generation. These learned concepts naturally exhibit interpretability from both local atomic environments and global symmetry patterns, and generalize to crystals from different distributions. By recombining such concepts, our framework enables controllable exploration of novel crystals beyond the training distribution, rather than relying solely on unconstrained random sampling. To further improve composition efficiency, we introduce a composition generator and iteratively refine it using high-quality samples generated by the model itself. The resulting concept compositions are then used to condition downstream crystal generation. Numerical experiments on MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 show that compositing concepts separately increase base model up to 53.2% and 51.7% on V.S.U.N metric, with particular gains in novelty.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond What to Select: A Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-Volume Scheduling for Efficient Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14773)
*Suorong Yang,Hanqi Zhu,Hai Gan,Fangjian Su,Guang Li,Furao Shen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PODS通过振荡式数据量调度解决数据选择中的静态数据量问题，交替使用低比率正则化与高比率恢复阶段，在保持优化稳定性的同时利用选择诱导的正则化效果，兼容现有数据选择方法并适用于多种训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法主要关注样本重要性标准（选择什么），但在整个训练过程中固定数据量为目标比例，呈现"动态样本身份、静态数据量"的特点。从优化角度揭示关键权衡：较低比率放大选择诱导的正则化效果，而较高比率保留数据覆盖度和优化保真度。

Method: 提出PODS框架，通过在训练过程中交替执行低比率正则化阶段和高比率恢复阶段来动态调度数据量，而非引入新的样本评分指标。PODS直接调度目标选择比率，在正则化效果与优化稳定性之间取得平衡。

Result: ImageNet-1k训练成本降低50%且精度提升；LLM指令微调加速超过2倍且无性能下降；在多种数据集、架构和任务上始终改善效率-泛化权衡。

Conclusion: PODS是一个轻量级、比率级、任务无关的模块，可与现有的静态和动态数据选择方法兼容，适用于各种训练范式。在ImageNet-1k上实现50%训练成本降低且精度提升，在LLM指令微调中实现超过2倍加速且无性能下降。

Abstract: Data selection accelerates training by identifying representative training data while preserving model performance. However, existing methods mainly focus on designing sample-importance criteria, i.e., deciding what to select, while typically fixing the selected data volume as the target ratio throughout training. Thus, they are often dynamic in sample identity but static in data volume. In this work, we revisit data selection from an optimization perspective and show that selected-data training induces an implicit regularization effect modulated by the instantaneous selection ratio. This reveals a key trade-off: lower ratios amplify selection-induced regularization, whereas higher ratios preserve data coverage and optimization fidelity. Motivated by this insight, we propose PODS, a Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling framework. Rather than introducing another sample-scoring metric, PODS serves as a lightweight module that dynamically schedules how much data to select over training. Under the target selection ratio, PODS alternates between low-ratio regularization phases and high-ratio recovery phases to exploit selection-induced regularization without sacrificing optimization stability. With its lightweight, ratio-level, and task-agnostic design, PODS is compatible with existing static and dynamic selection methods and broadly applicable across training paradigms. Experiments across various datasets, architectures, and tasks show that PODS consistently improves the efficiency-generalization trade-off, e.g., reducing ImageNet-1k training cost by 50% with improved accuracy and accelerating LLM instruction tuning by over 2x without performance degradation.

</details>


### [176] [Peng's Q($λ$) for Conservative Value Estimation in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14779)
*Byeongchan Kim,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CPQL是一种无模型的离线多步强化学习算法，首次在理论上和实证上证明了保守值估计与多步操作符结合在离线RL中的有效性。核心创新是利用PQL算子替代Bellman算子实现更优的保守估计，平衡了过度悲观问题，同时为离线到在线学习提供了有效的预训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保守方法在离线强化学习中存在过度悲观估计的问题，导致性能低于行为策略。此外，缺乏对多步操作符在离线RL中理论分析和实证验证的研究。

Method: CPQL采用Peng's Q(λ)算子替代Bellman算子进行保守值估计。该算子的不动点更接近行为策略的价值函数，从而自然地引入隐式行为正则化。通过多步操作符全利用离线轨迹，同时平衡保守性与性能。

Result: CPQL在D4RL基准测试中一致且显著地优于现有离线单步基线方法。离线到在线学习场景中，预训练的Q函数使在线PQL智能体避免微调初期的性能下降并获得稳健的性能提升。

Conclusion: CPQL在D4RL基准测试中显著优于现有离线单步基线方法，同时为离线到在线学习框架提供了新的可能性——使用CPQL预训练的Q函数进行在线微调可避免性能下降并实现稳健的性能提升。

Abstract: We propose a model-free offline multi-step reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, Conservative Peng's Q($λ$) (CPQL). Our algorithm adapts the Peng's Q($λ$) (PQL) operator for conservative value estimation as an alternative to the Bellman operator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work in offline RL to theoretically and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of conservative value estimation with a \textit{multi-step} operator by fully leveraging offline trajectories. The fixed point of the PQL operator in offline RL lies closer to the value function of the behavior policy, thereby naturally inducing implicit behavior regularization. CPQL simultaneously mitigates over-pessimistic value estimation, achieves performance greater than (or equal to) that of the behavior policy, and provides near-optimal performance guarantees -- a milestone that previous conservative approaches could not achieve. Extensive numerical experiments on the D4RL benchmark demonstrate that CPQL consistently and significantly outperforms existing offline single-step baselines. In addition to the contributions of CPQL in offline RL, our proposed method also contributes to the offline-to-online learning framework. Using the Q-function pre-trained by CPQL in offline settings enables the online PQL agent to avoid the performance drop typically observed at the start of fine-tuning and to attain robust performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/oh-lab/CPQL.

</details>


### [177] [Understanding Imbalanced Forgetting in Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14785)
*Alberto Tamajo,Srinandan Dasmahapatra,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于排练的类别增量学习中类别间遗忘不平衡现象，通过构建三个梯度级干扰系数揭示了遗忘差异的机制，为未来通过减少类别间干扰源差异来缓解不平衡遗忘提供了有价值的见解和方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在类别增量学习（CIL）场景中，尽管采用平衡的排练策略，某些类别仍被遗忘得更多。这种不平衡遗忘现象尚未得到充分探索，需要揭示其内在机制。

Method: 该研究通过原理性分析构建了三个最后一层系数，用于捕捉增量学习步骤中影响每个旧类别遗忘的不同梯度级干扰源，并验证了这些系数对类别遗忘排名的预测能力。

Result: 三个最后一层系数能够可靠预测旧类别在增量步骤结束后的遗忘排名。其中，捕捉自我诱导干扰的系数是最强预测因子，控制实验证据表明该系数受新类别干扰系数的影响。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过减少识别出的干扰源在类别间的差异，可以有效缓解类别间遗忘不平衡问题。自我诱导干扰系数被发现是最强的预测因子，实验证据显示其受新类别干扰系数的影响。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from catastrophic forgetting in class-incremental learning (CIL) settings. Rehearsal$\unicode{x2013}$replaying a subset of past samples$\unicode{x2013}$is a well-established mitigation strategy. However, recent results suggest that, despite balanced rehearsal allocation, some classes are forgotten substantially more than others. Despite its relevance, this imbalanced forgetting phenomenon remains underexplored. This work shows that imbalanced forgetting arises systematically and severely in rehearsal-based CIL and investigates it extensively. Specifically, we construct, from a principled analysis, three last-layer coefficients that capture different gradient-level sources of interference affecting each past class during an incremental step. We then demonstrate that, together, they reliably predict how past classes will rank in terms of forgetting at the end of that step. While predictive performance alone does not establish causality, these results support the interpretation of the coefficients as a plausible mechanistic account linking last-layer gradient-level interactions during training to class-level forgetting outcomes. Notably, one coefficient$\unicode{x2013}$capturing self-induced interference$\unicode{x2013}$emerges as the strongest predictor, with controlled experiments providing evidence consistent with this coefficient being influenced by the new-class interference coefficient. Overall, our findings provide valuable insights and suggest promising directions for mitigating imbalanced forgetting by reducing class-wise disparities in the identified sources of interference.

</details>


### [178] [GFMate: Empowering Graph Foundation Models with Test-time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14809)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GFMate是一种与预训练无关的测试时图提示调优方法，通过重心提示和层提示以及互补学习目标，有效解决现有方法对源域依赖和未标记数据利用不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 将源域信息嵌入提示，导致对其他域和不同GFM的泛化受限；(2) 现有GFM提示仅依赖少样本微调，未充分利用未标记目标域测试数据的丰富信息。

Method: 提出GFMate框架，采用后训练方式应用重心提示（centroid prompts）和层提示（layer prompts），避免与特定源域和模型预训练的耦合；同时设计测试时互补学习目标，充分利用标记和未标记的目标域数据。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上的实验表明，GFMate性能提升高达30.63%，兼具优异的性能和高效性。

Conclusion: GFMate通过引入后训练阶段应用的重心提示和层提示，实现了与预训练无关的测试时图提示调优。通过利用标记和未标记的目标域数据，该方法在12个基准数据集上展示出优异性能和高效性，改进幅度高达30.63%。

Abstract: Graph prompt tuning has shown great potential in graph learning by introducing trainable prompts to enhance the model performance in conventional single-domain scenarios. Recent research has extended graph prompts to improve Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) by few-shot tuning auxiliary prompts. Despite their progress, most existing methods embed source-domain information into prompts, which serve either as input to GFMs or encoded during model pre-training. Such prompt entanglement with specific source domains and GFM pre-training strategy restricts their generalisability to other domains and different GFMs. Furthermore, existing GFM prompts merely rely on few-shot tuning for adaptation, neglecting the rich information in unlabelled target domain test data. Motivated by these insights, this paper aims to empower GFMs with pre-training-agnostic test-time graph prompt tuning, named GFMate. GFMate introduces centroid and layer prompts applied after pre-training on target domains, avoiding entanglement with specific source domains and model pre-training. In addition, a test-time complementary learning objective is devised to exploit both labelled and unlabelled target domain data for effective test-time prompt tuning. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance and efficiency of GFMate, achieving improvements of up to 30.63%. Code is available at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/GFMate.

</details>


### [179] [ToMAToMP: Robust and Multi-Parameter Topological Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14824)
*Ludo Andrianirina,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: We introduce ToMAToMP, the first topological clustering method able to handle multiple functions simultaneously with theoretical guarantees, using MMA decomposition from multi-parameter persistent homology. It overcomes ToMATo's limitations (graph hyperparameter dependency, outlier sensitivity, single-function constraint) and demonstrates superior performance through experiments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing topological clustering method ToMATo has three critical limitations: (1) requires user-provided graph hyper-parameter that needs fine-tuning, (2) sensitive to outliers in function values, and (3) can only handle one function at a time, which is inadequate for many real-world applications requiring multiple input functions.

Method: The method leverages Multi-parameter Morse Agreement (MMA) decomposition from multi-parameter persistent homology to design the clustering algorithm. This approach provides robustness guarantees through the theoretical framework.

Result: The proposed ToMAToMP achieves robustness guarantees theoretically. Numerical experiments demonstrate strong improvement over both non-topological and topological baselines across various datasets, successfully decoupling clustering from graph tuning requirements and enhancing outlier robustness.

Conclusion: ToMAToMP represents the first topological clustering method capable of handling multiple functions simultaneously with theoretical guarantees. The algorithm successfully addresses three major limitations of ToMATo: graph hyperparameter dependency, outlier sensitivity, and single-function constraint.

Abstract: Topological clustering, and its main algorithm ToMATo, is a clustering method from Topological Data Analysis (TDA) which has been applied successfully in several applications during the last few years. This is due to its high versatility, as clusters are detected from the persistent components in the sublevel sets of any user-defined function (gene expression, pixel values, etc), and efficiency, as topological clustering enjoys robustness guarantees. However, ToMATo is also limited in several ways. First, a graph on the data points needs to be provided as a hyper-parameter of the method (whose fine-tuning is left to the user). Second, ToMATo is known to be very sensitive to outlier values in the function range. Finally, and most importantly, ToMATo can only handle one function at a time, whereas it is critical to use several functions in various applications. In this article, we introduce ToMAToMP: the first topological clustering method able to handle several functions at the same time with theoretical guarantees. More specifically, we leverage a recent tool from multi-parameter persistent homology, called MMA decomposition, to design our clustering algorithm, and prove that it enjoys robustness properties. As corollaries, we show that it can be used to make ToMATo independent of graph tuning, and robust to outliers. Finally, we provide a set of numerical experiments showcasing the efficiency and quality of the clusterings produced by ToMAToMP, by showing strong improvement over non-topological and topological baselines for various datasets.

</details>


### [180] [GenAI for Energy-Efficient and Interference-Aware Compressed Sensing of GNSS Signals on a Google Edge TPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14839)
*Thorben Wegner,Lucas Heublein,Tobias Feigl,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Alexander Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式AI（VAE）的方法，在Google Edge TPU上对GNSS干扰信号进行实时压缩与分类。通过8位量化适配硬件，实现超过42倍的数据压缩，同时对约72种干扰类型保持高分类精度（F2=0.915），接近原始信号分类性能（F2=0.923）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNSS干扰分类方法依赖后处理和云端传输，存在成本高、延迟大、功耗高的问题。随着GNSS干扰事件日益增多，亟需一种适合功率受限环境的实时干扰检测与数据压缩方案。

Method: 使用变分自编码器（VAE）及其变体（条件VAE、FactorVAE）压缩GNSS原始IQ数据、FFT数据和手工特征；采用8位量化将大模型部署至Google Edge TPU实现边缘计算；评估多种自编码器架构在保留干扰特征前提下的重建质量。

Result: 系统实现超过42倍数据压缩，对72种干扰类型分类F2分数达0.915，与原始信号分类（F2=0.923）性能相当；消融实验验证了潜在特征解耦对模型可解释性和可信度的提升。

Conclusion: 基于边缘生成式AI的GNSS干扰检测方案在保证高压缩率和分类精度的同时，大幅降低了数据传输成本，为敏感干扰应用中的ML方案提供了实用且可信的解决路径。

Abstract: Traditional methods for classifying global navigation satellite system (GNSS) jamming signals typically involve post-processing raw or spectral data streams, requiring complex and costly data transmission to cloud-based interference classification systems. In contrast, our proposed approach efficiently compresses GNSS data streams directly at the hardware receiver while simultaneously classifying jamming and spoofing attacks in real time. Given the growing prevalence of GNSS jamming, there is a critical need for real-time solutions suitable for power-constrained environments. This paper introduces a novel method for compressing and classifying GNSS jamming threats using generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), specifically variational autoencoders (VAEs), deployed on Google Edge tensor processing units (TPUs). The study evaluates various autoencoder (AE) architectures to compress and reconstruct GNSS signals, focusing on preserving interference characteristics while minimizing data size near the receiver hardware. The pipeline adapts large-scale AE models for Google Edge TPUs through 8-bit quantization to ensure energy-efficient deployment. Tests on raw in-phase and quadrature-phase (IQ) data, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) data, and handcrafted features show the system achieves significant compression (>42x) and accurate classification of approximately 72 interference types on reconstructed signals (F2-score 0.915), closely matching the original signals (F2-score 0.923). The hardware-centric GenAI approach also substantially reduces jammer signal transmission costs, offering a practical solution for interference mitigation. Ablation studies on conditional and factorized VAEs (i.e., FactorVAE) explore latent feature disentanglement for data generation, enhancing model interpretability and fostering trust in machine learning (ML) solutions for sensitive interference applications.

</details>


### [181] [In-Context Learning for Data-Driven Censored Inventory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14840)
*Sohom Mukherjee,Anh-Duy Pham,Richard Pibernik,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究决策依赖审查下的库存控制问题，提出ICGPS框架结合生成模型与在线学习，理论证明regret bound并通过ChronosFlow实现有效解决重复报童问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策依赖的审查（censoring）在库存控制中普遍存在；现有参数化汤普森采样（TS）方法在先验不匹配时脆弱；离线填补方法不一定能迁移到在线学习场景。

Method: 提出ICGPS方法，使用离线元训练并在在线部署时通过上下文自回归生成的现代生成模型；理论证明贝叶斯 regret受TS基准 regret加部署惩罚控制；通过ChronosFlow实例化（冻结的时间序列Transformer主干+可训练的归一化流头）实现快速审查一致的采样。

Result: 理论上，ICGPS的贝叶斯 regret受理想完成核的TS基准regret加部署惩罚控制；对于R-NV问题，通过将审查反馈归约为 bandit凸优化反馈，推导出次线性贝叶斯 regret；在线完成不匹配由离线审查预测不匹配控制，离线预测质量可迁移到在线性能。

Conclusion: ICGPS通过ChronosFlow实现，在基准实验中匹配正确指定的TS、优于短视和UCB风格基线，并对先验不匹配和分布偏移具有鲁棒性，在SuperStore真实数据集上表现良好，尤其是重度审查情况下。

Abstract: We study inventory control with decision-dependent censoring, focusing on the censored or repeated newsvendor (R-NV), where each order quantity determines whether demand is fully observed or censored by sales. Existing approaches based on parametric Thompson sampling (TS) can be brittle under prior mismatch, while offline imputation methods need not transfer to online learning. Motivated by the predictive view of decision making, we combine these ideas by taking oracle actions on learned completions of latent demand. We propose in-context generative posterior sampling (ICGPS), which uses modern generative models that are meta-trained offline and deployed online by in-context autoregressive generation. Theoretically, we show that the Bayesian regret of ICGPS with a learned completion kernel is bounded by the Bayesian regret of a TS benchmark with the ideal completion kernel plus a deployment penalty scaling as $\sqrt{T}$ times the square root of the completion mismatch. This yields a plug-in template for operational problems with known TS regret bounds. For R-NV, we derive sublinear Bayesian regret by reducing censored feedback to bandit convex optimization feedback. We also show that, under reasonable coverage and stability assumptions, the online completion mismatch is controlled by the offline censored predictive mismatch, so offline predictive quality transfers to online performance. Practically, we instantiate ICGPS with ChronosFlow, which combines a frozen time-series transformer backbone with a trainable conditional normalizing-flow head for fast censoring-consistent sampling. In benchmark experiments, ChronosFlow-ICGPS matches correctly specified TS, outperforms myopic and UCB-style baselines, and is robust to prior mismatch and distribution shift. ChronosFlow-ICGPS also performs well for the real-world SuperStore dataset, especially under heavy censoring.

</details>


### [182] [GPart: End-to-End Isometric Fine-Tuning via Global Parameter Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14841)
*Paolo Mandica,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GPart通过消除低秩瓶颈，提出一种仅需单个等距随机投影的参数高效微调方法。该方法以极简设计(d维可训练向量+随机种子)实现了端到端等距映射，在多种任务上达到或超越现有PEFT方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA的bilinear结构引入关键限制：从可训练参数到权重更新的映射不保持距离，扭曲了优化景观。现有方法如Uni-LoRA虽改善参数效率，但其后续bilinear映射仍破坏端到端等距，距离保真问题未被根本解决。

Method: GPart采用单一等距分区矩阵，将d维可训练向量直接映射到模型完整权重空间。核心设计包括：单一随机投影实现端到端等距，仅需一个超参数(d)，存储开销仅为d+1个值(可训练向量+随机种子)。

Result: 在自然语言理解、计算机视觉和数学推理任务上，GPart实现了优于或持平于现有PEFT方法的性能，达到最先进的效率和性能表现。

Conclusion: GPart通过移除LoRA的低秩结构约束，提出了一种简洁优雅的参数高效微调范式。其核心突破在于使用单一线性映射实现端到端等距，避免了传统方法中bilinear结构导致的距离扭曲问题。这项工作表明，低维随机子空间足以支撑有效微调，无需低秩矩阵结构的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the dominant paradigm for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of large language models (LLMs). However, its bilinear structure introduces a critical limitation: the mapping from trainable parameters to weight updates is not distance-preserving, distorting the optimization landscape. Methods that project a low-dimensional vector into LoRA's parameter space, such as Uni-LoRA, improve parameter efficiency, but the subsequent bilinear LoRA map breaks end-to-end isometry, leaving the core distance-preservation problem unresolved. We propose GPart (Global Partition fine-tuning), a highly parameter-efficient fine-tuning method which removes the low-rank bottleneck entirely. Our method uses a single isometric partition matrix to map a $d$-dimensional trainable vector directly into the full weight space of the model. The result is an extremely minimal fine-tuning pipeline: one random projection, end-to-end isometric, with a single clean hyperparameter ($d$) and storage cost of $d+1$ values (the trainable vector plus a random seed). GPart builds on the theoretical premise that effective fine-tuning can emerge from random low-dimensional subspaces of the full weight space, without imposing low-rank matrix structure. We empirically demonstrate the superior or comparable performance of GPart to existing PEFT methods on natural language understanding, computer vision tasks, and mathematical reasoning. Overall, GPart achieves state-of-the-art efficiency and performance by removing structural constraints, offering a straightforward and elegant path to PEFT.

</details>


### [183] [Exploitation of Hidden Context in Dynamic Movement Forecasting: A Neural Network Journey from Recurrent to Graph Neural Networks and General Purpose Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14855)
*Lukas Schelenz,Shobha Rajanna,Denis Gosalci,Lucas Heublein,Jonas Pirkl,Jonathan Ott,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Tobias Feigl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了NBA球员轨迹预测模型，对比传统方法与多种深度学习架构，发现结合上下文信息的混合LSTM在准确性、效率上取得最佳平衡，但无单一架构在所有指标上均占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育场景中物体运动具有突发性速度与方向变化，传统线性模型难以捕获非线性动力学；现有ML方法虽具灵活性，却常忽视时序依赖与上下文交互的协同建模需求。

Method: 对多种预测模型进行系统性评估与对比实验，涵盖传统方法（ARIMA、卡尔曼滤波、粒子滤波）与机器学习方法（LSTM、TCNN、GAT、Transformer）。实验考察输入历史长度泛化能力及上下文信息整合能力，测试前瞻时间范围达2秒。

Result: 混合LSTM结合上下文信息取得最低最终位移误差（FDE）1.51m，优于TCNN、GAT及Transformer，且所需训练数据与时间更少。

Conclusion: 混合LSTM模型在综合性能上表现最佳，但研究强调在快速、动态环境中进行轨迹预测时需根据具体任务需求进行架构选择。

Abstract: Forecasting within signal processing pipelines is crucial for mitigating delays, particularly in predicting the dynamic movements of objects such as NBA players. This task poses significant challenges due to the inherently interactive and unpredictable nature of sports, where abrupt changes in velocity and direction are prevalent. Traditional approaches, including (S)ARIMA(X), Kalman filters (KF), and Particle filters (PF), often struggle to model the non-linear dynamics present in such scenarios. Machine learning (ML) methods, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, graph neural networks (GNNs), and Transformers, offer greater flexibility and accuracy but frequently fail to explicitly capture the interplay between temporal dependencies and contextual interactions, which are critical in chaotic sports environments. In this paper, we evaluate these models and assess their strengths and weaknesses. Experimental results reveal key performance trade-offs across input history length, generalizability, and the ability to incorporate contextual information. ML-based methods demonstrated substantial improvements over linear models across forecast horizons of up to 2s. Among the tested architectures, our hybrid LSTM augmented with contextual information achieved the lowest final displacement error (FDE) of 1.51m, outperforming temporal convolutional neural network (TCNN), graph attention network (GAT), and Transformers, while also requiring less data and training time compared to GAT and Transformers. Our findings indicate that no single architecture excels across all metrics, emphasizing the need for task-specific considerations in trajectory prediction for fast-paced, dynamic environments such as NBA gameplay.

</details>


### [184] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REALM是一种回顾性蒸馏框架，将预训练的多会话双向LFP模型蒸馏为轻量级因果学生模型，实现实时LFP解码。该方法在行为解码任务中超越现有SOTA，且参数量减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍，为无线植入式BCI提供了LFP-only的可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前BCI系统面临高通道数和无线化趋势，但尖峰信号的高采样率造成功耗和带宽瓶颈。虽然LFP具有更好的长期稳定性、更低能耗和带宽需求，但LFP解码模型精度较低且通常依赖非因果架构，无法满足实时部署需求。

Method: REALM采用回顾性蒸馏框架：(1)使用掩码自编码目标预训练双向Mamba-2教师模型；(2)通过表征对齐和任务监督的联合目标将教师模型蒸馏到紧凑的因果学生模型。

Result: REALM在行为解码任务中持续超越因果和非因果的LFP-based SOTA方法，在减少50%参数量的同时实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: REALM框架成功地将离线双向模型的知识蒸馏到轻量级因果学生模型，实现了性能提升、参数减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍，证明了LFP-only模型在神经解码中具有竞争力，为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了实用且可扩展的替代方案。

Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

</details>


### [185] [AIMing for Standardised Explainability Evaluation in GNNs: A Framework and Case Study on Graph Kernel Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14884)
*Magdalena Proszewska,N. Siddharth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIM框架用于评估GNN可解释性，通过准确性、实例级和模型级三个维度进行综合评估。该框架克服了现有方法难以跨模型比较的缺陷，并在GKN和PN上验证有效性，最终基于AIM洞察开发出改进版xGKN模型，为GNN可解释性研究提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN可解释性评估框架存在明显局限：后置解释方法难以跨模型比较，可解释模型评估维度单一，缺乏全面通用的评估方案。

Method: 采用三维度评估框架AIM（准确性、实例级解释、模型级解释），在管道中提取可解释GNN的解释并评估，结合GKN案例分析指导模型改进，迭代开发出改进的xGKN模型。

Result: 成功构建了AIM评估框架，验证了其在GKN和PN上的适用性；基于AIM洞察改进了GKN，开发出保持高准确性的xGKN模型，显著提升了可解释性。

Conclusion: AIM框架通过提供全面、可比的评估方案，推动了GNN可解释性研究的发展，为理解和改进复杂模型提供了更实用的工具；xGKN案例验证了该框架的实用价值。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have advanced significantly in handling graph-structured data, but a comprehensive framework for evaluating explainability remains lacking. Existing evaluation frameworks primarily involve post-hoc explanations, and operate in the setting where multiple methods generate a suite of explanations for a single model. This makes comparison of explanations across models difficult. Evaluation of inherently interpretable models often targets a specific aspect of interpretability relevant to the model, but remains underdeveloped in terms of generating insight across a suite of measures. We introduce AIM, a comprehensive framework that addresses these limitations by measuring Accuracy, Instance-level explanations, and Model-level explanations. AIM is formulated with minimal constraints to enhance flexibility and facilitate broad applicability. Here, we use AIM in a pipeline, extracting explanations from inherently interpretable GNNs such as graph kernel networks (GKNs) and prototype networks (PNs), evaluating these explanations with AIM, identifying their limitations and obtaining insights to their characteristics. Taking GKNs as a case study, we show how the insights obtained from AIM can be used to develop an updated model, xGKN, that maintains high accuracy while demonstrating improved explainability. Our approach aims to advance the field of Explainable AI (XAI) for GNNs, providing more robust and practical solutions for understanding and improving complex models.

</details>


### [186] [TILBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Tabular Imbalanced Learning Across Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14915)
*Ruizhe Liu,Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TILBench是一个针对表格数据不平衡学习的大规模实证基准测试平台，系统评估了40余种算法在57个数据集上的表现。研究发现没有单一方法能通吃所有场景，效果主要取决于数据特征和算力限制，并据此给出实用的方法选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管不平衡学习研究已长达数十年，但学界仍缺乏对不同方法在预测性能、数据特征鲁棒性和计算可扩展性方面的系统性实证比较研究。

Method: 创建了TILBench大规模基准测试平台，评估超过40种代表性算法在57个多样化表格数据集上的表现，累计完成超过20万次受控实验。

Result: 研究表明不存在单一方法在所有场景下始终占优；不平衡学习方法的有效性高度依赖于数据集特征和计算约束条件。

Conclusion: 提供了实用的方法选择建议，指导从业者在实际应用中根据数据特征和计算约束选择合适的处理不平衡学习方法。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning remains a fundamental challenge in tabular data applications. Despite decades of research and numerous proposed algorithms, a systematic empirical understanding of how different imbalanced learning methods behave across diverse data characteristics is still lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how different method families compare in predictive performance, robustness under varying data characteristics, and computational scalability. In this work, we present Tabular Imbalanced Learning Benchmark (TILBench), a large-scale empirical benchmark for tabular imbalanced learning. TILBench evaluates more than 40 representative algorithms across 57 diverse tabular datasets, resulting in over 200000 controlled experiments across a wide range of data characteristics. Our findings show that no single method consistently dominates across all settings; instead, the effectiveness of imbalanced learning methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics and computational constraints. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for selecting appropriate methods in real-world applications.

</details>


### [187] [A Mutual Information Lower Bound for Multimodal Regression Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14917)
*Leonardo Ferreira Guilhoto,Akshat Kaushal,Paris Perdikaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Two-Index框架解决连续回归主动学习中的多模态不确定性识别问题，通过输出与认知索引间的互信息作为获取目标，推导了可计算的MI-LB闭式近似。在多模态基准测试中，MI-LB显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续回归的主动学习中，当预测分布呈多模态时，现有的获取函数存在根本性缺陷：方差无法捕捉模态间的分歧，而像BAL这样信息论方法专为离散输出设计，不适用于连续场景。这意味着现有方法无法正确识别和利用认知不确定性来指导数据选择。

Method: 该研究提出Two-Index框架，将预测中的不确定性解耦为两个独立的索引：随机索引（选择不同模型假设，认知来源）和内部索引（管理假设内的随机性，偶然来源）。基于此框架，他们将获取目标定义为输出与认知索引之间的互信息，并针对连续输出难以精确计算的问题，推导出MI-LB（互信息下界）作为Mixture Density Network集成的闭式近似获取函数。

Result: 在包含多模态系统的基准测试中，MI-LB与所有评估的基线方法持平或更优，且是唯一能持续保持竞争力的方法。基于几何和Fisher的基线方法仅在输入空间已编码多模性时才能竞争，否则会失效。理论证明方面，该互信息量随训练数据增长而趋于零，验证了其恰好捕捉数据可解决的不确定性。

Conclusion: 该研究为连续回归的主动学习提供了一个理论上严谨且实践有效的框架。MI-LB作为首个专门针对多模态场景设计的获取函数，在各种基准测试中表现稳健，填补了现有方法在高不确定性场景中的空白。互信息熵分解为理解学习过程中的不确定性来源提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Active learning for continuous regression has lacked an acquisition function that targets epistemic uncertainty when the predictive distribution is multimodal: variance misses modal disagreement, and information-theoretic targets like BALD are designed for discrete outputs. We introduce a Two-Index framework that makes this separation explicit: one stochastic index selects among competing model hypotheses (epistemic source), while a second governs within-hypothesis randomness (aleatoric source). An entropy decomposition within the framework identifies the mutual information between the output and the epistemic index as a principled acquisition objective, and we prove this quantity vanishes as the model is trained on growing datasets, confirming that it captures exactly the uncertainty data can resolve. Because this mutual information is intractable for continuous outputs, we derive the Mutual Information Lower Bound (MI-LB) acquisition function, a closed-form approximation for Mixture Density Network ensembles. On benchmarks featuring multimodal systems, MI-LB matches or beats every baseline evaluated and is the only method to do so consistently -- geometric and Fisher-based baselines compete only when the input space already encodes the multimodality, and collapse otherwise.

</details>


### [188] [A Hardware-Aware, Per-Layer Methodology for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14929)
*Earl Killian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOP is a post-training quantization method for LLM weights achieving near-lossless fidelity at 4.5-6 bits per weight using per-layer LUT decode. It combines fixed/dynamic codebook pairs, signed per-block scales, and sparse-residual correction. The recommended FP6 (6.5 bpw) outperforms FP8 (8.0 bpw) baselines while using 1.5 bpw less storage.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Need to achieve near-lossless quantization fidelity for large language model weights at very low bit-widths (4.5-6 bits per weight) on hardware with per-layer LUT decode.

Method: SOP methodology combines: per-layer search of fixed and dynamic codebook pairs with per-block selection bit; signed per-block scales; activation-weighted cosine selection; multiple-choice knapsack promotion of sensitive layers with outlier and sparse-residual correction. Fixed codebooks include NF4, BOF4, Split87, SH4; per-layer optimized codebooks (DD4) are hosted in LUT SRAM. New hardware-efficient LUT output format (HIF) proposed.

Result: Recommended FP6 operating point (E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw) achieves lower weight reconstruction error than conventional per-layer-POT FP8 baseline (E4M3, 8.0 bpw) at 1.5 bpw lower storage cost.

Conclusion: Block-scaled small atoms with carefully chosen scale precision can replace conventionally-deployed FP8, offering both lower storage cost (1.5 bpw reduction) and lower weight reconstruction error.

Abstract: Scaled Outer Product (SOP) is a post-training quantization methodology for large language model weights, designed to deliver near-lossless fidelity at 4.5--6 bits per weight on hardware with per-layer LUT decode. The methodology combines per-layer search of fixed and dynamic codebook pairs selected by a per-block selection bit, signed per-block scales, activation-weighted cosine selection, and multiple-choice knapsack promotion of sensitive layers with outlier and sparse-residual correction. Fixed codebooks include NF4, BOF4, Split87, and SH4; per-layer optimized codebooks (DD4) are hosted in LUT SRAM. A new hardware-efficient LUT output format (HIF) is proposed to improve performance, energy, and cost. Across six open model families, the recommended FP6 operating point (E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw) achieves lower weight reconstruction error than the conventional per-layer-POT FP8 baseline (E4M3, 8.0 bpw) at 1.5 bpw lower storage cost, demonstrating that block-scaled small atoms with carefully chosen scale precision can replace conventionally-deployed FP8. Full evaluation across the 4.5--6 bpw range, including layer promotion and sparse residual correction, is reported in a companion paper.

</details>


### [189] [Slot-MPC: Goal-Conditioned Model Predictive Control with Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14937)
*Jonathan Spieler,Angel Villar-Corrales,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Slot-MPC，一种结合目标中心表示和模型预测控制的世界建模框架，通过基于槽位的视觉编码器捕捉场景中的独立对象，利用可微分动态模型进行基于梯度的动作规划，实现对未见情况的适应性决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标中心世界模型和强化学习方法主要学习反应式策略，这些策略在推理时是固定的，限制了模型对新情况的泛化能力。

Method: Slot-MPC框架包含三个核心组件：1) 使用视觉编码器学习基于槽位（slot）的表示，将场景中的单个对象编码为独立的表示；2) 基于这些结构化表示学习动作条件的目标中心动态模型；3) 推理时通过MPC进行动作规划，并利用可微分的世界模型进行基于梯度的MPC优化，相比无梯度采样方法更高效。

Result: 在模拟机器人操作任务上的实验表明，Slot-MPC相比非目标中心的世界模型基线在任务性能和规划效率上都有提升；在离线设置且状态-动作覆盖有限的情况下，基于梯度的MPC比无梯度采样方法表现更好。

Conclusion: Slot-MPC demonstrates that object-centric representations provide strong inductive bias for controllable and generalizable decision-making, with gradient-based MPC outperforming sampling-based approaches in offline settings with limited coverage.

Abstract: Predictive world models enable agents to model scene dynamics and reason about the consequences of their actions. Inspired by human perception, object-centric world models capture scene dynamics using object-level representations, which can be used for downstream applications such as action planning. However, most object-centric world models and reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn reactive policies that are fixed at inference time, limiting generalization to novel situations. We propose Slot-MPC, an object-centric world modeling framework that enables planning through Model Predictive Control (MPC). Slot-MPC leverages vision encoders to learn slot-based representations, which encode individual objects in the scene, and uses these structured representations to learn an action-conditioned object-centric dynamics model. At inference time, the learned dynamics model enables action planning via MPC, allowing agents to adapt to previously unseen situations. Since the learned world model is differentiable, we can use gradient-based MPC to directly optimize actions, which is computationally more efficient than relying on gradient-free, sampling-based MPC methods. Experiments on simulated robotic manipulation tasks show that Slot-MPC improves both task performance and planning efficiency compared to non-object-centric world model baselines. In the considered offline setting with limited state-action coverage, we find that gradient-based MPC performs better than gradient-free, sampling-based MPC. Our results demonstrate that explicitly structured, object-centric representations provide a strong inductive bias for controllable and generalizable decision-making. Code and additional results are available at https://slot-mpc.github.io.

</details>


### [190] [Octopus: History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization for Continual Learning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14938)
*Yuehao Liu,Shanyan Guan,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Yanhao Ge,Wei Li,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Octopus，一个基于梯度正交化的两阶段持续学习框架，用于多模态大语言模型，旨在解决灾难性遗忘问题，无需存储历史数据即可实现任务间的参数正交化，在多个指标上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在根本性局限：基于架构的方法计算开销大且泛化性差；基于回放的方法依赖存储历史数据，存在隐私和存储问题；传统正则化策略不足以完全防止参数干扰。

Method: 提出两阶段持续学习框架Octopus，基于无历史梯度正交化(HiFGO)，在历史任务数据缺失的情况下强制执行梯度级正交性，将任务适应与正则化解耦，在可塑性和稳定性之间实现原则性平衡。

Result: 在UCIT上建立SOTA性能，Avg和Last指标分别比之前最优方法提升2.14%和6.82%。

Conclusion: Octopus在UCIT上建立了最先进的性能，在Avg和Last指标上分别超过之前的SOTA 2.14%和6.82%。

Abstract: Continual learning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) aims to sequentially acquire knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, yet existing methods face inherent limitations: architecture-based approaches incur additional computational overhead and often generalize poorly to new tasks, rehearsal-based methods rely on storing historical data, raising privacy and storage concerns, and conventional regularization-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully prevent parameter interference. We propose Octopus, a two-stage continual learning framework based on History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization (HiFGO), which enforces gradient-level orthogonality without historical task data. Our proposed two-stage finetuning strategy decouples task adaptation from regularization, achieving a principled balance between plasticity and stability. Experiments on UCIT show that Octopus establishes state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior SOTA by 2.14% and 6.82% in terms of Avg and Last.

</details>


### [191] [Efficient Online Conformal Selection with Limited Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14953)
*Sreenivas Gollapudi,Kostas Kollias,Kamesh Munagala,Ali Sinop*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在最小反馈条件下的共形选择问题，通过对ACI更新规则应用于控制参数，在bandit和semi-bandit反馈模型下证明了该方法的对抗性有效性和随机效率，为有限反馈在线学习与分布无关不确定性量化之间建立了理论联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线共形预测虽能保持观测序列的有效性，但在有限反馈下最小化资源成本（效率）的选择问题仍具挑战性。

Method: 通过Lyapunov函数的分析框架，将ACI更新规则应用于控制参数或对偶变量，在统一的算法技术下处理bandit和semi-bandit反馈模型。

Result: 该方法具有对抗性有效性（对任意输入序列平均满足成功目标，可应对分布漂移）和随机有效性（对i.i.d.输入达到次线性效率遗憾）。

Conclusion: 该方法为具有有限反馈的高效在线学习与分布无关的不确定性量化之间建立了新的理论桥梁，同时比先前工作处理更复杂的设置，且反馈需求显著更少。

Abstract: We address the problem of conformal selection, where an agent must select a minimal subset of options to ensure that at least one ``success'' is identified with a pre-specified target probability $φ$. While traditional online conformal prediction focuses on maintaining validity for the observed sequence, minimizing the resource cost (efficiency) of such selections, especially under limited feedback, remains a significant challenge. In this work, we consider settings with the most limited ``bandit'' feedback, and demonstrate that the simple Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) update rule, when applied to the appropriate control parameter or dual variable, is both adversarially valid, ensuring the success target is met on average for any input sequence (and hence under distribution shifts), and stochastically efficient, achieving sublinear efficiency regret for $i.i.d.$ inputs against an appropriate stochastic benchmark. We show such guarantees under canonical models capturing bandit and semi-bandit feedback to the agent via a unifying algorithmic technique, and analytic framework involving Lyapunov functions. Our approach handles more complex settings than prior work, while requiring significantly less feedback, and our results provide a new theoretical bridge between efficient online learning with limited feedback and distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [192] [InfoSFT: Learn More and Forget Less with Information-Aware Token Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14967)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Adel Javanmard,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoSFT提出一种针对SFT的加权策略，聚焦于中等置信度标记以提升学习效率，在多种任务和模型上优于标准SFT，且更好地保留预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT均匀处理所有样本——包括那些在基础模型下可能性较低的样本——这可能导致过度拟合特定样本而非学习目标行为。此外，适应这些低可能性样本会引发显著的策略偏移，从而降低先前的能力。现有方法通过过滤、再生或降权低可能性数据来缓解这一问题，但在此过程中往往抑制了基础模型尚未学习的特定新行为。

Method: InfoSFT是一个原则性的加权方案，用于SFT目标，通过将学习信号集中在最大信息量的中等置信度标记上（即那些对基础模型既不过于熟悉也不会因过于不可能而不稳定的标记）来实现。

Result: 在数学、代码和思维链任务中，InfoSFT明显改善了泛化能力，优于 vanilla SFT 和基于似然加权的基线方法，同时更好地保留了预训练能力。

Conclusion: InfoSFT requires only a one-line modification to the standard token-wise loss and demonstrably improves generalization over vanilla SFT and likelihood-weighted baselines across math, code, and chain-of-thought tasks with diverse model families, while better preserving pre-existing capabilities.

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides the standard approach for teaching LLMs new behaviors from offline expert demonstrations. However, standard SFT uniformly fits all samples -- including those with low likelihood under the base model -- which can disproportionately drive training updates toward overfitting specific samples rather than learning the target behavior. Moreover, adapting to these unlikely samples induces substantial policy shifts that degrade prior capabilities. Existing methods mitigate this by filtering, regenerating, or down-weighting low-likelihood data. In doing so, they often suppress precisely the novel behaviors the base model has yet to learn.
  We propose InfoSFT, a principled weighting scheme for the SFT objective that concentrates learning signals on maximally informative, medium-confidence tokens -- those neither overly familiar to the base model nor too unlikely to cause instability. Requiring only a one-line modification to the standard token-wise loss, InfoSFT demonstrably improves generalization over vanilla SFT and likelihood-weighted baselines across math, code, and chain-of-thought tasks with diverse model families, while better preserving pre-existing capabilities.

</details>


### [193] [Second-Order Actor-Critic Methods for Discounted MDPs via Policy Hessian Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14982)
*Sanjeev Manivannan,Shuban V*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对折扣奖励强化学习中的策略梯度问题，提出了利用Hessian向量积的二阶Actor-Critic方法。通过双时间尺度框架（批评者快速演化），使行动值函数在Actor更新期间可被视为局部常数，从而在保持稳定性的同时实现计算高效的二阶优化更新，加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶更新在策略梯度方法中存在收敛速度慢的问题，而二阶优化虽能加速收敛，但Hessian估计的高计算复杂度限制了其应用

Method: 在双时间尺度actor-critic框架下，利用Hessian向量积(HVP)计算二阶近似，其中批评者以更快的时间尺度演化，在Actor更新期间可被视为准平稳的

Result: 提出了一个针对折扣奖励设置的计算高效且稳定的二阶Actor-Critic方法

Conclusion: 通过双时间尺度框架，行动值函数可被视为准平稳的，从而实现稳定且计算高效的二阶优化更新

Abstract: We address the discounted reward setting in reinforcement learning (RL). To mitigate the value approximation challenges in policy gradient methods, actor-critic approaches have been developed and are known to converge to stationary points under suitable assumptions. However, these methods rely on first-order updates. In contrast, second-order optimization provides principled curvature-aware updates that are proven to accelerate convergence, but its application in RL is limited by the computational complexity of Hessian estimation. In this work, we analyze second-order approximations for the actor update that leverage the full curvature information of the objective as much as possible. A stable approximation requires treating the action-value function as locally constant with respect to policy parameters, which does not generally hold in policy gradient methods. We show that this approximation becomes well-justified under a two-timescale actor-critic framework, where the critic evolves on a faster timescale and can be treated as quasi-stationary during actor updates. Building on this insight, we formulate a second-order actor-critic method for the discounted reward setting that leverages Hessian-vector product (HVP) computations, resulting in a computationally efficient and stable second-order update.

</details>


### [194] [DeepTokenEEG Enhancing Mild Cognitive Impairment and Alzheimers Classification via Tokenized EEG Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15009)
*Thinh Nguyen-Quang,Minh Long Ngo,Ngoc-Son Nguyen,Nguyen Thanh Vinh,Huy-Dung Han,Bui Thanh Tung,Nguyen Quang Linh,Khuong Vo,Manoj Vishwanath,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种名为DeepTokenEEG的轻量级深度学习模型，用于通过EEG信号诊断阿尔茨海默病。该模型仅需0.29百万参数，通过空间和时间分词器在时频域提取AD相关生物标志物。在274名受试者（180例AD患者，94名健康对照）的数据集上训练后，在特定频段达到100%准确率，较现有最优方法提升1.41-15.35%。该模型因其紧凑的参数量和高效性，具备良好的临床应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于EEG的AD检测面临数据稀缺、深度学习准确性不足、以及专家解读耗时等挑战，亟需轻量化高性能的诊断模型

Method: 采用空间和时间分词器（tokenizer）捕捉EEG信号中与AD相关的生物标志物，同时在时域和频域进行特征提取

Result: 在特定频段上达到100%准确率，较同类最先进方法提升1.41-15.35%

Conclusion: DeepTokenEEG模型仅有0.29M参数，易于部署，具有早期筛查和临床应用的潜力

Abstract: The detection of Alzheimers disease (AD) is considered crucial, as timely intervention can improve patient outcomes. Electroencephalogram (EEG)-based diagnosis has been recognized as a non-invasive, accessible, and cost-effective approach for AD detection; however, it faces challenges related to data availability, accuracy of modern deep learning methods, and the time-consuming nature of expert-based interpretation. In this study, a novel lightweight and high-performance model, DeepTokenEEG, was designed for the diagnosis of AD and the classification of EEG signals from AD patients, individuals with other neurological conditions, and healthy subjects. Unlike traditional heavy-weight models, DeepTokenEEG ultilizes spatial and temporal tokenizer that effectively captures AD-related biomarkers in both temporal and frequency domain with only 0.29 million paramaters. Trained in a combined dataset of 274 subjects, including 180 AD cases, and 94 healthy controls, the proposed method achieves a maximum recorded accuracy of 100% on specific frequency bands, representing an improvement of 1.41-15.35% over state-of-the-art methods on the same dataset. These results indicate the potential of DeepTokenEEG for early detection and screening of AD, with promising applicability for deployment due to its compact size.

</details>


### [195] [Generalized Priority-Aware Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15018)
*Kiljae Lee,Ziqi Liu,Weijing Tang,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper generalizes priority-aware Shapley value to handle arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs with cyclic structures, providing axiomatic foundations, computational methods, and diagnostic tools. Applied to LLM ensemble valuation on the cyclic Chatbot Arena preference graph, the work demonstrates that priority-aware valuation involves meaningful trade-offs between different priority configurations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing priority-aware Shapley value methods restrict pairwise priorities to be binary and acyclic, which is violated in real-data scenarios such as aggregated human preferences and multi-criterion comparisons, requiring a more flexible generalization.

Method: Extended random order value framework defined on arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs; axiomatic characterization with consistency and symmetry properties; computational methods for practical implementation; priority sweeping diagnostic tool.

Result: GPASV can handle cyclic priority structures; different parameterizations yield substantively different valuations of the same data, demonstrating that priority-aware valuation requires careful calibration rather than being a one-button operation.

Conclusion: GPASV provides a unified framework for priority-aware valuation that handles cyclic structures in real-world data, with practical applications in LLM ensemble evaluation. The key insight is that different balances of pairwise priority versus individual soft priority produce substantially different valuations.

Abstract: Shapley value and its priority-aware extensions are widely used for valuation in machine learning, but existing methods require pairwise priority to be binary and acyclic, a restriction spectacularly violated in real-data examples such as aggregated human preferences and multi-criterion comparisons. We introduce the generalized priority-aware Shapley value (GPASV), a random order value defined on arbitrary directed weighted priority graphs, in which pairwise edges penalize rather than forbid order violations. GPASV covers a range of classical models as boundary cases. We establish GPASV through an axiomatic characterization, develop the associated computational methods, and introduce a priority sweeping diagnostic extending PASV's. We apply GPASV to LLM ensemble valuation on the cyclic Chatbot Arena preference graph, illustrating that priority-aware valuation is not a one-button operation: different balances of pairwise graph priority versus individual soft priority produce substantively different valuations of the same data.

</details>


### [196] [TopoPrimer: The Missing Topological Context in Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15035)
*Zara Zetlin,Kayhan Moharreri,Maria Safi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TopoPrimer通过持久同调和谱层坐标将全局拓扑结构显式引入预测模型，在Chronos和TimesFM上最高提升7.3%精度，尤其在季节性峰值和冷启动等困难场景下效果显著，拓扑特征与per-series训练形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测模型未将序列群体的全局拓扑结构作为显式输入，导致冷启动和季节性需求高峰等困难场景下性能受限

Method: 使用持久同调(persistent homology)和谱层坐标(spectral sheaf coordinates)预计算拓扑特征，部署为per-token组件或轻量适配器

Result: 四个公开基准上MSE最高提升7.3%；季节峰值时性能下降控制在10%内；冷启动MAE降低27%；零样本和微调骨干上优势幅度一致

Conclusion: TopoPrimer通过引入拓扑特征与per-series训练形成互补信号，在困难场景下优势显著。框架设计轻量灵活，既可作为完全训练模型的即插即用组件，也可作为预训练骨干的轻量适配器。

Abstract: We introduce TopoPrimer, a framework that makes the global topological structure of the series population an explicit input
  to any forecasting model. TopoPrimer improves accuracy across diverse domains, stabilizes forecasts under seasonal demand
  spikes, and closes the cold-start gap. Precomputed once per domain via persistent homology and spectral sheaf coordinates,
  TopoPrimer deploys per token for fully-trained models and as a lightweight adapter for pre-trained backbones. Of these two
  components, sheaf coordinates are the primary accuracy driver. Across four public benchmarks on Chronos and TimesFM,
  TopoPrimer consistently improves forecasting accuracy, with gains of up to 7.3% MSE on ECL. The topology advantage persists
  with near-identical magnitude across zero-shot and fine-tuned backbones, suggesting topology and per-series training
  capture complementary signals. The gains are most pronounced in difficult regimes. Under peak seasonal demand, classical
  and zero-shot models degrade by up to 50%, while TopoPrimer stays within 10%. At cold start with no item history,
  TopoPrimer reduces MAE by 27% over a topology-free baseline.

</details>


### [197] [Separating Intrinsic Ambiguity from Estimation Uncertainty in Deep Generative Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15050)
*Yuxin Guo,Dongrui Deng,Pulkit Grover*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper proposes a structural decomposition method for separating intrinsic ambiguity from inference uncertainty in posterior estimation using deep generative models, enabling better calibration and diagnostics for high-stakes inverse problems in medical imaging and neuroscience.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In high-stakes applications (medical imaging, scientific discovery), posterior uncertainty is critical, but interpreting it is challenging because it mixes ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with inference-propagated uncertainty.

Method: The authors propose a structural decomposition that isolates intrinsic ambiguity from uncertainty propagated through inference, implemented via a cascade formulation that makes ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis.

Result: The approach is validated on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then demonstrated on accelerated MRI, and applied to EEG source imaging with calibration diagnostics.

Conclusion: The cascade formulation enables both qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that can reveal failure modes not detectable through reconstruction quality metrics alone.

Abstract: Recently, deep generative models have been used for posterior inference in inverse problems, including high-stakes applications in medical imaging and scientific discovery, where the uncertainty of a prediction can matter as much as the prediction itself. However, posterior uncertainty is difficult to interpret because it can mix ambiguity inherent to the forward operator with uncertainty propagated through inference. We introduce a structural decomposition of posterior uncertainty that isolates intrinsic ambiguity. A cascade formulation makes this ambiguity accessible for calibration analysis, enabling qualitative diagnostics and simulation-based calibration tests that reveal failure modes that remain hidden when models are selected by reconstruction quality alone. We first validate the approach on a Gaussian example with analytical posterior structure, then illustrate the decomposition on accelerated magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), and finally apply the calibration diagnostics to electroencephalography (EEG) source imaging.

</details>


### [198] [An Interpretable Latency Model for Speculative Decoding in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15051)
*Linghao Kong,Megan Flynn,Michael Peng,Nir Shavit,Mark Kurtz,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对推测解码在LLM生产服务系统中的性能建模问题，提出了一种基于Little定律的延迟模型，将每请求需求分解为预填充、起草和验证阶段的负载相关与负载无关分量。该模型通过vLLM大规模测量验证，可解释加速效果随负载增加而衰减的现象，并可扩展至混合专家模型场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究在隔离或固定批量设置中展示了显著的加速效果，但生产服务系统中的推测解码行为仍缺乏理解：请求负载随时间变化，有效批量大小由服务系统涌现而非直接控制或观测。

Method: 通过Little定律从请求率推断有效批量大小，并将每请求需求分解为预填充、起草和验证的负载无关分量和负载相关分量。研究者使用vLLM进行了广泛测量，涵盖验证器和起草者模型大小、预填充和解码长度、请求率、起草长度和接受概率等多维度参数。

Result: 模型准确描述了观测到的延迟，解释了为何加速效果随服务器负载增加而减弱，揭示了起草长度、接受率和验证器-起草者规模如何在不同服务条件下影响延迟。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展至混合专家模型，并提供了理解真实LLM服务系统中推测解码的结构化方法，对部署系统中SD的配置具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a smaller draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a larger target model in parallel. While prior work demonstrates substantial speedups in isolated or fixed-batch settings, the behavior of SD in production serving systems remains poorly understood: request load varies over time, and effective batch size emerges from the serving system rather than being directly controlled or observed. In this work, we develop a simple and interpretable latency model for SD in LLM serving. We infer effective batch size from request rate using Little's Law and decompose per-request demand into load-independent and load-dependent components for prefill, drafting, and verification. We validate our model using extensive measurements from vLLM across verifier and drafter model sizes, prefill and decode lengths, request rates, draft lengths, and acceptance probabilities. The model accurately describes observed latency, explains why speedups often diminish as server load increases, and characterizes how draft length, acceptance rate, and verifier-drafter size shape latency across serving conditions, with implications for configuring SD in deployed systems. We further show how the framework extends to mixture of experts models, where sparse expert activation changes the effective service costs across load regimes. Together, our results provide a structured framework for understanding SD in real LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [199] [TFGN: Task-Free, Replay-Free Continual Pre-Training Without Catastrophic Forgetting at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15053)
*Anurup Ganguli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TFGN提出一种创新架构覆盖层，通过Read/Write分解机制解决LLM在异构域持续预训练中的灾难性遗忘问题，无需回放、任务ID或Fisher惩罚。在1B tokens/phase规模下于6个异构域验证，实现了近乎零的反向转移和正向迁移，并首次在LLM规模实现闭环元控制器和算子级潜在规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在异构文本域上进行持续预训练时，在无回放或任务标签的条件下避免灾难性遗忘仍是未解决的架构问题。现有方法依赖回放缓冲、任务标识符、正则化惩罚（扩展性差）或仅在句子分类规模评估，无法满足LLM规模的需求。

Method: TFGN是一种用于Transformer语言模型的架构覆盖层，核心创新为Read/Write分解：前向传播保持全密集，通过L2正交梯度分离实现跨域参数更新结构化，确保不向先验域子空间写入。提供两种扩展：（A）闭环元控制层映射到System A/M角色；（B）算子级计划向量。实验覆盖6个异构文本域、3种模型规模（~398M、~739M、~9B参数）和两种训练模式（From-Scratch和Retrofit）。

Result: 在6个异构文本域（Prose、Python、Math、Biomedical、Chinese、JavaScript），每阶段10亿token的设置下：LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit反向转移达-0.007；HellaSwag保留率0.506/0.504/0.510；域对间梯度L2正交分离度≥99.59%；跨域正向转移显著（Python训练使JavaScript PPL在LLaMA-8B上降低26.8%，GPT-2 Medium上降低62.0%）。Extension A在~398M规模上额外减少81%遗忘；Extension B在30对源-目标算子上实现99.96%余弦保真度。

Conclusion: TFGN是首个同时解决LLM规模灾难性遗忘、实现闭环自主学习元控制器、并携带算子级潜在规划器的架构。其核心突破在于Read/Write分解机制，使前向传播完全密集化，同时通过结构化更新防止对先验域子空间的覆盖。两个扩展分别针对遗忘问题（Extension A减少81%遗忘）和前向行为重塑（Extension B达到99.96%余弦保真度）。

Abstract: Continually pre-training a large language model on heterogeneous text domains, without replay or task labels, has remained an unsolved architectural problem at LLM scale. Existing methods rely on replay buffers, task identifiers, regularization penalties that scale poorly, or sentence-classification-scale evaluation.
  We introduce TFGN, an architectural overlay for transformer language models that produces input-conditioned, parameter-efficient updates while leaving the rest of the transformer unchanged. On six heterogeneous text domains (Prose, Python, Math, Biomedical, Chinese, JavaScript) at 1B tokens per phase across three model scales (~398M, ~739M, ~9B) and two regimes (From-Scratch and Retrofit), TFGN achieves backward transfer of -0.007 at LLaMA 3.1 8B Retrofit, HellaSwag retention 0.506/0.504/0.510, and >=99.59% L2-orthogonal gradient separation between domain pairs - with no replay, no task IDs, no Fisher penalty.
  The same matrices show positive cross-domain forward transfer: held-out JavaScript PPL drops 26.8% at LLaMA-8B Retrofit and 62.0% at GPT-2 Medium From-Scratch purely from Python training. Two extensions on the same substrate close further open problems. A closed-loop meta-control layer (Extension A) reduces forgetting by an additional 81% at ~398M, mapping onto the System A and System M roles of Dupoux et al. (arXiv:2603.15381). An operator-level plan vector (Extension B) reshapes forward-pass behavior at 99.96% cosine fidelity over 30 source->target pairs.
  The architectural insight is a Read/Write decomposition: the forward pass is fully dense, while cross-domain parameter updates are structured so prior-domain subspaces are not written to. To our knowledge, TFGN is the first architecture that simultaneously closes catastrophic forgetting at LLM scale, realizes a closed-loop autonomous-learning meta-controller, and carries an operator-level latent planner.

</details>


### [200] [DiffusionOPD: A Unified Perspective of On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15055)
*Quanhao Li,Junqiu Yu,Kaixun Jiang,Yujie Wei,Zhen Xing,Pandeng Li,Ruihang Chu,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffusionOPD提出了一种基于在线策略蒸馏的多任务训练新范式，用于扩散式文本到图像模型，通过先独立训练任务特定教师再蒸馏到统一学生的方式解决了多任务强化学习的挑战，并在效率和性能上超越了现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing reinforcement learning methods for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models are limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging due to cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL approaches are cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting.

Method: The method uses Online Policy Distillation (OPD) where task-specific teachers are first trained independently, then their capabilities are distilled into a unified student along the student's own rollout trajectories. A closed-form per-step KL objective is derived via mean-matching for both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement, which provides lower variance gradients compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients.

Result: DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks. The analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients.

Conclusion: DiffusionOPD provides a more efficient multi-task training paradigm for diffusion-based text-to-image models, achieving state-of-the-art results across all evaluated benchmarks with better training efficiency than existing methods.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as a powerful tool for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models, but existing methods are largely limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging: joint optimization suffers from cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL is cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting. We propose DiffusionOPD, a new multi-task training paradigm for diffusion models based on Online Policy Distillation (OPD). DiffusionOPD first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their capabilities into a unified student along the student own rollout trajectories. This decouples single-task exploration from multi-task integration and avoids the optimization burden of solving all tasks jointly from scratch. Theoretically, we lift the OPD framework from discrete tokens to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form per-step KL objective that unifies both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching. We formally and empirically demonstrate that this analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients. Extensive experiments show that DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, while achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [201] [Learning from Language Feedback via Variational Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15113)
*Yang Li,Erik Nijkamp,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Rayhan Joty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPD通过变分EM框架协同进化师生策略，E步由自适应信任域更新主动优化教师策略从语言反馈中提取可操作信号，M步由学生内化密集分布指导，从而克服了被动蒸馏的局限性，在科学推理和代码生成任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在复杂推理任务中面临稀疏结果信号的严重探索瓶颈。现有on-policy自蒸馏方法依赖固定的被动教师解释反馈，随着学生策略改进，教师零样本评估能力停滞，最终阻碍学习。

Method: 提出VPD框架，将语言反馈学习形式化为变分EM问题。E步：教师通过自适应信任域更新在轨迹结果上主动优化，将文本反馈转化为动态改进的目标token分布；M步：学生在自己的on-policy rollout上内化这种密集的分布指导。通过持续改进教师从文本批评中提取可操作信号的能力，VPD克服了被动蒸馏的局限性。

Result: VPD在科学推理和代码生成任务上的一致评估表明，该方法始终优于标准RLVR和现有自蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: VPD通过持续改进教师策略从文本批评中提取可操作信号的能力，克服了被动蒸馏的局限性，并在科学推理和代码生成任务上始终优于标准RLVR和现有自蒸馏基线。压力测试揭示了反馈驱动的自蒸馏与纯环境驱动的RL之间的基本界限。
